koṭiyāḷarkaḷ
  
1.	māttamiḻ marapu  pā  paṭṭaṟi - kuvittu
2.	ve. campatkumār (kuruvareṭṭiyūr, īrōṭu -mā) kuvittu
3.	mū. catyamūrtti (pāṇṭiccēri) kuvittu
4.	acōkkumār. a (ilālkuṭi, tirucci mā) - kuvittu
5.	iramēcukumār. kē.pi. (nākarkōvil) -kuvittu
6.	kiruṭṭiṇa cekaṉ. po. (nākarkōvil) - kuvittu
7.	āṟumukam. a (pāṇṭiccēri) - kuvittu
8.	cētumātavaṉ. pa (kaṭalūr) – kuvittu
9.	tamiḻnāṭaṉ (kuṇavācal, kaṭalūr mā) - kuvittu

tirikaṭal ōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭacceṉṟa maṇṇil iruntu
koṇṭu tāy moḻiyām tamiḻiṉ mēl vaṟṟāta paṟṟukkoṇṭu vāḻum tamiḻ uḷḷaṅkaḷi mutalil vaṇaṅkukiṟēṉ. ivvaruntamiḻk kaḷañciyam nāṉkām tokuti veḷivaruvataṟku utaviya kuvittu koṭiyāḷarkaḷukku  neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi.



kaṭappāṭṭu uri
tamiḻ iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻippaṭṭa oli, vari, vaṭivukaḷiyum iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal, meyyiyal, vāḻviyal, paṭippiyal eṉpavaṟṟi aṭimaṉiyākavum koṇṭa moḻi.
ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē eṉṉum mūla muḻakkatti muḻuvatākak koṇṭatu nammoḻi eṉpati ōrāṟṟāṉ viḷakkuvatāka amintatu ik kaḷañciyam.
āṇivēr, pakkavēr, callivēr eṉpavi illāmal, veḷippaṭa evarum aṟintu koṇṭuḷḷa vaḻakku, ceyyuḷ eṉpavi koṇṭē viḷakkuvatu ikkaḷañciyam.
itaṉi, ūṉṟiyum virumpiyum kaṟpār tāmum ivvāyvu ceyya viyalum eṉpatit tūṇṭa valla āyvum itu! ātalāl, paṭippāḷiyip paṭippāḷi ākkavallatu eṉpati eṉ paṭṭaṟivut teḷivākak kaṇṭu varukiṟēṉ! eḷimiyāy - āḻnta nuṇukkaṅkaḷi - ōraḷavu kaṟṟārum, koḷḷa vaḻikāṭṭum `kiviḷakku aṉṉatu itu.
itaṉi mutal - iṭi - niṟivu eṉṉum mūṉṟu nili kaḷilum uṟṟa peṟalarum utavunarāka iruntu muṟṟuvittatuṭaṉ, tamiḻ ulakap payaṉpāṭṭukkum vaḻiyamittut tanta `kalaṅkari viḷakkam cemmoḻic cemmal muṉivar tiruttaku ka. irāmacāmi avarkaḷē āvar. ātalāl, avarkaḷi neñcārap pōṟṟuvatu eṉ talikkaṭaṉām!
tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli niṟuvaṉa vaḻiyē ippaṇi ceyya vāytta aḷavil coṟṟiraṭṭiyum meyppup pārttuma utaviya peruntaku kēṇmiyum uḻuvalaṉpum uṭiya muṉivar peruntaki ku. tirumāṟaṉār avarkaḷukkum em eḻuttuppaṇiyit tam paṇiyāk koṇṭu toṭarntu ceytuvarum toyvilāt toṇṭar meyppup pārttalil vallār muṉivar pi. tamiḻakaṉār avarkaḷukkum neñcārnta naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
eṉ eḻuttip paṭiyeṭuttum, koṅku vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷit tokuttum utaviya pulavar kalimaṇiyār avarkaḷukkum, ippaṇiyil illi eṉiṉum uḻuva laṉpālum urimip pēṟṟālum eṉ eḻuttukaḷip paṭiyeṭuttu utaviya tirukkuṟaḷ cemmal perumpulavar mu. paṭikkarāmu avarkaḷukkum perunaṉṟiyum vāḻttum uṭiyēṉ.
ittoki aṉittiyum kaṇiṉippaṭuttiyum, meyppup pārttum, varavu celavu kaṇakkit takka vakit taṇikkikku uriyaviyākac ceytum, paṇiniṟivi oppaṭittum perunaṉṟāṟṟiya kavaṉakac cemmal muṉivar kali. ceḻiyaṉār aruntoṇṭip pārāṭṭi naṉṟi perituṭiyēṉ.
paṇi niṟivuppayaṉ, tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakap payaṉpāṭu ātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉē! illākkāl ceyta paṇiyāl āvateṉṉa? ceyyāmi oppatu tāṉē! avvāṟu ākāmal tāyttamiḻaka ārvalarkaḷuṭaṉ, ayalakat tamiḻp perumakkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉum tāyttamiḻaka ārvalarkaḷilum mēmpaṭṭa ārvalarkaḷākak kuviyattu, amerikkā, kaṉaṭā, maliyakam, ciṅkapuri, īḻam mutalāka vāḻvār ārvat taḷirppum tūṇṭalum muṉṉiṟkum pēṟu viyappukkum perunaṉṟikkum uriyatām;
ipperumakkaḷ aṉivar uḷḷamum ōruruvāka vāyttu, karuvi nūṟpaṇiyit toki tokiyāka vakippaṭutti vāṉ peruntoṇṭākac ceytalē em piṟavik kaṭaṉ eṉak koṇṭa tamiḻp pōrāḷi tirumiku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koṇṭu ceyaṟkariya ceyvār periyar eṉpati nilināṭṭiyatip pōṟṟuvataṉṟi eṉ colvēṉ?
kaḷañciyam kaṇiṉippaṭuttiya aḷavil niṟivuṟumā? pattut tokutikaḷiyum orumuṟikku mummuṟiyāy muḻutuṟa meyppup pārttal, eḷimip pāṭā? kaṇpuri vaḷarntālum iṭittaṭi nērntālum ittoṇṭu tamiḻaṉṉi taḷirkkum toṇṭu eṉṟu imip poḻutum cōrātu kaṭaṉāṟṟiya kaṭappāṭṭāḷar meyppuccemmal muṉivar 
pi. tamiḻakaṉārkkup peruṅkaṭappāṭu uṭiyēṉ; patippuc cumiyi ēṟṟami eṉ cumi ēṟṟamiyām!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nalaṉē! 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ

kaḷañciyam
vaḷamāṉa vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻum nilakkiḻār perunilakkiḻār ākiya uḻavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṉ uṭpakutiyil kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ amittiruppar. kaḷañciyam irukkum vīṭu eṉṟāl vaḷamikka uḻavarvīṭu eṉpatu poruḷ.
kaḷattil varum tavacaṅkaḷ payaṟu vakikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi aṅkē pūcciyarippu, maṭkutal ēṟpaṭāvakiyil taḷattiṉmēl uyarttiyum puṉku vēmpu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ taḻikaḷip parappiyum, pātukāppar. avvāṇṭu muḻumikkum payaṉpaṭum. aṭutta āṇṭu viḷivu kuṟintālum kaḷañciyap poruḷ kavaliyiṉṟi vāḻa utavum.
ēruḻavar pōlac collēr uḻavarām cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ, akkālattārkkē aṉṟi ekkālattārkkum payaṉpaṭum vakiyil pātukāttup payaṉ koḷḷac ceyvatu nūl kaḷañciyamākum. 
intiyap parappil tamiḻ moḻiyil tāṉ mutaṉmutal `kalik kaḷañciyam uruvāṉatu eṉpatu perumitappaṭattakkatām. ippoḻutu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattāl veḷiyiṭappaṭum centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam cevviyal moḻik koṭiyāka vantu, tamiḻmaṇ patippaka veḷiyīṭṭāl, tamiḻkūṟu nallulakap poruḷāvatu, cemmoḻic cemmal muṉivar tirumiku ka. irāmacāmi avarkaḷ tūṇṭal vaḻiyākat tulaṅkiya tulakkamākum. ātalāl paṭippāḷaṉ eṉṟa niliyil neñcārnta naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.



tamiḻk kaḷañciyam
moḻiyiṉ vaḷam, ammoḻiyip pēcuvōr payaṉpaṭuttum coṟkaḷālum, am moḻiyil uḷḷa ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya vakikaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻiṉ vaḷam ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷilum niṟintuḷḷatu. coṟkaḷiṉ vaḷamum, perukkamum moḻiyiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭum. coṟkaḷiṉ perukkik kāṭṭa ettaṉiyō nikaṇṭukaḷum, akarātikaḷum, kaḷañciyaṅkaḷum tamiḻil vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ pala piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum tamiḻc coṟkaḷāyk karutip poruḷ tantuḷḷaṉa. piṟa moḻikaḷil ēṟiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷum vēṟṟāṭi uṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi aṟintu tamiḻeṉak kāṭṭa, moḻi uṇarvum, ilakkiya ilakkaṇap pulamiyum, tamiḻ muḻutaṟi takutiyum, makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷi vaḻaṅkum cūḻalil kēṭṭaṟitalum, coṟ poruḷāyvum, colliyal neṟimuṟikaḷum, vērc collāyvum, kūr matiyum niṟintirukka vēṇṭum.
ittamiḻc coṟ kaḷañciyam coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi maṭṭumā tarukiṉṟaṉa? coṟkaḷiṉ vērum, ataṉ virivum, avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkamum, cāṉṟukaḷum, makkaḷiṉ potu vaḻakkum, vaṭṭāra vaḻakkum, yāḻppāṇat tamiḻ vaḻakkum ceṟintuḷḷa tamiḻk kaḷañciyamām.
oru viṉic coṟkaḷ toki tokiyākiya toki ikkaḷañciyam. `aṭittal collaṭiyāka 145 coṟkaḷ uṇṇal vakikaḷ, ūrp peyar īṟukaḷ (504) nōy vakikaḷ (229), nōy viṉikaḷ (216), matil poṟi vakikaḷ (28) mali vakikaḷ (25) mutaliya ettaṉiyō vakikaḷum tokikaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ tokuppu kaṟpāri viyakka vikkum!
ik kaḷañciyat tokutikaḷuḷ nūṟṟukkaṇakkil maraput toṭarkaḷ, iṇi moḻikaḷ, olikkuṟippukaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.
coṟkaḷ viḷakkattiṟku aṉṟāṭac ceytikaḷum (ceytittāḷkaḷ) cāṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟṟilakkiya vakikaḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟu eṉpar. āṉāl, paṟpala nūṟuvakikaḷ uḷḷamiyi ikkaḷañciyam kāṭṭum.
mēṉāṭṭu uṭikaḷi māṭṭiyatāl tamiḻar mēṉāṭṭār ākāraṉṟō? pajji ētō piṟamoḻi eṉṟē mayaṅki niṟpar. atu paccik kāykaṟikaḷāl ceyyappaṭuvatu.
tēvanēyap pāvāṇar uṭaṉ paḻakiyamiyālum, avar nūlkaḷit tōyntu tōyntu kaṟṟamiyālum, avarē pārāṭṭiyamiyālum aiyā, pala coṟkaḷukku vērccol vaḻi mūlamum kiḷiyum kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. puḻuti, pūḻti, pūti.
pala coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷum kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: virippu- virippu; viṟippu - toyviṉmi;
ikkaḷañciyam tavacaṅkaḷām coṟkaḷ koṭṭappaṭṭu niṟintuḷḷatu. paṭippār tattamakkut tēviyāṉa tavacaṅkaḷi aḷḷi aḷḷip payaṉ koḷḷalām. evvaḷavu aḷḷiṉum eṉṟum kuṟiyātu ikkaḷañciyam.
tamiḻariṉ, moḻi, iṉa, ilakkiya, ilakkaṇam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu, paṇpāṭu, nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ aṭaṅkalkaḷip paṭi paṭiyākat tiraṭṭit tokuttu veḷiyiṭum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil mutaliṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
aiyā mutumuṉivar, iḷami mutal tokutta coṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷic cērttuc cērmāṉamākkit tiraṭṭit tantuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷē! iṅkē vammiṉ! kaḷañciyam koṇmiṉ!! tamiḻi vaḷarmiṉ!!!
pi. tamiḻakaṉ.

aṇinturi
centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉṉum innūl iṉṟu nammiṭiyē vāḻum tolkāppiyarāka viḷaṅkum mūtta tamiḻaṟiñar centamiḻ antaṇar mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa oṉṟākum. ēṟattāḻa 8000 tamiḻc coṟkaḷukkup poruḷ viḷakkattiṉi mēṟkōḷ cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkum innūl tōrāyamāka 3254 pakkaṅkaḷil pattut tokutikaḷāka veḷivantu taṉṉērilāta tamiḻ moḻikkut taṉipperumi cērkkum muṉṉōṭip paṇiyāka amintuḷḷatu. akaramutalikaḷil iṭampeṟāta coṟkaḷ, marapuccoṟkaḷ, vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ pōṉṟavi ikkaḷañciyattil iṭampeṟuvatu kuṟippiṭattakka tākum. ici, maruttuvam, kalveṭṭu, aṟiviyal ākiya tuṟikaḷ cārnta iṉṟiyamiyāta  coṟkaḷukkum poruḷ viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. akarattil toṭaṅkum coṟkaḷuk kāṉa poruḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē 276 pakkaṅkaḷil mutal tokutiyākavum taṉit tokutiyākavum amintiruppatu nūliṉ ceṟiviyum viriviyum paṟi cāṟṟuvatāka amintuḷḷatu. ayaṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ aṟavē tavirkkap paṭṭiruppatu nūliṉ taṉicciṟappākum.
coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyap paṇiyi niṟivēṟṟum vakiyil nūlāciriyarukkut tūṇṭutalāka amintatu olkāp pukaḻ tolkāppiyamē. kuṟippāka, ellāc collum poruḷkuṟittaṉavē (tol. 640) eṉṉum nūṟpā. in nūṟpāviṉi mūla muḻakkamākak koṇṭatu tamiḻmoḻi eṉpati viḷakkum poruṭṭē ikkaḷañciyam uruvākkap paṭṭateṉa nūlāciyir kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar kāṭṭiyavāṟu āṇivēr, pakkavēr, callivēr eṉak kāṇāmal veḷippaṭiyāka aṟiyakkūṭiya coṟkaḷukku maṭṭumē amitiyum tiripum poruḷ viḷakkamum kāṭṭuvatu ikkaḷañciyam eṉpatum nūlāciriyar kūṟṟākum. poruḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉūṭē kīḻkkāṇum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭṭiṉiyum uṇaralām.
poruṇmi teritalum coṉmi teritalum
colliṉ ākum eṉmaṉār pulavar.	(tol. 641)
teripuvēṟu niliyalum kuṟippiṉ tōṉṟalum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa poruṇmi niliyē. 	(tol. 642)
poruṭkup poruḷteriyiṉ atuvaram piṉṟē.	(tol. 874)
poruṭkut tiripilli uṇartta valliṉ	(tol. 875)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.	(tol. 877)
oru poruḷ pala coṟkaḷ mikavum nuṭpamāy ārāyap paṭṭut tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. `aḻaku poruṇmiyik kuṟikkum 67 taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷi aṭiyāḷaṅkaṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku nuṭpamāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷit tantiruppatu āciriyariṉ nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiṟkum vaḷamāṉa paṭṭaṟiviṟkum cāṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
perumatippiṟkuriya aiyā iḷaṅkumarāṉāriṉ neṭuṅkāla avā innūl veḷiyīṭṭiṉ vaḻi niṟivēṟiyuḷḷami makiḻvaḷikkiṟatu. itu kālatti veṉṟuniṟkum nilitta paṇi. tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa taliciṟanta nōkkīṭṭu nūl. ulakeṅkum vāḻum tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷ innūliṉi varavēṟṟup pōṟṟuvārkaḷ eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli. kaḷañciyap paṇi oru toṭar paṇi. ippattut tokutikaḷi aṭittaḷamākak koṇṭu toṭarntu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tokutikaḷ veḷivantu tamiḻiṟku vaḷamcērkka vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ avā.
taṉittamiḻp paṟṟāḷarākavum ariya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ patippuc cemmalākavum viḷaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar tiru. kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār ikkaḷañciyatti arumuyaṟciyuṭaṉ ciṟappuṟa veḷikkoṇarntiruppatu pārāṭṭiṟkuriyatākum.
muṉivar ka. irāmacāmi
muṉṉāḷ pērāciriyar - tuṇi iyakkunar
intiya moḻikaḷiṉ naṭuvaṇ niṟuvaṉam, micūr
muṉṉāḷ poṟuppu aluvalar
cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉam, ceṉṉi

nuḻivuri
tamiḻ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil putintu kiṭakkum aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit taṉakkē uriya naṭiyil iruntu  tamiḻiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷi akaḻntu kāṭṭum ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ niṉivāṟṟali niṉintu niṉintu  makiḻpavaṉ.
avar arukil irukkavum, avaruṭaṉ uriyāṭavum, vāḻnāḷil eṉakkuk kiṭitta perum pēṟākavum karutukiṟēṉ. moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, centamiḻ aṟiñar ilakkuvaṉār, mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār mutalāṉa perumakkaḷi eṭṭi iruntu pārttavaṉ. avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu nalaṉkaḷik paṭittuc cuvittavaṉ. ip perumakkaḷiṉ motta uruvamāka ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉārip pārttu neñcam nekiḻpavaṉ.
ayyā avarkaḷiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷik kaṭanta kālattil pēraḷavil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu avariṉ uḻippi tamiḻ kūṟum ulakukku aḷittatil niṟivu aṭikiṟēṉ.
iṉṟu uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻum centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam eṉum ivvaruntamiḻt tokuppi uruvākkuvataṟku evvaḷavu kālam uḻittiruppār eṉpati niṉittup pārkkiṟēṉ. viyantu pōkiṟēṉ. iccentamiḻt tokuppu tamiḻ akarāti varalāṟṟil tamiḻukkup putu varavākum. ituvari yārum ceyya muṉvarāta perumpaṇi yākum. ayyāviṉ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇap perumparappit tāṅkivarum ippoṟ kuviyali ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuvatil perumakiḻcci aṭikiṟēṉ.
 pāvāṇar pōl vērkāṭṭi, vaḷam koṭukkum coṟkaṭalāy vāḻum ayyāviṉ pēruḻippāl viḻinta ilakkiya ilakkaṇak kaḷañciyatti veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam eṉṉiyē nāṉ perumip paṭuttik koḷkiṟēṉ. eṉ vāḻkkiyil kiṭitta perum pēṟākak karutukiṟēṉ. 
ayyāviṉ 75ām (30.01.2005) akavi niṟivi tiruccit tirunakaril tamiḻar aṉivarum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ koṇṭāṭiya nikaḻvil pulamikkut talivaṇakkam eṉum niṟivumalariṉi tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu makiḻntatu. anta malaril malēciyap pāvaracu cu.ai.ulakanātaṉ eḻutiya pāṭaliṉi kīḻē cuṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ.
vāyttirukkum akaviyelām
     varalāṟu paṭikkiṉṟār
      viyakamē vantu pōṟṟu
nam muṉ cāṉṟāka vāḻum `aiyā avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa ikkaḷañciyatti tamiḻ ulakukku vaḻaṅkuvatil perumiyum, pūrippum aṭikiṟēṉ.

	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ


āciriyar vivaram
mutumuṉivar  irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār
piṟappu 	: 	30. 1. 1930
peṟṟōr 	: 	irāmu - vāḻavantammi (uḻavarkuṭi)
ūr 	: 	caṅkaraṉkōvil vaṭṭam, vāḻavantāḷpuram
kalvi 	: 	toṭakkak kalvi - uḷḷūril 
naṭunilik kalvi - pāvāṇar payiṉṟa muṟampu paḷḷi
vittuvāṉ - tamiḻt tērvu - taṉit tērvar
āciriyar paṇi : 
toṭakkap paḷḷi - 16ām akaviyil toṭakkam
uyarnilip paḷḷi - tamiḻāciriyappaṇi
āyvuppaṇi : 
pāvāṇaruṭaṉ - centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalit tiṭṭam, maturik kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakattil - kalic col viḷakkam tokuppup paṇi, tamiḻaka vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc col tokuppup paṇi.
tamiḻppaṇi : 
kaṭṭuri, kaviti, kati, nāṭakam, kāppiyam, ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya uri - tamiḻc collāyvu - paḻantamiḻnūl patippukaḷ - taṉimaṉita - ilakkaṇa - iyakka, ici, varalāṟu.  tamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam - nāṭakam.  kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ - āyvup poḻivu - tokuppu nūlkaḷ nāḷoṉṟukku 18 maṇinēra eḻuttup paṇi tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli niṟuvi iyakkutal.  centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam ...
nūlkaḷ : 
civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, īrōṭu kuṟaḷāyam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi 420kkum mēl
veḷināṭṭu payaṇam : 
tamiḻīḻam, ciṅkappūr, malēyā, amerikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil tamiḻ amippukaḷil poḻivukaḷ.
tirumaṇam nikaḻttutal : 
tamiḻakam, ciṅkappūr, malēyāvil 4000kkum mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻneṟit tirumaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻttunar.
iyakkappaṇi : 
tamiḻakat tamiḻāciriyar kaḻakam, ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakam, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakam, tirukkuṟaḷ pēravi, īrōṭu kuṟaḷāyam, tamiḻccāṉṟōr pēravi
virutu : 
ilakkiyac cemmal, centamiḻ antaṇar, mutumuṉivar mutaliyaṉa.

patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ		:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr		:	uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷit 				tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimik kaḻakam eṉṉum amippiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻittavar. iḷam talimuṟikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkiyi neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭa maṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakatti niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyum, putiya paṭippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum, 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumiyākap poruḷ vaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttiri patittu varupavar.
potunili
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, tanti periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā,  moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōri vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkikaḷi niṟivēṟṟa   ayarātu uḻittu varupavar.

poruḷaṭakkam
1.	ka varicic coṟkaḷ	2
2.	kā		201

ka mutal kī vari












ka varicic coṟkaḷ

ka:
ka:1
uyirmey variciyil mutal eḻuttu. uyirkkuṟil kakara oṟṟoṭu ēṟiyatāl uyirmeyk kuṟil (k + a = ka) uyirkkuṟil māttiri aḷavē, uyirmeyk kuṟil aḷavum ātalāl, oru māttiri aḷavē peṟṟatu. kakaram oṟṟukku ari māttiri; uyirkkuṟilukku oru māttiri; ātalāl, oṉṟari māttiri āka vēṇṭāvō? oru māttiri āyatu ēṉ eṉiṉ, orupaṭi nīril arippaṭi uppip pōṭṭāl oṉṟarip paṭiyākāmal oru paṭiyē āṉāl pōlvatu itu eṉpar uriyāciriyar. paḻam paṭiyaḷavu innāḷ liṭṭar eṉappaṭṭatām. oṟṟiṉ mēl uyir ēṟutal,
uṭalmēl uyirvan toṉṟuvatu iyalpē
eṉpatālum (naṉ. 204)
meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu uyirtōṉṟu niliyē	-tol. 18
meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum	-tol. 46
eṉpavaṟṟālum , k + ā = ka āyiṟṟām.
meyyoṭum uyir cērtalāl meyyuyir eṉpataṉṟō muṟimi eṉiṉ, uyirilā uṭal iyaṅkāmiyum, uyiruḷḷa uṭalē iyaṅkutalum iyakkuvatu muṉṉum, iyaṅkuvatu piṉṉumākap peyarīṭu peṟṟatām.
ka eṉṉum meyyiyum piṟameykaḷiyum ikara uyir iṉṟi olikka iyalāmiyum, ik eṉa olikka iyalvatiyum eṇṇit teḷika. ka eṉpatil akara uyir ēṟiyamiyāl ikara uyir vēṇṭiyatilli yāyiṟṟu.
vaṇṭikaḷ - poṟi ūrtikaḷ - ākiyavi muṉṉiyaṅkalum piṉṉiyaṅkalum kāṇkiṟōm allavō! attakiya iyakkam eṉka.
ka:2
tamiḻ eṇkaḷiṉ toṭakkamām eṇ oṉṟu. av voṉṟi, iv oṉṟām uyirmeyyālēyē muṉṉōr eḻutiṉar eṉpatu eṇṇi makiḻat takkatām.
ka:3
ka = kaṭavuḷ.
tamiḻil, ka  kā vit tavira eṉṉa uṇṭu eṉṟōr eḷḷal kaṭanta kālattil oruvarāl eḻuppappaṭṭatu avar  ka, kā eṉṟatu kaṭavuḷ, kātal eṉpavaṟṟiyām. ātalāl ka kaṭavuḷ poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
ka:4
viyaṅkōḷ poruḷil ka varum.
e-ṭu:
vāḻka ! ōṅkuka ! uyarka !
ka:5
ka = āṉmā.
uyirum meyyum kūṭiya eḻuttu. ātalāl āṉmā eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mey= uṭal.
ka:6
kaṇ eṉpataṉ īṟu kuṟital.
e-ṭu:
kakkaṭṭi.
kakkaṭṭi = kaṇpaṭṭiyil uṇṭākum oruvakik kaṭṭi kaṇkaṭṭi eṉpatu kakkaṭṭi (kaṭkaṭṭi > kakkaṭṭi) āyatu. ataṟkuk kāvik kaṭṭiyi nīril naṉittu urāyttu pōṭṭup paḻukka vittu uṭiyac ceyvar. kāvi maṇṇāl āyatāl ataṟku maṇkaṭṭi > makkaṭṭi > makkaṭṭi eṉpār.
kakkaṭṭikku makkaṭṭi maruntu (ma. va.)
x.neh.:
maṇku > maṭku > makku.
ka:7
paḻaṅkāla niṟuttal aḷaviyuḷ oṉṟu. atu kāṟpalam eṉappaṭṭatu.
toṭippuḻuti kaḵcā uṇakkiṉ piṭitteruvum
vēṇṭātu cālap paṭum	-tiruk. 1037
kuṟinta niṟiyik kuṟikka uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkamām āyta eḻuttip payaṉpaṭuttiyatu poruḷ potinta āṭciyām.
kaḵṟu:
karu > kaṟu > kaḵṟu eṉpatu karumip poruḷatu.
  kaḵṟu eṉṉum kallatar attam
eṉpatik kuṟippici eṉpār caṅkara. (naṉ. 101)
kakkam:
kamukkam > kakkam = tōṭpaṭṭiyiṉ uṭpakuti; akkuḷ.
kamukkam = maṟiviṭam, maṟivāṉa ceyti kakkattil maṟittuk koṇṭupōyviṭṭatip pārkkavilli ma.va. kaṭkam eṉpatum itu.
* kaṭkam kāṇka.
kakkal:
uṇṭatik kakkak eṉṉum oliyoṭu veḷit taḷḷal kakkal ākum. kakkuvāṉ eṉpatu irumal nōy. irumalum olikkuṟippē.
kakkal kaḻiccal:
	kakkal  	= 	vānti yeṭuttal.
	kaḻiccal 	= 	vayiṟṟōṭṭam pōtal.
oruvarik, kakkal kaḻiccal orē vēḷiyil iruntu vāṭṭiṉāl ataṉi, vānti pēti eṉpar pēti vayiṟṟōṭṭamām.
kaḻital, mikutal, viṭṭup pōtal, akalutal eṉṉum poruḷuṭiyatu. kaḻivatik kūṭa viṭṭu viṭalām. āṉāl nāḷiyum poḻutiyum vīṇē kaḻippāri, eṉṉa piṟavi eṉṟu ēcāmal tīrātu!  ittakiyarit tāṉō kaḻicaṭi eṉpatu!
kakkal olikkuṟippu; vikkal pōl.
kakkal vikkal:
	kakkal 	= 	vānti yeṭuttal.
	vikkal 	= 	kuṭalukkuc cellāmal tokkik koḷḷutal.
kakkalil oliyuṇṭākum; vikkalil tiṇaṟaluṇṭākum, iṉi vikkal nīrvēṭkiyāl uṇṭāvatāyiṉ ataṟku oliyuṇṭām.
kakkal kakkuvāṉ nōy eṉa oru nōyākak kuḻantikaḷi āṭṭip paṭippatuṇṭu. vikkalum nōyāka mutiyavarkaḷi vāṭṭuvatuṇṭu. muṭiyāta niliyil paṭuttavarik kakkalum vikkalumākak kiṭakkiṟār; eppoḻutō teriyātu eṉak kivirippatuṇṭu.
vikkal olikkuṟippu; vikkuḷ eṉpatum itu.
nāc ceṟṟu vikkuḷmēl vārāmuṉ nalviṉi
mēṟceṉṟu ceyyap paṭum	-tiruk. 335
kaṅkaṇam:
kaṅkaṇam:1
kaṇkāṇam > kaṅkāṇam > kaṅkaṇam.
teyvam - kākka vēṇṭum - kaṇkāṇittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṭṭum mantirippu mañcaḷ kayiṟum, kivaḷiyum kaṅkaṇam eṉappaṭum.
kaṅkaṇam:2
kaṅku + aṇam = kaṅkaṇam = (veppamikutal) mukil kūṭṭam, nīrttuḷi.
veppu mikalāl uṇṭākum mukilum, mukil coriyum maḻittuḷiyum kaṅkaṇam āyiṟṟām ( ve.vi.pē)
kaṅkam:
kaṅku > kaṅkam.
tīkkaṅku, tīkkaṅku niṟattiyuṭiya paruntu.
kaṅkam vantuṟṟa ceyyanam kaḷattu	-kam. uyut. 1233
kaṅkaḷavu:
kaṅku + aḷavu = kaṅkaḷavu. kaṅku = veppam; tīkkaṅku.
erinta kaṭṭiyil tīttuṇṭik kaṅku eṉpatu mukavi, nelli vaḻakku. naṭavu nilattil payir, nila veppattāl vāṭip pōkāmal iruppataṟkāka naṭavu ceytu orunāḷ viṭṭu mūṉṟām nāḷil pāyccappaṭum taṇṇīrik kaṅkaḷavu eṉpatu uḻavar vaḻakku. eṭupput taṇṇīr eṉpatum itu.

kaṅkaṉ:
	kaṅkaṉ 	= 	pakivarkkut tīkkaṅku aṉṉavaṉ; civanta niṟattavaṉ.
naṉṉūl ceyvitta vēntaṉ, kaṅkaṉ.
karuṅkaḻal veṇkuṭik kārnikar vaṇkit
tiruntiya ceṅkōl cīya kaṅkaṉ	-naṉ. ciṟappup.
cēraṉ paṭittalivarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ, kaṅkaṉ.
tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟal kaṅkaṉ	-akam. 44
kaṅku:
kaṅku:1
kaṅku = neruppup paṟṟi erinta viṟakuk kaṭṭiyiṉ tuṇṭu kaṅku. poṟkollar, kollar ākiyōr kaṅkukoṇṭē paṇi ceyvar. karit tuṇṭukaḷil tīppaṟṟa vittu ūti, avarkaḷ ataṉik koṇṭu  tam paṇi ceyvar. muṉṉāḷil kaṅku koṇṭē tīppaṟṟa vittuc camittal vaḻakkam, ciṟṟūrkaḷil iruntatu. ataṟkeṉat tīkkaraṇṭiyum iruntatu itu teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku.
*  kaṅkaḷavu kāṇka.
kaṅku:2
kaṅku = paruntu, tiṉi.
kaṅku eṉṉum tīyiṉ civappu niṟattāl paruntiyum tiṉiyiyum kuṟittatu.
kaṅku:3
kaṅku = ōram.
kaṅku eṉpatu ōr ōram eṉṉum poruḷil makkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. antak kaṅkil pār (ma.va.)
kaṅku:4
kaṅku = elli.
kaṅku eṉpataṟku elli eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭu. kaṅkukari illāta kaṭalē eṉpatu vaḷḷālar āṭci (tiruvaruṭ. 2118).
emmōr ākkak kaṅku uṇṭē	-puṟam. 396
poruḷ: emmaṉōrākiyiravalar avaṉpāṟ peṟṟa celvattukku elli illiyām.  (auvi. cu.tu. uri)
kaṅkukari:
kaṅku = elli muṭivu.
kari = elli muṭivukku aṭiyāḷamām varampu.
kaṅku kari illi, kaṅkukari kāṇāta kaṭal eṉpavi kaṅkukari iṇiyic cuṭṭum. kaṅkum illi; kariyum ilali eṉṟu koḷka.
kaṅkāvātu ōr elliyiṉ kaṭici; kariyāvatu ak kaṭici elliyil vikkappaṭṭa varappu allatu kari.
kaṅkul:
kaṅku > kaṅkul . kaṅku = elli.
pakaliṉ elli iravu; iraviṉ elli viṭiyal; ivaṟṟiṉ iṭippaṭṭatu kaṅkulām.
kaṅkul, elli kāṇpaḷavum	-kam. ayō. 1051
kaṭittōṉṟiya kaṭikkaṅkul	-puṟam. 400
kacakacappu:
viyarvik kacivu vaḻiyāka uṇṭākum viyarvi nāṟṟamum viyarvi uṇarvum kacakacappu eṉappaṭum. itaṉi nacanacappu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivi makkaḷ vaḻakku.
iṉi veḷippaṭap pēcāmal pēcalum palapēr oruṅku pēcalum kacakacappu eṉappaṭalum makkaḷ vaḻakku. teruvē kacakaca veṉṟu irukkiṟatu. eṉpar.
kacakkip piḻital:
	kacakkip piḻital 	= 	kaṭumiyāy vēli vāṅkal.
paḻaṅkaḷik kacakkutalum, kacakkiyatip piḻintu cāṟu eṭuttalum naṭimuṟic ceyti. atu pōlac cilari vāṭṭi vēli vāṅki muṭittuk koḷḷutal celvarkaḷ allatu accelvarkkut tuṇi niṟpār ceyal. icceyali uvamiyāl kuṟippatē kacakkip piḻital eṉpatām. kacakkutal eṉpatu iṭakkaraṭakkākavum varum
paḻaṅkaḷi yaṉṟik karumpi āṭṭic cāṟu koḷvatum, accāṟṟāl kaṭṭiyākkik koḷḷalum, āṭṭip paṭittal eṉappaṭum māvāṭṭal, eṇṇey āṭṭal eṉpaṉa vellām ivvaki cārntaṉa.
kacakkutal:
kiyāl acakkutal kacakkutal ākum.
ki acakku = kacakku, kacakkappaṭṭatu kacaṅkutalām. kīḻkki iṭakkiyāy, ataṉmēl katir, nel, payaṟu, neṟṟu mutaliya vaṟṟi vittu valakkiyi mēṟkiyāyp pakkam paṭaraviṭṭut tēyttal kacakkutalām.
aḻukkut tuṇiyi naṉittu irukiyālum kummit tōyttal - tuvittal - tuṇi kacakkutalām. kantiyāṉalum kacakkik kaṭṭu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
kaṇṇik kacakkutal kavali, varuttak kuṟiyām. oṉṟic coṉṉāl pōtum uṭaṉē kaṇṇik kacakkuvāy eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kacaṭaṉ:
kacaṭu + aṉ = kacaṭaṉ.
uṭalaḻukku kacaṭu; āṉāl uḷa aḻukku ākiya aḻukkāṟu (poṟāmi) avā, vekuḷi, iṉṉāccol ākiya maṉa aḻukkukaḷi yum piṟa kūṭā oḻukkaṅkaḷiyum uṭiyavaṉ kacaṭaṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭuvāṉ.
kacaṭu:
kacaṭu:1
uṭalil uṇṭākum viyarviyik kacakacappu eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka uṇṭākiya col kacaṭu. kacaṭāvatu aḻukku, mācu eṉappaṭuvatu uṭal aḻukku kacaṭu, maṉa aḻukkum, maṉa aḻukku vaḻi ēṟpaṭṭa maṟṟi aḻukkukaḷum mācām.
maṉattukkaṇ mācila ṉātal	-tiruk. 34
kalvik kaḻaku kacaṭaṟa moḻital	-veṟ. vēṟ
kaṟka kacaṭaṟa	-tiruk. 391
ivaṇ aiyam tiripaṟak kaṟṟalē aṉṟi, maṉa aḻukkut tīrat kaṟṟalumām
kacaṭu:2
kacaṭu = vaṭu allatu taḻumpu.
pitta pāmpiṉ tutti yēyppak
kikkaca ṭirunteṉ kaṇṇakaṉ taṭāri	-poru. 69-70
kacaṇṭi:
kacam >fr©o.
muṭi muḻuvatāka illāta vaḻukkit taliyik kacaṇṭi eṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakkākum. kacakacakkum viyarviyaṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil vantirukkalām.
o. neh.:
vaḻu vaḻu eṉṟu iruppatāl tali vaḻukkikkum tēṅkāy vaḻukkikkum peyarāyiṟṟu. vaḻu vaḻuppu vaḻiyākap peṟṟa peyarē vāḻi eṉpatu.
kacappu:
kacappu:1
aṟucuviyuḷ oṉṟu; kippu eṉpatu atu; kacappu āyatu eṭṭikkāy, vēmpu ivaṟṟiṉ kacappi māṟṟa muṭiyumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
kacappu:2
iṉippup pōl virumpappaṭutal illātatāl kacappukku veṟuppu eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itik kēṭṭuk kēṭṭuk kacantu pōṉatu (ma. va.)
kacam:
kayam > kacam.
kayam eṉpatu āḻamāṉa nīrnili. mēliruntu pārkka nīrum teriyāmal iruḷē terintamiyāl, iruṭṭuk kacam eṉpar oru ceṟinta kāṭō, veḷiccamilā vīṭō iruṇṭiruttal iruṭṭuk kacamāka irukkiṟatu eṉa vaḻakkil ūṉṟiyatu itu mukavi, nelli vaḻakku.
kacavāḷi:
kacam > kacavu > kacavāḷi.
kayami eṉpatu kayam eṉṉum iruṇmip poruḷatu. iruṇmi yuḷḷattāṉ kayavaṉ; kayattaṉmi yuṭiyavaṉ kayavāḷi (kacavāḷi) karumi eṉappaṭum taṉmiyaṉi avaṉ īyākkacavāḷi eṉpatum, kacavāḷi eṉpatum teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku.
kacaṟal:
kacaṟu + al = kacaṟal
tara iyalāta - vilimāṉam kaṭṭāta - poruḷi mīḷa mīḷak kuṟittuk kēṭṭup peṟuvatik kacaṟal eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kacaṟuvārkkum tārātavarip picunāṟi eṉap paḻippatum vaḻakku. piyttut tarutal illāta iḻivaṉ picunāṟi. kacanāṟikku ēṟṟa picunāṟi eṉpatum makkaḷ vaḻakkē.
kacāyam:
kaḻāyam > kacāyam.
naṉṟākak kaḻuviyum, tūymi ceytum, mācu kaḷintu kaḻa kaḻa  eṉak kotikkak kotikka vēkaviṭṭu vaṭikaṭṭip pakkuvappaṭuttum maruttuva muṟi vaṭippu nīr, kaḻāyam eṉappaṭum. atu makkaḷ vaḻakkil kacāyamāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
mūṉṟu vēḷi kaḻāyam (kacāyam) kuṭi. katakatappup pōyviṭum (ma.va.)
kacāli:
aḵkam + cāli = aḵka cāli > kacāli.
māṭu kaṭṭum toḻuvik kacāli eṉpatu paramakkuṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum atu, aḵka cāli (akka cāli) eṉpataṉ mutaṟkuṟiyākum tavacam koṭṭum koṭṭilum, kācu aṭikkum kolluliyum aminta iṭam aḵka cāli eṉappaṭṭatu akkam = tavacam, kācu. koṟkiyi aṭuttuḷḷa akkācāli, paḻiya aḵka cāliyām.
māṭu eṉpatu celvam ātalāl ataṉik kaṭṭiya iṭam akkacāli eṉappaṭṭu, kacāli āyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:
kacālit teru (cēṟṟūr, virutunakar māvaṭṭam).
kacital:
kacivu > kacital = aṉpuṟutal.
putu maṇ pāṉiyil nīr vittāl kacivu uṇṭākum atupōl kuḷakkari, vayaṟkari, varappu ākiyavaṟṟilum nīr uḷḷa pōtu veḷiyē kacivatuṇṭām. kacital eṉpatu nīr ciṟitaḷavāy ūṟi veḷippaṭutalām.
iṉi, ikkacital aṉpuṭiyārkkum uḷḷami,
aṉpiṟkum uṇṭō aṭikkuntāḻ ārvalar
puṉkaṇīr pūcal tarum
eṉṉum kuṟaḷāl (71) viḷaṅkum. oruvaṉiṭattē aṉpu uḷḷatu eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka iruppatu kaṇṇīr eṉappaṭutalāl akkaṇṇīrk kacivē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭu kiṟatām.  uṉakkuk kacivē illi; uṉ maṉam eṉṉa irumpā? kallā? eṉa vetumpi urippār uriyil kacivu aṉpātal viḷaṅkum.
kacippu:
 kacital > kacippu.
kacital vaḻital, aṉpu celuttutal eṉṉum poruḷatu kaciya māṭṭāṉ eṉṟāl, kāṭṭamāṭṭāṉ eṉpatu nīr coṭṭutal iṉṟic ciṟitu patappaṭuttum niliyik kacital (kaṇṇīr) eṉpar.
paṭṭi mutaliyavaṟṟik kāyccit tuḷittuḷiyāka vaṭiyac ceyyum cārāyattik kacippu eṉpatu yāḻppāṇa vaḻakku.
kacimpu:
kacital > kacimpu.
taṇṇīr oḻukutal, vaḻital , vaṭital, koṭṭil, coṭṭutal, tuḷittal, pocital, kacital eṉa aḷavumikuti curukkam eṉpavi paṟṟiya pala peyarkaḷip peṟum. kacital eṉpatik kacimpu eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu nelli vaḻakku.
kaccakam:
kaccu + akam = kaccakam. kaccu = iṟukkamākak kaṭṭuvatu.
kiyāl iṟukkamākap paṟṟip piṭikkum kuraṅku kaccakam eṉappaṭum (ve. vi.pē) kuraṅkuppiṭi eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaccakkāy:
kaccu > kaccam > kaccakkāy.
kaccal eṉpatu ciṟu eṉṉum poruḷatu. ciṟiya vāḻippaḻam katali eṉappaṭuvatum, ciṟiya mīṉ kacali eṉappaṭuvatum potuvaḻakku. kaccal eṉpatu māmpiñcu eṉṉum poruḷil vantu ataṉi veṭṭi ūṟappōṭutalākiya ūṟukāyik kaccakkāy eṉpatu virutunakar vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. vaṭu eṉpatu māmpiñciṉ potuvaḻakku.
kaccam:
kaccam:1
kaccu + am = kaccam = iṟukkik kaṭṭiya kayiṟu.
kaccam tiṉṟa kaḻal tayaṅku tiruntaṭi	-maturik. 436
poruḷ: kōttukaṭṭiya kaccuk kiṭantu taḻumpirunta vīrakkaḻaliyum piṟakkiṭāta aṭiyiṉiyum  (uri. nac)
kaccam, koccak kayiṟu eṉa makkaḷāl vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṟatu.
kaccam:2
kaccam = kaṭaṉ.
makaḷir mārppuk kaccu, āṭavar iṭikkaccu, pōrvīrariṉ meymmaṟi eṉpavi ellām poruḷkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭum. ivaṟṟoṭu kaṭaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ itaṟkuṇmi arumi mikkatām. oruvari iṟukkik kaṭṭic ceyalaṟac ceyvatu kaṭaṉātal māṉap piṟaviyarpāl mikap poruntuvatām. kavali kaṟiyit tiṉṉum eṉpar. māṉikaḷ paṭṭa kaṭaṉ kavali, uyiriyē tiṉpatu kaṇkūṭu.
kaccam:3
kaccam = āmi.
kaṭṭiyāṉatum iṟukkamikkatumām  ōṭṭiyuṭiya āmi eṉpatum kaccam eṉappaṭum. (ve.vi.pē)
kaccammāḷ:
kaccu + ammāḷ = kaccammāḷ.
aṭuttaṭuttup piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟu vāyilum vayiṟṟilum kiyilum kālilum eṉpatu pōla ciṉṉañ ciṟiyavarkaḷi uṭiyavaḷik kaccammāḷ eṉpatu ucilampaṭṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. iṅkuk kaccu eṉpatu aṭuttaṭuttu neruṅkip piṟanta ciṟumakkaḷik kuṟittu varukiṉṟatu.
kaccāṅkāṟṟu:
kaccu + ām + kāṟṟu = kaccāṅkāṟṟu.
teṉmēṟkup pakkattil iruntu aṭikkum kāṟṟik kaccāṅkāṟṟu eṉpatu teṉtamiḻaka - kuṟippākak kumari māvaṭṭa - vaḻakku. kaccāṅkāṟṟu vīciṉāl maḻivarum eṉpar kaccu eṉpatu iṟukkam, ceṟivu maḻinīr ceṟinta kāṟṟu kaccāṅ kāṟṟu.
kaccāṭi:
kaccu + āṭi = kakkāṭi
kaccaṇam eṉpatu tāyccīli; atu koṅku vaṭṭārattil kaccāṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu kaccaṇam, kaccaṇat tuṇi, tāyccīli eṉpavi nelli, mukavi māvaṭṭa vaḻakkukaḷ.
kaccāṉ:
ciṟu tūṟālāka viḻuvatik kaccāṉ eṉpatu kumari māvaṭṭa vaḻakkākum. kaccu eṉpatu ciṟumip poruḷ vaḻiyāka aminta kaccu eṉpatu ciṟitāya tuṇiyāl aminta mārpuk kaṭṭu eṉpatum kaccaṇam eṉpatu āṇpāl ariyil kaṭṭum tuṇi eṉpatum kaccu eṉpataṉ ciṟumip poruḷ kāṭṭum iṟukkap poruḷum tarum.
* kaccakkāy kāṇka.
kaccil:
kaccu + il = kaccil
iṭiveḷiyiṉṟik paṟṟik kiṭakkum muṭkīṟṟukaḷik koṇṭirukkum ī(r)ntu kaccil eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭutal. ataṉ iṟukkattiyum iṭippukal ariya taṉmiyiyum kāṭṭum kaccūram eṉpatum itu.
kaccu:
kaccu:1
kaccu = arippaṭṭiki.
kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ	-tirumuru. 208
kaccu:2
tōṭpaṭṭiki.

kaccu:3
kutiri mutuku vār.
ciṟumaṇi toṭarntu peruṅkaccu niṟīi	-naṟ. 220 (ca.i.po.ka)
kaccu:4
makaḷir mārpukkaṭṭu kaccu eṉappaṭutal paṇṭu mutal iṉṟaḷavum uṇṭu.
kacci:
oru kaṭṭi naṉṟākap pituṅkāmal otuṅkāmal iṟukkik kaṭṭuvatik kacceṉak kaṭṭutal eṉpar. nekiḻak kaṭṭum vēṭṭiyiṉum iṟukkik kaṭṭum vīrar uṭi kacci eṉappaṭṭatu.
nīlak kacci pūvār āṭi
eṉpatu puṟappāṭal (274)
kaccit toṅkaliṉṟi mēlē tūkkik kaṭṭutal uḻavar vaḻakkam avvuṭi kaccaṇam eṉappaṭum. kaccu + aṇ + am = kaccaṇam. aṇ =mēlē ariñāṇ mēl tūkki iṟukkik kaṭṭal atu.
kacci kaṭṭal:
kacci kaṭṭal = ēviviṭal.
kacci eṉpatu iṭuppil kaṭṭum uṭiyiyum, iṭivāriyum kuṟikkum. kacci kaṭṭutal pōrkkup pukuvār ceyal. ataṉāl kacci kaṭṭutal eṉpatu ēviviṭal poruḷukku uriyatāyiṟṟu. oruvar etirpārā etirppiṉ pōtō, taṭuppiṉ pōtō amintiruppār. avaric cila cila colli etirttu eḻuvataṟkum, tākkutaṟkum ēviviṭṭu viṭuvar cilar. itaṉik kacci kaṭṭutal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
kañalal:
kaṉalal > kañalal.
neruṅkal, viḷaṅkutal, oḷiviṭal, niṟital ākiya polivum valivumām poruḷ tarum , kañaṟal eṉpatum itu. (ve.vi.pē).
kaliyiṉ vāḻvu kañalum vāḻvuk kaṉṉiyēkuṇṭala.tamiḻvāḻttu
kañcakam:
kāñcakam > kañcakam = kaṟivēppili.
kāynta eṇṇey mēl pōṭṭu vaṟuttu allatu porittu kāynta kuḻampu, cāṟu toṭukaṟi ivaṟṟiṉ mēl pōṭṭut tāḷikka utavutalāl kaṟivēppili kañcakam eṉappaṭṭatu.
cētā naṟumōr veṇṇeyiṉ mātuḷan
turuppuṟu pacuṅkāyp pōḻoṭu kaṟikalantu
kañcaka naṟumuṟi aḷii-perum. 306 - 308
kañcaṉ:
kaccaṉ > kañcaṉ.
kaccal pataṉ keṭṭa ciṟiya vāḻikkāy; taḷḷupaṭiyākiya kāy; avvāṟu ciṟukuṇamum īviṉiyum uṭiyāṉ kaccaṉām kañcaṉ. kuṭikkum kañciyum utavāk karumi kañcaṉ eṉiṉumām (ma. va)
kañcaṉ taṉmi, kañcattaṉam.
puṇṇukkuk kaṭṭac cuṇṇāmpum tārātavaṉ ; eccil kiyāl kākkiyum ōṭṭātavaṉ; īrak kiyiyum utaṟātavaṉ eṉpavi paḻamoḻikaḷ.
kañci:
kañci:1
kāycci vaṭittuk koṭṭiyatik kañci eṉal mutuvaḻakku, vaṭikañci eṉpatu putuvaḻakku, karical uṇavik kañci eṉal potuvaḻakku.
cōṟuvākkiya koḻuṅkañci
āṟupōlap parantoḻuki	-paṭ. 44-45
kañci:2
kañcattaṉam eṉpatiyuṭiyavaṉik kañci eṉpatu ma.va kañcaṉ eṉpatu āṇpāl. kañcam = karumittaṉam.
kañci:3
kāñci  eṉpataṉ mutaṟkuṟukkam
kañci kāyccal:
kañci kāyccal = kiṇṭal ceytal.
kēḻvaraku, cōḷam, kampu ākiya tavacaṅkaḷi, iṭittu, arittu māvākki ūṟa vittup kiṇṭik kiṇṭik kañci kāyccutal vaḻakkam. kañciyāvataṟkuḷ atu paṭumpāṭu perumpāṭu. appāṭukaḷ ellām oruvaṉip paṭuttutal kañci kāyccalāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatām. kiṇṭal, kēli, nakiyāṇṭi paṭuttutalē iṅkuk kañci kāyccal. kiṇṭal eṉpatu kīḻmēlākavum mēlkīḻākavum puraṭṭip puraṭṭi eṭuttal, uppumā kiṇṭal, kōḻi kiṇṭal aṟika.
kēḷikki, kēḷiyāyk kēliyāy uḷḷatu. nakiyāṇṭi niyāṇṭi āyiṟṟu.
kañci taṇṇīr:
	kañci taṇṇīr 	= 	kañciyum, taṇṇīrum.
	kañci	=	nīrāḷamākak kāyccap peṟṟa  cōṟṟup poṟukkum nīrum.
	taṇṇīr 	=	cōṟṟil viṭṭuvittuk kāṭiyāṉa nīr.
kañci, aṉṉappāl eṉappaṭum. aṉṉappāl kāṇāta ēḻikaṭku nalla āviṉpāl eṅkē kiṭikkumammā? eṉpatu vaṟumiyar viṉā? kañcippaci, kañcik kalayam, kañcit toṭṭi eṉpavi evarum aṟintavi. kañciyi aṉṉacārak kañci eṉṟum maruttuvar cuṭṭuvar.
taṇṇīr eṉpatu cōṟṟut taṇṇīr: taṇṇīr eṉpatum atu puḷippu mikkatākaliṉ atu kāṭi eṉavum paṭum. kāṭik kañciyāṉālum mūṭik kuṭi eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. kañci taṇṇikku vaḻiyilli eṉpatu vaṟumi oli.
kañcukam:
kāyntu > kāñcu > kāñcukam > kañcakam = caṭṭi.
uṭalil veppō kuḷirō tākkāmal aḷavē veppamirukka aṇiyum caṭṭi kañcukamākum paṇṭu, caṭṭi iṭṭa mutaṉmiyar(pirāṉiyar) kañcuka māntar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.

nīlaṉ mutaliya kañcuka mākkaḷ	-cilap. 28: 80
eṇ pērāyattār uṭikaḷuḷ oṉṟu kañcukam (cilap. 5: 157. arum.)

kaṭakam:
kaṭakam ōr aṇikalam. āṇkaḷ kaṭakam aṇivatik kampar, kaṭakakki puṭittu eṉṟu kuṟippār (ayō. 414). kaṭakam vaḷivu uṭiyatu. paṉinārāl piṉṉappaṭum perum peṭṭi kaṭakappeṭṭi. kaṭakappeṭṭi eṉappaṭuvatu kumari nelli vaḻakkākum.

aṇikalattiyum, aṇinta iṭattiyum cuṭṭukiṟatu ivvaṭi.
kaṭakāl:
nīr ēṟā mēṭṭukku nīr ēṟṟa iṟipeṭṭi pōṭṭu aḷḷi viṭuvar.  atu iruvar, nālvar ceyyum paṇi. nīri oruvar aḷḷi viṭum aḷavil amintatu kaṭakāl (kaṭi kāl) eṉappaṭṭatu. atu, nelli māvaṭṭa vaḻakkukaḷil uḷḷatu.
kaṭattal:
piṟar aṟiyā vakiyil iruvar pāli nilattuk kaṭantu cellutal kaṭattal ākiyatu.
koṇṭu talikkaḻittal eṉpatu ilakkaṇar vaḻakku atu piṉṉē kaṭantu pōtal poruḷāki vaḷarntatu ataṉpiṉ piḻiyāṉa vaḻiyilē peṇṇik kaṭattik koṇṭu pōtaliyum, poruḷkaḷiyum, poruḷpaṟikkum vakiyākap piḷḷikaḷik kaṭattik koṇṭu pōtaliyum ulakam kāṇkiṟatu
kaṭattal kēṭṭit taṭuttu niṟutta oṟṟum, kāvalum paṭiyum tiṇaṟal kaṇkūṭāyuḷḷatu. kaṭattal kaṭattutalum ākum.
kaṭantaṭutal:
kaṭantu + aṭutal = kaṭantaṭutal.
maṟintō muṟimāṟākavō tākkāmal nērukku nērāka niṉṟu pakiyit tākki aḻippatu kaṭantu aṭutal ākum. kaṭantu eṉpatāl taṉ nāṭu kaṭantu pakivaṉ nāṭu ceṉṟu aḻittalum ākum.

kaṭamalikkuṇṭu:
kaṭam eṉpatu matattiyum, matayāṉiyiyum, matayāṉi vāḻum malippakutiyiyum, yāṉik kūṭṭattiyum kuṟikkum. kaṭamali eṉa iṇiyum pōtu matamali eṉa āki yāṉiyi uruvakattāl kuṟikkum.
malipaṭukaṭām eṉpatu pattupāṭṭuḷ pattām pāṭṭu. appāṭṭiṉ peyark kāraṇam viḷakkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. malikku yāṉiyi uvamittu ataṉkaṭ piṟanta ōciyik kaṭāmeṉac ciṟappitta vataṉāl ippāṭṭiṟku malipaṭukaṭām eṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉār eṉkiṟār.
maliyi yāṉikku uvamiyākak kūṟuvatu paḻavaḻakku, varimaruḷ vēḻam eṉṟu malipaṭukaṭāmum, maliyeṉat tēṉiṟi koḷḷum irumpal yāṉi eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟum (17) pukalukiṉṟaṉa.
kuṇṭu eṉpatu āḻperum akaḻiyiyum paḷḷattiyum kuṟikkum kuṇṭukaṇ akaḻi eṉṟār aiyūr mūlaṅkiḻar (puṟam. 21) ivaṟṟāl kaṭamalikkuṇṭu eṉpatu matamaliyākiya yāṉikaḷ mikunta paḷḷattākku eṉpatām. piṉṉē ataṉic cārnta ūrik kuṟittatu.

kaṭami:

kaṭami:3
kaṭaṉ > kaṭam > kaṭami.
ulakam nammit tantatu; uṟṟār uṟaviṉar utaviṉar; iyaṟki vaḷam corintu vāḻa vikkiṟatu; ivaṟṟi yellām peṟṟu vāḻum nām, avaṟṟukkuc ceyya vēṇṭuvatu eṉṉa? nam piṟavimutal peṟṟa nalaṅkaḷukkuk kimmāṟākac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu kaṭamiyākum entak kimmāṟṟiyum etirpārāta koṭikaḷ ivi. ikkoṭiyik kimmāṟu vēṇṭāk kaṭappāṭu eṉṉum kuṟaḷ (211). ceyyāmal ceyta utavi eṉṟum collum (101). piṟappurimiyoṭu vantatu nam kaṭami allatu kaṭaṉ oruvariṭam peṟṟa kaṭaṉik kaṭṭāyam tīrkka vēṇṭuvatu pōlak kimmāṟu karutātu vaḻaṅkiyavaṟṟukku nām ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉavē kaṭami - kaṭappāṭu - eṉpavi.
ikkaṭamiyē ulaki vāḻa vikkum. nammi ulakil vāḻntu kaṭami ceytavarākap pativu ceyyum! kaṭami ceyyār peṟṟa urimikaḷ pāḻum urimikaḷ; ulakukkuk kēṭāṉavi.
urimiyip peṟuvatu uyiriṉ nōkku. āṉāl ataṟkut taravēṇṭiya vili kaṭamiyākum tattam kaṭamiyic ceyyāmal kālam taḷḷupavar ulakil iruntu kaṭaṉ kārarākavē pōvavarāvar.
urimiyip peṟu; kaṭamiyic cey ivviraṇṭum poṟkāciṉ iru pakkaṅkaḷākum.

kaṭampāl:
kaṭam eṉpatu kāṭu, ceṟivu eṉṉum poruḷatu. tali kāṭākak kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpatu pēccu vaḻakku ceṟivuṭiya allatu keṭṭiyāṉa cīmpālik kaṭampāl eṉpatu virutunakar vaṭṭāra vaḻakku.
kaṭampu:
kaṭam > kaṭampu.
valimi mikkatoru maram; kaṭappamaram eṉpatum atu kaṭampa marattik kāval maramākak koṇṭavaṉ naṉṉaṉ. eṉavē. kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil naṉṉaṉ  eṉappaṭṭāṉ (patiṟ. pati. 4)
kaṭampu māli aṇintavaṉum kaṭampa marattamarnta vaṉumām murukaṉ kaṭampaṉ kaṭampamar celvaṉ (pari. 8) kaṭampamar neṭuvēḷ (perum. 75-76) eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
kaṭampaṉ eṉpatu oru kuṭiyiṉ peyar.
tuṭiyaṉ pāṇaṉ paṟiyaṉ kaṭampaṉeṉ

kaṭampiṉ tiṇmiyum tiraṭciyum,
tiṇinilik kaṭampiṉ tiraḷari	-kuṟiñ. 176
eṉpatāl viḷaṅkum.
kaṭampa maram muracam ceyvataṟku ēṟṟatu eṉpatu patiṟṟuppattāl viḷaṅkum (patiṟ. 11,17; akam 347).
kaṭali:
kaṭa > kaṭal > kaṭali.
kaṭali eṉpatu kuṟuṅ koṭivaki; atu ceṭi vakiyākavum vaḷarntatu. kaṭalik koṭiyiṉ āṇivēr pakkavēr callivēr eṉap pala vērkaḷil, āṇivēril kaṭali kāyttal illi. āṇi vēr kaṭanta pakkavēr callivēr āyavaṟṟil kottuk kottākak kaṭalikkāy kāykkum. akkāyiṉ koṭṭi kaṭali eṉappaṭum. pacciyākavō avittō vaṟuttō tiṉṉa utavum. eṇṇey eṭukkavum piṇṇākkup peṟavum ākum.
maṇilāvil iruntu iṅku vantatāl maṇilāk koṭṭi eṉappaṭum makkaḷ vaḻakkil mallāṅkoṭṭi eṉa vaḻu vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. vēril kāyppatāl vērkkaṭali eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
nilattuḷ kāy iruppatāl nilakkaṭali eṉappaṭṭatu. itupōṉṟa oṉṟu ceṭiyāyt talimēl kottuk kottākak kāyttup pacciyākat tiṉṉavum avittum vaṟuttum tiṉṉavum, māvu ākkiya payaṉpaṭavum āyatik koṇṭik kaṭali eṉṟaṉar ataṉi vaṟuttum porittum payaṉpaṭuttuvatāl vaṟukaṭali, porikaṭali eṉṟaṉar. poṭṭu paruppiṉ mēl iruppatāl poṭṭukkaṭali eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
vēril kāykkāmal ceṭiyiṉ ucciyil - koṇṭiyil - kāyttalāl koṇṭik kaṭali eṉappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu paṭṭāṇi eṉṉum payaṟum veḷi mānilattil iruntu vantuḷḷatu. veḷḷip paṭṭāṇi eṉappaṭukiṟatu atu. kaṭali paḻampayiri eṉpatiyum, ataṉi neyyil vaṟuttut tiṉṟaṉar eṉpatiyum puṟanāṉūṟu (120) kūṟukiṟatu.
naṟuneyk kaṭali vicippac cōṟaṭṭu
poruḷ: naṟiya neyyilē kaṭali tuḷḷa ataṉoṭu cōṟṟi aṭṭu pa.u
kaṭalikkāy:
kāy eṉpatu ceṭi, koṭi, maraṅkaḷil kāyppatu. vēril tōṉṟuvatik kāy eṉpatu illi. nilakkaṭali eṉṟē kūṟuvar āṉāl cēlam māvaṭṭattār kaṭalikkāy eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. maṇilāk koṭṭi eṉpatu atu vanta varalāṟu kūṟuvatu.
kaṭal:
kaṭa > kaṭal.
elli kaṭanta viriviṉatum, nāṭu viṭṭu nāṭu cellak kaṭakkumvaḻiyāka iruntatum kaṭal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum peyarum, kaṭaṟkaṭampart tolitta vīṟum puṟappāṭṭum patiṟṟup pattum pukalum. kaṭal, kappal eṉpavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷi eṇṇiya aḷavāl tamiḻar kaṭalāṭciyiṉ māṭci pulappaṭum. kaṭaluḷ māynta iḷam peruvaḻuti eṉpāṉ oru pāṇṭiyaṉ. tēṉakkavār poḻil māṉakkavāram, munnīrp paḻantīvu paṉṉīrāyiram eṉpavi ellām veṉṟu vanta cōḻar meykkīrttikaḷ mikappala.
kaṭal kaṭantu paṉṉāṭṭu vāṇikam ceytavar tamiḻar. ātalāl avvaṇika nāvāy ulaka valam varuvataṟku muṟpaṭṭē iṭappaṭṭatāka vēṇṭum. iccol. aruvi, āṟu, ēri, kuḷam, teppam, ēntal, tāṅkal eṉa ēṟpaṭṭa avaṟṟiṉum kaṭattaṟku arumiyum nōkkil aṭaṅkā neṭumiyum karuti iṭappaṭṭa peyar itu.
ceṟivik kāṭu eṉṟum, pēruyaratti vāṉam eṉṟum karutiyamiyāl nilam kāṭākak kiṭakkiṟatu, talikāṭākak kiṭakkiṟatu eṉṟum vāṉkuruvi vāṉkōḻi, vāṉavar nāṭu eṉṟum peyariṭṭatu pōl, kaṭal perum parappiṉatum vaḷamikkatumām nilinōkki tuṇikkaṭal, nūṟkaṭal, tuyarkkaṭal, iṭarkaṭal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
kaṭal cārnta ūr kaṭalūr eṉappaṭutal pōl kaṭal cārāta nīr nilikaḷum kaṭal eṉa makkaḷ vaḻakkil ākiṉa. nāri paṟavāta nāṟpatteṭṭu maṭikaḷiyuṭiyatu irācaciṅka maṅkala ēri eṉavum, mākkaṭalām maturāntaka ēri eṉavum, kuṟṟālattiṉ pēraruvikku mēl poṅkumā kaṭal eṉavum peyariṭṭaṉar.
ampā camuttiram, taḷapati camuttiram, tātā camuttiram, tirumali camuttiram, vāṇa camuttiram eṉa ērikaḷi mākaṭal (camuttiram) ākki avaṟṟiṉ peyarāl ūrppeyarkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
kaṭal cūḻnta ulaki mānīr vēli eṉṟaṉar(mutumoḻik) nīrārum kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭanti eṉṟu maṉōṉmaṇiyam cuntaraṉārum, puṭavikkaṇi tukileṉa vaḷar antakkaṭal eṉa aruṇakiriyārum(tirup) cuṭṭiṉar.
virivuṟak kaṟṟāri ilakkaṇakkaṭal, ilakkiyakkaṭal , tamiḻkkaṭal eṉalum paṇiyiṉ mikkāri, aṟakkaṭal aruḷkaṭal eṉalum cila vīṭukaḷik kaṭal pōla eṉalum pulamakkaḷ potumakkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷāka uḷa. kaṭalpōl makkaḷ kaṭal pōl paṭi eṉalum uṇṭu.
kaṭalaṉṉa kāmam 	-eṉpār vaḷḷuvar(1137)

kaṭaṟ ceyti paṟṟiya caṅkap pāṭalkaḷ mikappala. neytaṟ pāṭalkaḷik kaṭaṟpāṭalkaḷ eṉṟu colliviṭalām allāvō. ēlēlō pāṭṭuk kappaṟ pāṭṭē.

kaṭiki:1
kaṭi eṉpatu ōr uriccol. ati,

virivē accam
eṉap palaporuḷ tarum(naṉ. 457). kālam virintu cellum iyaṟkiyatu. ataṉ virivu kaṇṇimi, kinnoṭi eṉa noṭik kaṇakkil kaṇṭu ataṉiyum pakuttaṉar. noṭippakuppu ulaka viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷilum aṟiviyal viṇkalam ceykikkōḷ ēvalilum mikattulliyamākak kaṇakkiṭap paṭum.
paḻanāḷil poḻutu kāṇak kaṭiki eṉa oṉṟu vittiruntaṉar. atu nīr coṭṭal, maṇal corital koṇṭu aḷakkappaṭṭatu. katiriṉ niḻal koṇṭum aḷantatuṇṭu. itukāl kaṭikiyāram avviṭattip paṟṟik koṇṭuḷḷatu.
kaṭiki:2

kaṭittal:
kaṭittal:1
vaṉmiyāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷip pallāl valuvākak kaṭittut tiṉpatu kaṭittalām. kaṭittuk karumpiṉik kaṇtakara nūṟi eṉṟu nālaṭiyār kūṟum (156). kaṭittal vēṟu; kaṟittal vēṟu.
* kaṟittal kāṇka.
kaṭittal:2
kaṭittal = caṇṭiyiṭal.
nāy pūṉi mutaliyavi oṉṟiyoṉṟu pakittal kaṭippālēyē tam pakiyit tīrkkum. kaṭittal avaṟṟiṉ caṇṭikku aṟikuṟi. āṉāl, annāyum pūṉiyum naṭpāka irukkum pōtum kaṭikkum. ataṉic caṇṭik kaṭiyākak koḷvatilli. poykkaṭi, aṉpukkaṭi eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟē neruṅkip paḻakiya iruvar taṅkaḷukkuḷ caṇṭiyiṭum pōtu, eṉṉa iruvarum intak kaṭi kaṭikkiṟīrkaḷ! intak kacivum vēṇṭām; intak kaṭiyum vēṇṭām eṉṟu avarkaḷi aṟintōr aṟivuri kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. pōtum kaṭiyātē eṉac caṇṭiyiṭuvar taṅkaḷukkuḷ kūṟuvatum uṇṭu. kaṭippavar aṭutta nēramē kaṭi maṟantu kacipavar eṉka.
kaṭippāṉ:
mukaṭṭup pūcci eṉappaṭum mūṭṭip pūcciyik kaṭippāṉ eṉpatu irācapāḷiya vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. mūṭṭik kaṭi tāṅkavilli eṉpatu pēccu vaḻakku. kūḻukkut toṭukaṟiyik kaṭippāṉ eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku. kaṟittal = kaṭittal. iṉṉam pacumpul kaṟikkallā iḷaṅkaṉṟu eṉpatu kumarakuruparar vākku (mīṉāṭ. piḷ. 24)


kaṭippu:
kaṭippatu, kaṭittut tiṉṉum poruḷum kaṭippu eṉappaṭum. karuppuk kaṭṭiyik kaṭittuk koṇṭu kuṭikkum tēnīr kaṭiveḷḷam eṉappaṭutal maliyāḷa vaḻakku. irumuṉikaḷum kuvip piṭikkum iṭukki eṉṉum karuviyik kaṭippāṉ eṉpatu maturi, nelli māvaṭṭa vaḻakkākum.
kaṭimaram:
kaṭi + maram = kaṭimaram. kaṭi = uriccol.
kaṭiyeṉ kiḷavi kāppē kūrmi eṉṉum naṉṉūl(457). atil mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum kāppumaram kaṭimaramām kāval maram.
ūravar maṭṭum kākkum maramaṉṟu. nāṭum aracum oruṅkē kaṇṇum karuttumākak kākkum maram atuvām. atu koḻumi nīṅkik kāyavum kāṇap poṟār. aracaṉ vāḻvum tāḻvum aḻivum ammaramē eṉṉum kaṭippiṭiyāl aracuṭaṉ makkaḷum ataṉic cērak kāttaṉar. ak kaṭimaram pakivarāl aḻikkappaṭumāṉal araciyē aḻittatākak koṇṭu māṉap pōr toṭuppatu vaḻakkam.
kaṭimaram pakivarāl veṭṭappaṭum pōtu ataṉi aṟintum kāvalaṉ oruvaṉ kōṭṭikkuḷ patuṅki iruttali aṟintu,
kaṭimaram taṭiyu mōci taṉṉūr
neṭumatil varippil kaṭimaṉi iyampa....
malittaṉi eṉpatu  nāṇut taka uṭittē 	-(puṟam.36)
eṉkiṟār ālattūrkiḻār.
kaṭimarattil yāṉiyik kaṭṭutal iḻivu ceytalākak karutappaṭmiyāl, peruñcittiraṉār tamakku muṟiyoṭu paricil nalkāta veḷimāṉi ikaḻum vakiyāl kumaṇaṉ koṭutta yāṉiyi veḷimāṉ kaṭimarattil kaṭṭi,

kaṭiya neṭuvēṭṭuvaṉ:
kōṭi malit talivaṉ; vaḷḷal; peruntalic cāttaṉārāl pāṭappeṟṟavaṉ. (puṟam. 205).
kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār:
toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ mēl perum pāṇāṟṟup paṭiyiyum, karikāṟcōḻaṉ paṭṭiṉap pāliyiyum pāṭiyavar.
patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉparicu karikālaṉiṭam peṟṟavar eṉpatum, paṭṭiṉap pāli kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattup patiṉāṟukāl maṇṭapattu araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatum ciṟappuc ceytikaḷām.
akanāṉūṟṟilum (167), kuṟuntokiyilum (352) ivar pāṭalkaḷ uṇṭu.

kaṭu = nañcu

kaṭukaṭuppu:
ciṉattiṉ kuṟi; tēṉi, tēḷ koṭṭiyatāl uṇṭākum valip perukkam.

kaṭuku:
kaḷ + tu =kaṭu. kaḷ = kariyatu. kaṭu > kaṭuku = kariyatu.
kaṭippaki, aiyavi eṉpaṉavum kaṭukiṉ peyar. kaṭuku aḷavāl ciṟiyatu. ataṉāl ciṟṟaḷaviṉik kaṭukattaṉi eṉpar. kaṭuku kīḻē viḻuntāl uruṇṭu virivāka ōṭi maṟiyum; atē pōl kuyyil (tāḷitattil) vēkkāṭṭu eṇṇeyil pōṭṭāl virintu veṭittup pakkamellām maṇam parappum. ivvirivu, kaṭuka - viriya eṉap poruḷ tantatu; kaṭi eṉṉum uric col mūlamum āyiṟṟu.
kaṭuku ciṟuttālum kāram pōkātu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. kaṭukit tuḷittu eṉpatu tiruvaḷḷuva māli.
kaṭu, kaṭumip poruḷ tarutal vaḻiyāyk kaṭuvaṉ eṉa āṭavar peyarum, āṇ kuraṅkiṉ peyarum āyiṟṟu.
kaṭukkaṉ:
kaṭukki eṉpatu koṉṟi. ataṉpūp pōṉṟatum āṇkaḷ kātil aṇivatumām aṇikalam kaṭukkaṉ eṉap paṭutal nelli vaḻakku. mukkaṭṭu eṉṉum kātaṇiyil kal uṇṭu. kaṭukkaṉil kal illi eṉpatutāṉ vēṟupāṭu.
kaṭukkāy:
kaṭukkāy:1
viralāl kuṭintu eṭuttalum, ataṉi uṇṇalum nuṅkutal eṉappaṭum. nuṅkiṉāṉ pacikaḷ āṟa eṉpatu iraṭcaṇiya yāttirikam. avvāṟu viralāl patikka muṭiyā vāṟu keṭṭippaṭṭa nuṅkik kaṭukkāy eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku.
kaṭukkāy:2
kaṭukkāy eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭum maruntup poruḷ, karumi eṉṉum niṟappeyar vaḻiyatu ( e-ṭu: tirikaṭukam = mummaruntu). tuvarppuc cuviyil kaṭukkāy ciṟantatu.
kaṭukkāyum tāyum karutiloṉṟeṉ ṟālum

kaṭukkukkaṭukku:
keṭṭiyāṉa tiṉpaṇṭattik kaṭukkuk kaṭukku eṉak kaṭittut tiṉṟa nāy pōyiṟṟu eṉpatu pal pōyviṭṭa mutiyavar pāṭu.
kaṭuppāṉ:
tayir mōr viṭṭu uṇpataṟku ūṟukāy pōṉṟa maṟṟoṉṟu tuviyal ākum. atu maṟṟit toṭukaṟi viṭukaṟi ākiyavaṟṟiṉum urippu tūkkutalāka irukkum. akkaṭumi karutik kaṭuppāṉ eṉpatu oṭṭaṉ cattira vaṭṭāra vaḻakku veṟṟu vaṟṟali arittē kārat tuviyal eṉa uṇpatum uṇṭu. ataṉ kaṭuppu kaṇṇīr vara vikkavum vallatu.
kaṭuppu:
kaṭumi eṉṉum poruḷil kaṭuppu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu potupporuḷ. atu kaṭuñciṉam, uḷvēkkāṭu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkutal vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. avaṉ kaṭuppu  iṉṉum tīravilli eṉpar. uṉ kaṭuppu eṉṉi eṉṉa ceytuviṭum? eṉpatum uṇṭu. nīrkkaṭuppu vayiṟṟukkaṭuppu eṉpaṉa veppu mikiyāl ēṟpaṭum tuyar. itu teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku.
kaṭuvāt tāḷ:
	kaṭuvāt tāḷ 	= 	nūṟu urupāt tāḷ.
kaṭuvāy eṉpatu perumpuli! patiṉāṟu aṭi tāvum vēṅkiyik kaṭuvāy eṉpar. ataṉ piḷanta peruvāyiyum ataṉkoṭuṅkāṭciyiyum kaṇṭu kaṭuvāy eṉṟaṉar. kaṭuvāy eḷimiyākak kāṇak kūṭiya vilaṅkaṉṟu. ceṟinta kāṭukaḷiṉ iṭiyē aritil vāḻvatu. ataṉit tēṭi muyaṉṟē kāṇa muṭiyum. atu pōl aritil kāṇak kūṭiya periya paṇattāḷ kaṭuvāt tāḷ eṉappaṭṭatu. muṉpu, nūṟu maṭṭum tēṭu; nūṟṟukku mēl ūṟṟu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. ippoḻutu nūṟu urupā eṉpatu, paḻiya callik kācu nūṟukku oppu. kaṭuvā tāḷ eṉpatu paḻanāḷ paṇattāḷ matippi viḷakkum varalāṟṟu vaḻakkāṟām.
kaṭuvāy:
perumpuli allatu varippulikku orupeyar kaṭuvāy eṉpatu. kaṭuvāyppuli eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. ataṉ vāyamippu virivum ataṉi akala virittuk kaṭikkum kaṭumiyum oru cērak kuṟittatāy makkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu kaṭuvāy eṉpatām.
kaṭuvāyp puli:
	kaṭuvāy	=	koṭumiyāṉa vāyiyuṭiya perumpuli.
	puli	=	uṭalil variyum puḷḷiyum uṭiya ciṟupuli.
vāyip piḷakkutaṭi - kiyuṟi
vāḷum uruvutaṭi
eṉpār kavimaṇi. itu kaṭuvāyp puliyām.
ciṟupuli, ciṟutti eṉappaṭum.
kaṭuvāyp puli eṉṟāl, kaṭuvāy ākiya puli eṉṟākum. kaṭuvāy puli eṉṟāl, kaṭuvāyum puliyum eṉṟākum. kaṭuvāy puli kāṭṭil tiriyum kavaṉittup pō eṉpatu paṭṭaṟivāḷar uri.
kaṭi:

kaṇ kāl kaṭi	-naṉ. 302
kaṭi kaṇṇi:
kaṭi	=	taṉittaṉiyāy aminta vaṇika niliyam.
kaṇṇi	=	toṭarāka aminta kaṭivītiyum, cantiyum.
kaṭi = iṭam; iṭam eṉṉum poruḷtarum iccol vāyil eṉap poruḷ peṟṟatu. talikkaṭi, puṟakkaṭi allatu puḻikkaṭi eṉpavaṟṟik karutuka. kaṭiyil - vāyililē - vaṇikam ceytamiyāl ataṟkuk kaṭiyeṉap peyar vantatu.
kaṇṇi = iṇi; iṇiyākak toṭukkappaṭṭa allatu variciyāy aminta kaṭitteruvum cantiyum kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
kaṇṇiṇi pōl amikkappaṭum kaṇṇi eṉṉum pāvakiyiyum, pūkkaṇṇiyiyum valikkaṇṇiyiyum karicalāṅkaṇṇi, poṉṉāṅkaṇṇi mutaliyavum karutuka. 
kaṭikōṭi:
kaṭikōṭi = ākakkaṭici.
kaṭi eṉpatu kaṭici eṉṉum poruḷatu. kōṭi eṉpatu kaṭici eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar. atu teruk kōṭi teṟkuk kōṭi eṉa iṭattiṉ kaṭiciyik kuṟippatumāyiṟṟu. ivviraṇṭuñ cērntu ākakkaṭici eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatāka vaḻakkil ūṉṟiyuḷḷatu.
kaṭikōṭi, vīṭu, vayal eṉpaṉa vaḻakkukaḷ.
kaṭikkaṭṭil:
vāḻviṉ muṭivil kaṭiciyāy paṭukka vikkum kaṭṭil pāṭi ākum. pāṭiyik kaṭikkaṭṭil eṉpatu tirucceṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. pāṭikkaṭṭil kāl illātatāka irukkum. ātalāl ataṉik kāl kaḻi kaṭṭil eṉpatu caṅka nūl vaḻakku.
kaṭikkaṇ kāṭṭal:
kaṭikkaṇ kāṭṭal = kuṟippāl kaṭṭaḷiyiṭal.
kaṇṇaṭittal kātal talivaṉ pāṟpaṭṭateṉiṉ ikkaṭikkaṇ kāṭṭal kātal talivi pāṟpaṭṭatām. avaḷ avaṉiṉum aritiṟ pulappaṭak kāṭṭalāl aṭittal viṉiyiṉṟik kāṭṭal aḷavil niṉṟatām.
kaṇṇiṉ kaṭippārvi kātaliyar kāṭṭiviṭṭāl
maṇṇil kumararkku māmaliyum ōrkaṭukām
eṉpār pāvēntar. avvaḷavu eḷimiyāka maliyiyum puraṭṭa vittu viṭumām akkātaṟ kaṇkāṭṭal! inta vil eṉṉa, enta villiyum murikka muṭiyumām cītiyik kaṇṭa irāmaṉukku.
kuṟippaṟital eṉṉum ōratikārap peyar īriṭattu vaḷḷuvattil iyintamiyē itaṉ nuṇmiyik kāṭṭum.

kaṭinili:
paricil nīṭṭitta pōtu ataṉi veṟutta pulavaṉ, vēntaṉatu kaṭi kāvalariṭam muṉinturippatāka varum nūl kaṭinili eṉappaṭum. ikkaṭi niliyi vacippāṭṭu eṉṟum, aṅkatam eṉṟum kūṟuvar.

paricil nīṭṭikkap paruvaraluṟṟa pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ pala puṟanāṉūṟṟil iṭam peṟṟuḷa. kaṭinilikku muṉṉōṭi avi eṉka. (127,382,383,384,391,392,393,394,395,398)
kaṭinteṭuttal:
kaṭinteṭuttal = akaviyi mīṟiya aṟivu.
tayirik kaṭintu veṇṇey eṭuttaliliruntu vanta vaḻakkuccol itu. pāliṉ aḷavu kāya vikkum poḻutu cuṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ piriyiṭṭut tayirākkik kaṭintāl veṇṇey tiraḷum, attiraḷ ciṟitāyiṉum appāliṉ ūṭṭam aṉittum attiraḷil aṭaṅki viṭukiṟatu. atupōl ciṟiya akaviyil periya āḷukkuriya aṟivu, āṟṟal,ūṭṭam aṉittum attiraḷil aṭaṅki viṭukiṟatu.atupōl ciṟiya viṉāvutal iruppiṉ kaṭin teṭuttavaṉ avaṉ eṉpar. āṉāl, pārāṭṭutalāka aḵtu amiyāmal ikaḻtalāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉil akavikku viñciyatum poruntātatum ākiya aṟivuk kūrppē veḷippāṭāka iruttalāl.
ciṟantatāka iruntāl ciṟupperumi eṉṟum, ciṟumutuk kuṟivu eṉṟum collappaṭum.
kaṭimaṭi:
ēri, kuḷam, kālvāy nīr, viḷi nilaṅkaḷil pāyntu, kaṭiciyāka veḷiyēṟum maṭi, kaṭimaṭi yākum. viḷi nilaṅkaḷukku nīr mutaṟkaṇ varum maṭi talimaṭi.
talimaṭiyālē nīrpāyum kaṭimaṭiyālē viḻavum        								(te.i.kal.to.23:490)
kaṭiyam:
makaḷir kālaṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu kaṭiyam. kaṭical vēli mikkatu ātalāl peṟṟa peyar. kaṭakam kivaḷi, tōḷvaḷi āyavaṟṟik kuṟikkum āṭavaraṇi.
kaṭakakki puṭittu nakkāṉ 	- eṉpār kampar(ayōt. 413)
kaṭkam:
kaṭkam:1
kamukkam > kakkam > kaṭkam = maṟiviṭam; tōḷpaṭṭiyiṉ uṭpakuti.
kaṭkam:2
tōṭpaṭṭiyiṉ uṭpakutiyil vikkum vāḷ.
kaṭci:
kaṭṭappaṭṭatu; kūṭic cērntu palavaṟṟāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu kaṭci - kūṭu eṉṉum poruḷatu. paṟavi muṭṭi iṭuvataṟkāka āṇ paṟaviyum peṇpaṟaviyum kūṭik kaṭṭiyatu kaṭci eṉappaṭṭatu.
īṉil eṉṟa atu. uṟiviṭamum āyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ vilaṅkukaḷ uṟiyum kukiyāy āyatu. innāḷil kaṭci eṉpatu araciyal amippup peyarākiyatu. araciyalil āḷum. kaṭci, etirkkaṭci eṉa amintu karuttu mōtal ēṟpaṭuvatāl kaṭci kaṭṭutal eṉpatu kūṭi etirttal poruḷ tantatu. viḷiyāṭṭil kaṭci pirittal vaḻakkam.
eritta kaṭci, eriyāk kaṭci eṉac camayap pōrk kaṭcikaḷ mēṭi yēṟṟap paṭavum āyatu! kaṭci, kaṭci māṟi eṉa virivākkiviṭṭatu makkaḷ tērtal āṭci.
kaṭṭakkāl;
kuṭṭik kāl eṉpatu kaṭṭikkāl ākik kaṭṭakkāl eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu itu. kuṟuṅkāli yuṭiya paṉṟiyik kaṭṭakkāl eṉpatu tiruvillipputtūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. kaṭṭappiḷḷi neṭṭappiḷḷi eṉpatu nāṭṭuppuṟap pāṭṭu.
kaṭṭaṭam - kaṭṭiṭam:
kaṭṭu = aṭam = kaṭṭaṭam. itu kaṭṭu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiyum, aṭam eṉṉum iṟuti niliyiyum koṇṭatu. kaṭṭaṭam eṉpatu kaṭṭum toḻil vaḻiyāka vanta peyar. kaṭṭutal eṉpatu kaṟkaṭṭu, ceṅkaṟkaṭṭu, kaṭṭaṭa vēli muṭintatu; pūccut tāṉ naṭakka vēṇṭum! vīṭṭik kaṭṭippār; vīṭu kaṭṭi viḷiyāṭal ivi ellām kaṭṭaṭac colli viḷakkuvaṉa.
kaṭṭu = iṭam = kaṭṭiṭam. kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭutaṟkeṉat tērntu koṇṭa iṭam - maṉiyiṭam - kaṭṭiṭam. iṭam vāṅki viṭṭēṉ eṉpatu kaṭṭa iṭam vāṅki viṭṭēṉ eṉṉum poruḷatu. avviṭattil kaṭṭaṭam iṉimēltāṉ kaṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭatu.
kaṭṭiṭam, iṭappeyar; kaṭṭaṭam toḻiṟ peyar. muṉṉatil iṭam iṭappeyar; piṉṉatil aṭam toḻiṟpeyar.
kaṭṭaṭa vēli ceyyum kalviyaṟiyār eppaṭi eḻutiṉālum, poṟiyiyal kaṟṟa aṟiñarum iraṇṭum oṉṟāka eḻutalāmā? oṉṟeṉa eṇṇalāmā? oppanta viḷamparaṅkaḷ, ceytit tāḷkaḷ ivaṟṟilellām ippiḻi eṉṟāl, aṟiñarkaḷum ippiḻiyi viṭṭārkaḷā? iraṇṭum caritāṉ eṉṟu ettaṉi coṟkaḷic colvārkaḷō! ettaṉi kālam colvārkaḷō!
kaṭṭaṇam:
kaṭṭaṇam:1
kaṭṭu + aṇ + am = kaṭṭaṇam = kaṭṭa vēṇṭiya tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa toki. kaṭṭāyam pōlvatu añcaṟ kaṭṭaṇam, miṉkaṭṭaṇam, carakkuk kaṭṭaṇam, kalvik kaṭṭaṇam eṉpavi vaḻakkil uḷḷavi.
kaṭṭaṇam:2
kaṭṭaṇam eṉpatu pāṭi eṉṉum poruḷil nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. pāṭu eṉpatu paṭutal; kaṇṇimi mūṭutal; uṟaṅkutal; cettāri eṭuttuc cellum paṭukki amippu. kaṇpāṭu = uṟakkam pāṭivīṭu = paṭukki iṭam. kaṭṭikaḷi vittuk kaṭṭutal vaḻi vanta peyar itu.
kaṭṭam:
kaṭṭam:1
kaṭṭu + am = kaṭṭam.
kaṭṭuppāṭāka viḷiyāṭutaṟku aḷavu kōlākap pōṭappaṭṭatu kaṭṭam. enta viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷamum aḷavīṭu amintatu aṟika. kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭutaṟkum kaṭṭumāṉam ceytaṟkum oṉṟi otta aḷavil ceytaṟkum kaṭṭu vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. tāyakkaṭṭam, cūtu kaṭṭam, kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭumāṉam, kaṭṭaḷik kal, aṟak kaṭṭaḷi eṉpaṉavellām kaṭṭu vaḻiyē amintavi. kaṭṭu = varampu.
kaṭṭu vaḻiyē kaṭṭuri, kaṭṭāyam, kaṭṭāri eṉpaṉavum āyiṉa.
	kaṭṭu + uri		=	kaṭṭuri; kaṭṭaminta uri.
	kaṭṭu + āyam		= 	kaṭṭāyam; aḷaviṭṭa vari.
	kaṭṭu + āri		=	kaṭṭāri; aḷavāka veṭṭum karuvi.
	kaṭṭu + ārppu	= 	kaṭṭārppu > kaṭṭāppu. aḷaviṭṭuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa	 tōppu. kāṭu, nilam  mutaliyaṉa.
kaṭṭam:2
kaṭṭip pōṭṭa oṉṟaṉ iyakkam, illāmal oḻiyum. nōyt tuyar piṇi eṉappaṭutali eṇṇalām. piṇiyi oppatu kaṭṭu eṉpatu. kaṭṭip pōṭappaṭṭavar aṭiyum tuyar karutik kūṟap paṭṭa col kaṭṭam ākum. ataṉik kaṣṭam ākkiyatu moḻikkēṭām.
x.neh.:
iṭṭam > iṣṭam. iṭuvati virumpuvatu iṭṭam; viruppam.
kaṭṭaḷi:
ceṟittu aḷavāka amikkappaṭṭatu; aḷaviṭṭuk kāṇa amintatu varampiṭṭu amitta amippu; āṇi.
ceṅkal aṟuppataṟku aminta kaṭṭaḷic caṭṭam, ataṉuḷ ceṟikkum maṇceṟippu, piṟa toḻilakak kalloṭum otta aḷavīṭu eṉpavi karutuka. poṉmāṟṟu aṟiya aminta kal, kaṭṭaḷik kallām.
perumikkum ēṉic ciṟumikkum tattam
karumamē kaṭṭaḷik kal	-tiruk. 505
eṉpatu poṉmāṟṟaṟiyum kallām. iṉṉārāl ēṟpaṭuttap paṭum aṟam eṉpataṟkām aṟakkaṭṭaḷi innāḷil perukkamām. aṟavōr kaṭṭaḷi, ācāṉ kaṭṭaḷi eṉpavi āṇi eṉṉum poruḷatām.
kaṭṭaḷik kalippā:
caṅkac kāṉṟōrkku muṟpaṭṭōr kaṭṭaḷi aṭiyāl ceyyuḷ ceytali vaḻakkamākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. tolkāppiyar kūṟum aṭikaḷukkuriya eḻuttu pōla amintatu kaṭṭaḷi eṉṉum collāṭci.
kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟi eṉpatu nēraciyil toṭaṅkum aṭikkup patiṉāṟu eḻuttiyum niriyaciyil toṭaṅkum aṭikkup patiṉēḻu   eḻuttiyum koṇṭirukkum. kaṭṭaḷik kalippā ōraṭiyi iraṇṭu ariyākavum ariyaṭiyi nāṟcīr aḷaviṉatākavum, avvariyaṭi nēril toṭaṅkiṉ patiṉōreḻuttum, niriyil toṭaṅkiṉ paṉṉīreḻuttum peṟṟu varum. yāppāl peṟṟa peyar nūlukku āyiṟṟu.
kaṭṭaḷik kalippā nāṭṭuṅ kāli
orumāk kūviḷam oru mūṉṟiyiya
nērpati ṉoṉṟu niripaṉṉī reḻuttāy
naṭantaṭip pātiyāy nāṉkaṭi ottavāy
varuvatiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum neṟiyē	-toṉ. vi. 236
kaṭṭāṭi:
kaṭṭi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu tuṇikaḷi aṭittu veḷukkum calaviyarik kaṭṭāṭi eṉpatu yāḻppāṇa vaḻakku. kaṭṭu eṉpatu poti; potiyit tōḷil mutukil koṇṭu celvatum, kaḻutimēl ēṟṟic celvatum iṉṟum aritākak kāṇum kāṭcikaḷām.
niripparap poṟiya narippuṟak kaḻuti	-akam. 207
eṉpatu caṅkat toṭar.
kaṭṭāntari:
kaṭṭu + ām + tari = kaṭṭāntari.
keṭṭimikka - vaṉmiyāṉa - nilam kaṭṭāntari eṉpatām. uḻavukku ākāta nilam. kaṭṭutal = veṭṭutal. veṭṭip pakkuvap paṭutti viḷinila mākkat takka iṭam.
x.neh.:
kolli, ceṟu.
kaṭṭāppu:
kaṭṭu ārppu > kaṭṭāppu = nerukkamākak kaṭṭutal.
kōṭṭam, tōppu, payirnilam ākiyavaṟṟil āṭu māṭu pukāmal iṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭum vēli, allatu nerukkamāka aminta uyir vēli kaṭṭāppu eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭutal kaṇṭamaṉūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. potuvil teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakkamākum.
kaṭṭāyam:
kaṭṭu + āyam = kaṭṭāyam = vari celuttal pōṟ kaṇṭippu.
kaṭṭu = celuttu; āyam = vari. tavirkka muṭiyāmal ceytē āka vēṇṭiyatu kaṭṭāyamākum. ataṉic ceyyat tavaṟiṉāl taṇṭaṉiyil iruntu tappa muṭiyā nilimiyatu.
kaṭṭāṉ:
narampu nār ākiyavaṟṟāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu uṭal. ātalāl yākki(ākki) eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. yāttal, yāppu eṉpavi kaṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷatu. kaṭṭāṉa uṭal eṉpati viḷaṅkac ceyvatu elumpu ākum. elumpik kaṭṭāṉ eṉpatu maturi vaḻakku.
kaṭṭi:
kaṭṭi:1
maṇtiraṇṭu ceṟintatu. pakkuvam vāyā uḻavil nilam kaṭṭi kaṭṭiyākap peyarum kaṭṭi, tiraṇṭu ceṟintatu; valiyatu. maṇkaṭṭi, veḷḷikkaṭṭi, taṅkakkaṭṭi, paṉikkaṭṭi eṉa āyatu.
kaṭṭi:2
kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭam kaṭṭi āyatu. kalluk kaṭṭi
kaṭṭi:3
kaṭṭi eṉpāṉ valiya vīraṉ. kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi
kaṭṭi:4
uṭalil uṇṭākum tacittiraṭci kaṭṭikku maruntu kāvimaṇ. ataṟku maṇkaṭṭi > makkaṭṭi eṉpatu ma.va.
kaṭṭi:5
pāku, carukkarik kaṭṭi. kaṭṭi pūcik kaṭuttīṟṟal uriyāciriyarkaḷ. citta maruttuva iyal cārntatu.
kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟu:
kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟu = kaṟṟuk koṭutta kalvi.
kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟṟiṉ aḷavu mikumā? cuvitāṉ mikumā? tanta aḷavē aḷavāy amiyum. atu pōl kaṟṟuk koṭutta aḷavilēyē amiyum kalvi, kaṭṭik koṭut cōṟākac collappaṭum kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟum kaṟṟuk koṭutta kalviyum enta maṭṭō anta maṭṭō eṉṉum paḻamoḻi. ivvaḻakkut toṭariṉ poruḷi viḷakkum. kaṭṭik koṭutta cōṟu, kaṭṭuc cōṟu eṉappaṭum. tōḷil ataṉip pōṭṭuk koṇṭu pōṉa vaḻakkattāl tōṭkōppu eṉpatum atu.
kaṭṭik koḷḷal:
kaṭṭik koḷḷal = tirumaṇam ceytal.
tirumaṇam ceytalit tāli kaṭṭu eṉpatu vaḻakkam. tirumaṇa nikaḻvil kaṭṭāyam iṭam peṟuvatu tālikaṭṭu. tālikaṭṭu muṭintuviṭṭāl tirumaṇa viḻā muṭintatu eṉap pantiyil uṭkārum vaḻakkamē ataṉit terivikkum. vāḻttiṉāl eṉṉa? vāḻttu tirumaṇam āvatilli! tāli kaṭṭutalē tirumaṇamākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. maṟṟi maṟṟic caṭaṅkukaḷum kūṭa mutaṉmiyilli. ataṉāltāṉ, tirumaṇa viḻāvil tāli kaṭṭa maṟantatu pōla eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. tāli kaṭṭa maṟantāl tirumaṇamē naṭantatākātu eṉpatu teḷivu. ātalāl tāli kāṭṭal illāmalum, tāli kaṭṭal uṇṭu eṉpatē poruḷām. muṭiccup pōṭutal kaṭṭutaltāṉē. māli māṟṟik koḷḷal, viralāḻi māṟṟik koḷḷal, pativu ceytal eṉpavi putiya varavukaḷ.
kaṭṭippōṭutal:
kaṭṭip pōṭutal = aṭaṅkac ceytal.
kayiṟṟāl kaṭṭutaltāṉ kaṭṭutal eṉpatilli. collāl kaṭṭutalum kaṭṭē. kaṭṭaḷi, kaṭṭuri, kaṭṭuppāṭu, kaṭṭuppaṭuttal, kaṭṭumāṉam eṉpaṉavellām kayiṟṟoṭu toṭarpillāk kaṭṭukaḷē. cilar kotittu eḻum niliyilum oru collāl, oru viralacippāl, oru kaṇṇimippāl kotiyātu aṭaṅkiyirukkac ceyti viṭuvatu uṇṭu. avvāṟu aṭaṅkiyavar, eṉṉik kaṭṭip pōṭṭu viṭṭīrkaḷ; illāviṭṭāl eṉṉa naṭakkum - naṭantirukkum - eṉpatu eṉakkē teriyātu eṉpatu uṇṭu. pāratak katiyil tarumaṉ āṇikkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu niṉṟa vīmaṉ mutalāṉōr kotippaṭiya, kaṭṭuṇṭōm, poṟuttiruppōm, kālam māṟum eṉṟu tarumaṉ kūṟiyatu karutat takkatu.
kaṭṭi muṭṭi:
	kaṭṭi 	= 	kaṭṭippaṭṭa oṉṟu, kaṭṭi.
	muṭṭi 	= 	kaṭṭi uṭintu uṇṭāy tuṇṭu, muṭṭi.
karuppukkaṭṭi, vellakkaṭṭi, ceṅkaṟkaṭṭi, taṅkak kaṭṭi ivaṟṟil kaṭṭiyiṉ poruḷi aṟintu koḷka. iṉi maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷum, vaciyumāy amintatām.
ceṅkaṟkaṭṭi uṭintatic ceṅkalmuṭṭi eṉpatu vaḻakku.  uḻavaṭiyil kaṭṭi muṭṭitaṭṭutal oru pakutiyām. kūḻ, kaḷi kiṇṭum pōtu  kaṭṭi paṭāmalirukkak kavalippaṭuvar. atil periyakaṭṭi ciṟiya kaṭṭiiruntāl, eṉṉa kaṭṭiyum muṭṭiyumāka irukkiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku. itu kaṭṭāṇi muṭṭāṇi eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum.

kaṭṭiyam:
kaṭṭiyam:1
kaṭṭu + iyam = kaṭṭiyam.
tiṭṭamiṭṭuk kaṭṭiya collāl ōṅkik kural koṭuppatu - eccarippatu - kaṭṭiyam eṉappaṭum. kaṭṭiyaṅ kāraṉ - cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil varum kēṭaṉ.
kaṭṭiyam:2
aracaṉ tiruvōlakkattil vīṟṟirukkum pōtu, avvaracaṉ pukaḻi aviyiluḷḷār kēṭṭu makiḻumāṟu, kaṭṭiyaṅ kūṟuvōṉ kūṟuvatu pōl amikkap peṟum nūl kaṭṭiyam eṉappeṟum.
eṭuttuk koṇṭa pāṭal aṭiyiṉ cīr eṇṇikkiyi aḷavākak koṇṭu periya kaṭṭiyam ciṟiya kaṭṭiyam eṉa iru vakiyākak kūṟap peṟum. citampara aṭikaḷārāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa tiruppōrūr murukar periya kaṭṭiyamum, tiruppōrūr murukar ciṉṉa kaṭṭiyamum ivaṟṟukkuc cāṉṟām.
ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu eḻucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttattāṉum, piṉṉatu veṇcentuṟiyāṉum amin tuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭṭil:
kaṭṭu + il = kaṭṭil. il = iṭam.
kuḻantikaḷi uṟakkāṭṭat toṅkalākat tuṇiyāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu kaṭṭil eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉṉark kuḻanti paṭukka marattāl ceyyappaṭṭa nāṟkāl palakip paṭukkiyum kaṭṭil peyarpeṟṟatu. ataṉ piṉṉarp potumip paṭukkip peyarāyiṟṟu. toṭṭil kaṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkam karutuka.
kaṭṭu kāval:
	kaṭṭu 	= 	kōṭṭi, akaḻ, cuvar mutaliya araṇ kaṭṭākum.
	kāval	=	kāvalar, nāy ākiya kaṇkāṇippu kāval ākum.
kaṭṭumāṉam amintatu kaṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. paḻaṅkālak kōṭṭikaḷik kavaṉittāl kaṭṭu kāval amiti pulappaṭum. akaḻ, araṇ, kōṭṭi eṉpavi iruppiṉum avaṟṟiyum takarttō kaṭantō vantu aḻivu ceyyum pakivar iruppar. avarit taṭuttu niṟuttavum kāvalum vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu kōṭṭi vāyilil kāvalar iruppum, matilmēl kāvalar mēṭiyumiruttalik kaṇṭu teḷika. ataṉ eccam ikkāla vaḷamaṉi, aluvalakaṅkaḷ, karuvūlaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil iruttali aṟika.
kaṭṭukkaṇi:
iyaṟkiyāṉa muṭi illātavar ceyaṟkiyāka muṭi ceytu kaṭṭutal uṇṭu. acceyaṟki muṭiyik kaṭṭukkaṇi eṉpatu koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakku. kaṭṭukkaṇi = oṭṭu muṭi. kaṇi = kaṇṇi. toṭukkappaṭum kayiṟu, māli ākiyavi kaṇṇi eṉappaṭum.
kaṭṭu cīli:
kuḷicīli eṉpatum tāyccīli eṉpatum kōvaṇap poruḷaṉa. cīli (cīri)yil iruntu(kiḻinta cīliyil iruntu) kiḻittu eṭukkappaṭṭa tuṇiyik kōvaṇamākak koṇṭatāl uṇṭākiya peyarkaḷ ivi. kōvaṇattik kaṭṭucīli eṉpatu viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum.
kaṭṭuttari:
kaṭṭu + tari = kaṭṭuttari.
māṭu kaṭṭum toḻuvam kaṭṭuttari eṉpatām. cuṟṟuccuvar amintu, kaṭṭum muḷi,tīṉipōṭum kāṭi, nīr kuṭikkattakka toṭṭi āyavaṟṟik koṇṭatu kaṭṭuttari. kaṭṭut toḻuvam eṉpatum itu.
kaṭṭuppaṭutal:
kaṭṭuppaṭutal = kaṭṭaḷikku uṭpaṭutal.
peṟṟavarkaḷ periyavarkaḷ eṉpatāl avarkaḷ colluvatu maṉattiṟku ovvavilli eṉṉum oruvāṟu ēṟṟuk koṇṭu naṭappatuṇṭu. ataṟkukkaṭṭuppaṭutal eṉpatu peyar. naṉṟiyaṟital kāraṇamākavum ceytāri niṉintu kaṭṭuppaṭṭu naṭattalum uṇṭu, ūrkkūṭṭattil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭāl avarkaḷ collukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭēyāka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nilimi aṇmik kālam vari iruntatu. itil varum kaṭṭu āṇi eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatām. ‘C®¡f£L Ûwyhkh? eṉpatu vaḻakku moḻi. talivaṉ coṟpaṭi niṟkum kuṭumpa āṭciyit talikkaṭṭu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
kaṭṭuppāṭu:
kaṭṭu + pāṭu = kaṭṭuppāṭu.
kaṭṭuppaṭṭāka vēṇṭiya caṭṭa vitimuṟi; āṇi moḻi. aluvalakattil kaṭṭuppāṭē illi. vittavar varicitāṉ, talimikkuk kaṭṭuppāṭu illi! piṟaku evar kaṭṭuppāṭṭik koḷvār? eṉpavi makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaṭṭumaṭṭu:
	kaṭṭu 	=	varuvāykkut takkavāṟu kaṭṭuppaṭuttic celaviṭal.
	maṭṭu	=	evvaḷavutāṉ varuvāy vantālum tiṭṭappaṭutti itaṟku ivvaḷavē eṉṟu maṭṭuppaṭutti(elli allatu aḷavu paṭutti)c celaviṭal.
kaṭṭumaṭṭu uṭiyaṉ, kaṭṭumaṭṭāṉavaṉ eṉap potuvākak kūṟuvatum uṇṭu. iṉi itaṉik kaṭṭu meṭṭu eṉpatum uṇṭu.
kaṭṭumāṉam:
kaṭṭumāṉam:1
kaṭṭu + māṉam = kaṭṭumāṉam. māṉam = aḷavu kaṭṭa vēṇṭiya aḷavu
kaṭṭumāṉa vēli, kaṭṭumāṉa vili.
kaṭṭumāṉam:2
iṭṭukkaṭṭi - puṉintu - urittal. uṉ kaṭṭumāṉam tāṉ ituvā? eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaṭṭumāṉam:3
kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭutaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ.
kaṭṭumāṉam:4
vāṅkum - viṟkum - vili. kaṭṭumāṉam ākātu; tara vāykkātu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaṭṭuri:
kaṭṭuri eṉpatu ōr arumiyāṉa col; atu mikap poruṭ ceṟivuṭiya paḻañcollākum. cilappatikārattil kaṭṭuri, uripeṟu kaṭṭuri, kaṭṭuri kāti eṉṉum nūliṉ uṭpirivukaḷ amintu uḷḷaṉa. kaṭṭuri eṉṉum collum mikutiyāka eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟkup paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ poruḷ potinta col uṟutiyuṭiya col eṉap poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
kaṭṭumāṉam: nām oru vīṭu kaṭṭat tiṭṭamiṭṭāl mutaṟkaṇ kaṭṭumāṉap paṭam varivōm.ataṉpiṉ kaṇṭavāṟu kaṭṭaṭam eḻuppuvōm  .atu pōlavē kaṭṭuriyum tiṭṭamiṭṭu variyaṟippaṭuttik koṇṭu eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭum. ap poḻututāṉ ataṉ amippum poruḷum teḷivum ciṟappāka amiyum.
kaṭṭaḻaku: cilar uṭalip pārttatum kaṭṭāṉa uṭal eṉkiṟōm. V‹? aḷavukku viñci melivu illāmalō, ūtip pōkāmalō uriya aḷavil poruttamāka aminta nalla uṭaliyē kaṭṭuṭal eṉṟum, attakiyavariyē kaṭṭaḻakaṉ kaṭṭaḻaki eṉṟuma kūṟukiṟōm. ataṟku ēṟpavē kaṭṭuriyum aḷavālum amippālum kaṭṭaḻaku uṭiyatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭu virital:moṭṭu allatu mukiyāka irukkum pūvil maṇamum tēṉum uṇṭu; eṉiṉum, avi avvaḷavāka veḷippaṭṭup paravuvatu illi; āṉāl atu kaṭṭu virintuviṭṭāl eṅkēyō tiriyum vaṇṭum aṅkē vantu moykkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. antat tēṉilum naṟu maṇattilum taṉṉi maṟantu veḷippaṭuttik kaṟpavari vayappaṭuttuvatē kaṭṭuriyiṉ peṟṟikku aṭiyāḷamākum.
kaṭṭuccōṟu: kaṭṭuc cōṟum kaṟṟa vittiyum eṉpatu oru paḻamoḻi. kaṭṭuccōṟṟip poti cōṟu eṉpar. poti cōṟu tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟāl potintu vikkappaṭṭa cōṟāka maṭṭumillāmal cuvi potintum, keṭṭup pōkāta taṉmi potintum aminta cōṟākum. atu pōlak kaṭṭuriyum nalla naṭiyāl potiyap peṟṟu uyarnta poruḷ potintatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭuuri: kaṭṭuri eṉṉum colli iraṇṭākap pirittāl kaṭṭu uri eṉa iraṇṭu ēvalkaḷ uṇṭākum. av ēvali ēṟṟut tiṭṭamiṭṭuk kaṭṭi urikkum, uriyē kaṭṭuriyākat tikaḻum.
iṭṭuk kaṭṭutal: kaṭṭuri eṉpatu iṭṭuk kaṭṭic collum uri eṉavum poruḷ taruvatāyiṟṟu. poyyuri, puṉinturi ākiyaviyum kaṭṭuri eṉavē collappaṭum. eṉiṉum aviyum kaṟpavariyum kēṭpavariyum vayappaṭuttuvaṉavākavē amintāltāṉ nili peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati maṟakkak kūṭātu.
kaṭṭamippu: kaṭṭuri, ciṟappu aṭivataṟku uvami, paḻamoḻi, marapuccol ākiyavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttutal vēṇṭum. nām kūṟum karutti valiyuṟuttat takka mēṟkōḷkaḷit takka iṭattil ceyyuḷākavum iṭutal vēṇṭum. kiyeḻuttu aḻakō kaṟpavarkkuk kāṭci iṉpam taruvatāy muttuk kōttāl pōl viḷaṅka vēṇṭum
kaṭṭu tiṭṭam: kaṭṭuriyip pārttavuṭaṉēyē iṉṉa poruḷ paṟṟi ikkaṭṭuri eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpati aṟintu koḷḷum vakiyil muṉṉuriyum, ikkaṭṭuriyil aminta poruḷ muṭivu ituvē eṉpati aṟintu koḷḷum vakiyil muṭivuriyum koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iṭiyē kūṟappaṭum ceytikaḷum ēṟattāḻa otta aḷavil pattip pirivuṭaṉ viḷaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
muṭivuri: ivaṟṟip pōṟṟi eḻutum pulamiyi māṇavarkaḷ vaḷarttuk koḷvatu. tērvil matippeṇ perukuvataṟku vāyppāvatuṭaṉ piṉṉāḷilum matippu mikka eḻuttāḷar āvataṟkum vaki ceyyum eṉpatu uṟuti.
kaṭṭūr:
kaṭṭūr = pācaṟi.
kaḷappōrkkuc cellum vīrar taṅkuvataṟkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa pācaṟi vīṭukaḷ cila palavaṟṟik koṇṭa ūr, kaṭṭūr ākum. pōr karutiyē kaṭṭappaṭṭa kuṟukiyakkālak kuṭiyiruppu ātalāl appeyar peṟṟatu. appaṭippaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ nilitta vīṭukaḷāy amintu pāḷiyam eṉṟum pāḷiyappaṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkalāyatum uṇṭu pāḷiyam iṟaṅkal eṉpatu paṭikaḷ taṅkalām.
kaṭṭi:
marattuṇṭam, kuṭṭi eṉpavaṟṟik kuṟikkum kaṭṭi, potuvaḻakkup poruḷatu. āṉāl atu tuṟavar vaḻakkil uṭalik kuṟittu vaḻaṅkiyatu. intak kaṭṭikku iṉi eṉṉa vēṇṭum? intak kaṭṭi colkiṟatu eṉpar. itu potumakkaḷ vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu. uṭalvēkuvatik kaṭṭi vēkiṟatu eṉpatum atu. iṉik  kaṭṭiyilē pōvāṉ eṉṉum vaci moḻiyil uḷḷa kaṭṭi iṭukāṭṭil kaṭṭiyi erikka utavum viṟakuk kaṭṭiyik kuṟippatām.
kaṭṭik kālaṉ:
kaṭṭi > kuṭṭi = uyarak kuṟivu. kaṭṭikkālaṉ eṉpatu kāl uyaram kuṟintavaṉik kuṟittal potu vaḻakku. neṭṭik kālaṉukku māṟu. āṉāl, kaṭṭikkālaṉ eṉpataṟkup paṉṟi eṉṉum poruḷ mukavi māvaṭṭa vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. itaṉik kuṟuntāḷ ēṟṟi(perumpāṇ. 344) eṉpatu pulamakkaḷ vaḻakkākum.
kaṭṭippakuti:
kaṭṭippakuti = kuṟukkutteru.
neṭunteruviṉ ūṭu aminta kuṟunteru tamiḻakattil kuṟukkut teru eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. neṭiya marattik kuṟukkē taṟippatu kaṭṭi ākum. kaṭṭi > kuṭṭi.   nīḷakkuṟivuṭiya teruvik kaṭṭip pakuti eṉpatu īḻa vaḻakku.
kaṭṭipparutti:
kaṭṭi + parutti = kaṭṭipparutti,
uyaramāka vaḷarāta nāṭṭup paruttiyik kaṭṭip parutti eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. ciṉṉa parutti eṉṟum  kūṟuvar. uyarak kuṟivi muṉṉatu kāṭṭum, piṉṉatu, ciṉṉa iḻikaḷi yuṭiyatum ciṉṉa aḷavuṭiyatum kāṭṭum.
amerikkaṉ parutti vantapiṉ peritum kaṭṭip parutti payiriṭal aṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. ataṉip periya parutti eṉpar.
kaṇakku:
mēṟkaṇakku, kīḻkkaṇakku eṉpavi nūl eṉṉum poruḷit tarutal aṟintatu. nūlāyntu kaṟpikka vallār  kaṇakkāyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār  eṉpatu tamiḻaṟintatu.
kaṇakku eṉpatu paṟṟiya ilakkaṇattip paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal pakarkiṉṟatu.
mēlkīḻk kaṇakkeṉa iruvakik kaṇakkē	-paṉṉiru. 344
mēṟkaṇak keṉavum kīḻkkaṇak keṉavum
pāṟpaṭum vakiyāl pakarntaṉar koḷalē	-paṉṉiru. 345
akavalum kalippā vumpari pāṭalum
patiṟṟan tāti patiṟṟimpa tīṟā
mikuttuṭaṉ tokuppaṉa mēṟkaṇak keṉavum
veḷḷit tokiyum avvaki eṇpeṟiṉ
eḷḷaṟu kīḻkkaṇak keṉavu koḷalē	-paṉṉiru. 346
aimpatu mutulā ainnū ṟīṟā
aivakip pāvum poruṇeṟi marapiṟ
ṟokukkap paṭuvatu mēṟkaṇak kākum	-paṉṉiru. 347
aṭinimir pillāc ceyyuṭ ṭokuti
aṟamporuḷ iṉpam aṭukki avvat
tiṟampaṭa urippatu kīḻkkaṇak kākum	-paṉṉiru. 348
* kīḻkkaṇakku, mēṟkaṇakku kāṇka.
kaṇakku vaḻakku:
	kaṇakku	= 	kaṇakkiṭṭut tantatu.
	vaḻakku	=	ceymuṟikaḷāl tantatu.
eṉakkum avaṉukkum kaṇakku vaḻakku oṉṟum illi eṉa naṭappil kūṟuvatum, kaṇakku vaḻakkip pārttuk koṭuttu viṭu eṉap pakiyil kūṟuvatum kēṭkak cuṭiyavi.
vaḻakku = vaḻakkam, naṭimuṟi. moy, naṉkoṭi, aṉpaḷippu eṉat tantaviyum vaḻakkil vantuviṭum. tiṉṟatik kakku eṉpatu ciṟupiḷḷittaṉam eṉṟāl periyavarkaḷilum attakiyar uṇṭu eṉpataṉ cāṉṟu vaḻakku.
kaṇantuḷ:
āṭkāṭṭip paṟavi eṉpatu kaṇantuḷ paṟavikku makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum peyar. vaḻic celvārkku vaḻippaṟippāḷar maṟintiruppatik kāṭṭum paṟavi eṉpar.
neṭuṅkāṟ kaṇantuḷ āḷaṟi vuṟīi
āṟucel vampalar paṭitali peyarkkum	-kuṟun. 350
poruḷ: neṭiya kāli yuṭiya kaṇantuṭ paṟavi āṟali kaḷvari aṟivuṟutti vaḻippōkiṉṟavarkaḷiṉ paṭittiraḷi nīṅkac ceyyum.
āḷ aṟivuṟīi eṉpatu viḷaṅka arumiyākap paṭikkap paṭṭa peyar āṭkāṭṭip paṟavi eṉpatām. vaḻikāṭṭi, kikāṭṭi eṉa ūrp peyarkaḷ uḷavē. itaṟkuk kaṇantuḷ eṉa ēṉ peyariṭṭaṉar?
āṟṟayal irunta iruntōṭṭu añciṟi
eṉkiṟatu kuṟuttoki (350). perutta tokutiyaminta iṟakukaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya ciṟakiyuṭiyatu. kaṇantuḷ  tiraṇṭa kūntalik kaṇaṅkuḻi eṉkiṟatu tirukkuṟaḷ. ataṉi eṇṇiṉāl kaṇantuḷ eṉṉum peyariṉ poruḷ viḷakkamām. periya tokutiyākiya aḻakiya ciṟaku, iruntōṭu eṉappaṭṭatu (tōṭu = tokuti). itaṉi uṭimiyāl kaṇantuḷ āyatu.
aṇaṅkukol āymayil kollō kaṇaṅkuḻi
mātarkol mālumeṉ neñcu	-tiruk. 1081
kaṇam:
kaṇam:1
kaṇ + am = kaṇam = kaṇṇimip poḻutu; noṭip poḻutu.
uṉṉal kālē ūṉṟal ariyē
muṟukkal mukkāl viṭuttal oṉṟē
eḻuttiṉ oli aḷavu kaṇattāl aḷaviṭappaṭum.
kuṇameṉṉum kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟār vekuḷi
kaṇamēyum kāttal aritu	-tiruk. 29
kaṇam:2
kaṇam = kūṭṭam, tokuti.
* kaṇaṉ kāṇka.


kaṇaṉi:
kamyūṭṭar eṉṉum collik kaṇiṉi eṉat tamiḻakam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. īḻam ataṉik kaṇaṉi eṉkiṉṟatu. kaṇitam, kaṇakku eṉa iruvaki vaḻakkum uḷḷami eṇṇip pārkkalām. kaṇi, kaṇiyaṉ, kaṇippu eṉpavi kaṇi vaḻippaṭṭa coṟkaḷ.
kaṇakku, kaṇakkaṉ, kaṇakkāyar eṉpavi kaṇakku vaḻip paṭṭavi. kaṇ eṉpatu ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam eṉpatu aṟiviyal vallāṉ oruvaṉ mutukaṇṇaṉ eṉap paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil vaḻaṅkappaṭutal koṇṭu aṟiyalām. kaṇikaṇṇaṉ, kaṇivaṉ mulli eṉṉum peyarkaḷiyum eṇṇalām. kaṇṇakaṉ, kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum paḻiyaviyē.
kaṇaṉ:
kaṇ + aṉ = kaṇaṉ. kaṇam = kūṭṭam tokuti.
paṟaviyiṉ kaṇaṅkaḷ	-nālā. 282
kaṇam > kaṇaṉ.
kaṇaṉ aṭaṅkak kaṟṟārum il	-ciṟupañ. 29
kaṇaṉ aṭaṅka = nūṟparappaṉittum.
kaṇicam:
aḷantu koṭukkāmal kaṇṇāl aḷaviṭṭut taruvatik kaṇicam eṉpar. itaṟku ēṉ aḷantu koṇṭu; oru kaṇicamākak koṭuṅkaḷ eṉpar. piṉṉark kiyāl ōr aḷavākat tarutal kikkaṇicam eṉappaṭṭatu. kaṇṇāl aḷantu tarum aḷavu kaṇicam ākum. itu nelli vaḻakku.
kaṇi muṟṟūṭṭu:
kaṇiyarkku iṟiyiliyāka (vari illāmal) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa nilam.
paḷḷiccanta kaṇi muṟṟūṭṭu 	(ka. ka. co.a)
kaṇiyam:
kaṇiyam:1
kaṇ > kaṇi > kaṇiyam.
matiṟkataviṉ uḷḷāka iṭappaṭṭa kāppumaram. tiraṇṭa valiya maram kaṇiyam ākum. maṇveṭṭi, kōṭari āyavaṟṟukku iṭappaṭum kaṇiyiṉi aṟiyiṉ vayiramēṟiya vaṉmiyi uṇaralām.
x.neh.:
kaṇiyāvatu kippiṭiyāy aminta kōl.
	kaṇi 	= 	kaliyatu; kūriyatu (ampu) karumpiṉ kaṇi - valiyatu; muḷiyiṭuvatu; kaṭikka iyalātatu.
kaṇiyam:2
uṭal uḷḷuṟuppukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kallīral maṇṇīral kaṇiyanīr.
kaṇ:
kāṇa vāytta uṟuppu kaṇ; puṟattē kāṇum kāṭcikaḷi akattē patittu aṟivip perukkalāl akakkaṇ, aṟivukkaṇ eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. kāṭci eṉpataṟku aṟivup poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum
eṉṟum,
eṇṇeṉpa ēṉi eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum
kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻum uyirkku	-tiruk. 392
eṉṟum, eṇ eḻuttu ikaḻēleṉṟum muntiyar moḻikaḷ kaṇṇiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭuvaṉavām.
eṇcāṇ uṭampukkut taliyē talimiyateṉiṉum, uyariya iyakkamām mūḷi aṅkē iruppateṉiṉum, ceviyum mūkkum vāyum attaliyilēyē iruppavi eṉiṉum kaṇṇukkuriya ciṟappup peritām.
cevik kuṟiyiṉum kaṇkuṟiyē, evariyum eḷitil parivuṟac ceyvatāka iruppatu ataṉ arumiyālēyē ām. kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭum, nērnēr neruṅki oppavaminta amippāl kaṇṇiṇi eṉappaṭṭaṉa. makkaḷik kaṇmaṇikaḷ eṉṟaṉar. kaṇṇiṉ ciṟappāl kaṇṇē, kaṇṇā, kaṇṇu eṉa viḷippeyar aṉpuruvāka veḷippaṭalāyiṟṟu, iṇinta īraṭippāṭal kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭatu.
iyaṟkik koṭiyāka vāyttavi ilikaḷ. ivi nērukku nērāka īrkkil allatu taṇṭil iruntatāl kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu:
poṉṉāṅkaṇṇi, karicilāṅkaṇṇi.
kaṇ iṇi pōlak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kayiṟṟu vali, kaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭatu. māṉ kaṇ pōl aminta kālatar - pala kaṇkaḷiyuṭimiyāl - palakaṇiyām. māṉkaṇ kālatar māḷiki iṭaṅkaḷum (cilap. 5:8).
māṉkaṇ, yāṉikkaṇ, pulikkaṇ pōlāka aminta maṭivāy uṭiya nīrnili kaṇvāy ākum; aṇikkaṭṭil iruntu pirivatu, kaṇṇāṟu. maliyiṭi vaḻippiḷavu, irupāl malikaḷukkum iṭippaṭṭatākaliṉ kaṇvāy > kaṇavāy āyatu. kipar kaṇavāy, pōlaṉ kaṇavāy, pālakkāṭṭuk kaṇavāy, kaṇavāyppaṭṭi.
kaṇṇimikkum poḻutu kaṇam; atu oru māttiri aḷavu. kūṭṭamāy amintatum kaṇam eṉappaṭum. e-ṭu: māṉkaṇam, pulikkaṇam.ceṟivaminta pul kaṇampul ! kaṇampullar eṉpār nāyaṉāruḷ oruvar. kaṇṇāl piṟarik kavarpavaḷ kaṇiki

kaṇṇeḻuttup paṭutta eṇṇup palapoti	-cilap. 26:16
kaṇ > kaṇakku. kaṇakkāyar eṉa nūliyum nūlāy vāḷariyum kuṟikkum
mēṟkaṇakku; kīḻkkaṇakku; kaṇakkāyar makaṉār nakkīraṉār.
vēliyiyō vēli ceyvāriyō kaṇkāṇippavar kaṇkāṇi. kaṅkāṇi eṉpatu kocci allatu vaḻu vaḻakku. kaṇ ēṟu paṭumeṉa vikkappaṭum poymmi (pommi) iṉṟum viḷinilam, putumaṉikaḷil kāṇalām.
kaṇavarik kaṇṇāḷar eṉpatu aṉpiṉ uvakip perukkum uvami yamippumām. kaṇṇiṉ maṇipōl makkaḷik karutuvatu peṟṟōr perunili.
kaṇṇē maṇiyē tālāṭṭu
kaṇṇaminta vaṭicaṭṭi kaṇcaṭṭi; kalam vaṉivār ceyvatu. ataṉic, cippilittaṭṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ciṟpilit taṭṭu = ciṟitu ciṟitākap piliṟṟum - vaḻiya viṭum- taṭṭu arikkaṇcaṭṭi eṉpatum atu. arikkum kaṇṇamitta caṭṭi.

kaṇṇōṭal = orupakkam cārtal.
muṟi eṉappaṭuvatu, kaṇṇōṭātu uyirvuval	-kalit.133
paṟivakikaḷuḷ orupakkam aṭippatu, irupakkam aṭippatu eṉa iruvaki uṇṭu. avi orukaṭpaṟi, irukaṭpaṟi eṉpaṉa. kākattiṟkuk kaṇ iraṇṭēṉum kaṇmaṇi oṉṟē. oru  kaṇṇukku  orukāṭci.
kaṇpōl matittal kaṇṇiyam. kaṇṇāl aḷaviṭutal matippiṭutal kaṇittal kaṇakkiṭṭut tulliyamāka evaṟṟiyum kāṭṭum karuvi kaṇiṉi.
kaṇṇaḻaku mikkār kaṇṇaṉiyar. kaṇṇaṉ vaḻip peyarum kaṇṇi vaḻippeyarum perukiṉa kaṇṇaṉ, mutukaṇṇaṉ, kaṇikaṇṇaṉ iṉṉavi āṇpāṟ peyarkaḷāyiṉ kaṇṇammi, kaṇṇāyi, aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi, kuvaḷik kaṇṇi, tāmarikkaṇṇi - iṉṉavi peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ āyiṉa.
kaṇṇukku miyiṭuvār uṇṭu, aṇikalam iṭuvār illi! mūkku kātu, kaḻuttu, ki, kāl, iṭuppu āyavaṟṟil eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikaḷi aṇivārip paṇṭumutal iṉṟuvari kaṇṭu varukiṉṟōm. āṉāl kaṇṇukku aṇi uṇṭā? ām uṇṭu!
maṟṟi uṟuppukaḷiṉ aṇikalaṅkaḷil aḻaku enta aḷavu uṇṭō, anta aḷavu avalamum uṇṭu! āṉāl, avalamē illāta aṇi oṉṟaṉṟu palavaṟṟik koṇṭatu kaṇ! ataṉik kāṭṭiya perumi vaḷḷuvarkku uṇṭu!
kaṇṇukku aṇikalam kaṇṇōṭṭam
eṉṟatu (575) maṭṭumā? aḵtiṉṟēl puṇ eṉkiṟārē vaḷḷuvar.
kaṇṇōṭṭamē pēraḻaku; kaṇṇōṭṭattiltāṉ ulaka iyakkamē uḷḷatu eṉṟellām viyantu viyantu urikkiṟārē! puṉkaṇīr aṉpuccāṉṟu eṉṟārē avar. !
civaṉiya ulakam muḻu mutalvaṉi mukkaṇṇaṉākak kaṇṭatu! avaṉ kaṇṇi, neṟṟik kaṇ eṉṟatu.
peṇmiyip paḻikka allatu niliyāmiyic cuṭṭa vantārum vēṟkaṇṇaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇi mayaṅkātē! ivaḷ kōṟkaṇṇaḷ āvāḷ eṉpati maṟavātē eṉṟaṉar! āṉāl, caṅkac cāṉṟōr kaṇṇakaṉār, kāviyak kaṇṇakiyār, tirut toṇṭar kaṇṇappar, meyyiyal kaṇṇuṭiya vaḷḷalār, pāratiyāriṉ kaṇṇaṉ, kaṇṇammāḷ, tirip paṭappukaḻ kaṇṇatācaṉār eṉa ettuṇip pērkaḷ cāvā vāḻviṉarāy - neñcil vāḻvārāy- irukkiṉṟaṉarē. ivarkaḷ, nili yāmiyi veṉṟa nilipēṟṟāḷar eṉpataṉ cāṉṟāḷarkaḷ allarō!
kaṇ eṉpataṉit toṭakkamākak koṇṭa, vaḻaṅku coṟkaḷtām ettaṉi! ettaṉi!
‘kaṇ’ eṉpataṉi īṟākak koṇṭa, vaḻaṅku coṟkaḷ tām ettaṉi !ettaṉi!
kaṇ mutaṟ coṟkaḷ cila.

kaṇvittal
kuṟippu:
	1.	kaṇ eṉpatu ataṉ valliṉat tiripāki kaṭ eṉa iṇiyum iṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. kaṇṇiṉ muṉ ka, ca, ta, pa ākiya valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyāka avi irukkum. kaṇ cevi > kaṭcevi.
	2.  kaṇṭapaṭi, kaṇṭa maṭṭum, kaṇṭavāṟu, kaṇṭatum kaḻiyatum, kaṇṭavar viṇṭilar eṉpavi makkaḷ vaḻakkil peruka vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
kaṇ īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ cila.
akakkaṇ	akaṅkaṇ	akaṉkaṇ
aṅkaṇ	amarkaṇ	amarkkaṇ
amikaṇ	arikkaṇ	aruṭkaṇ
alakkaṇ	aḻukaṇ	āṅkaṇ
iṅkaṇ	iṭakkaṇ	iṭukkaṇ
iḷaṅkaṇ	iṉkaṇ	īṅkaṇ
īrṅkaṇ	uṭkaṇ	uṇkaṇ
uḷakkaṇ	ūṅkaṇ	eṇkaṇ
ēṇkaṇ	oṇkaṇ	oḷirkaṇ
ōrakkaṇ	ōrmikkaṇ	kaṭikkaṇ
kayaṟkaṇ	karuṅkaṇ	karuviḷaṅkaṇ
kalikkaṇ	kuvaḷikkaṇ	kuṟuṅkaṇ
kūrṅkaṇ	ciṟukaṇ	ceṅkaṇ

pukarkkaṇ	pulikkaṇ	puṟakkaṇ
puṉkaṇ	pūṅkaṇ	pūṉikkaṇ
peruṅkaṇ	poykkaṇ	poṟkaṇ
mayilkaṇ	maṟakkaṇ	māṟukaṇ
māṉkaṇ	meykkaṇ	meṉkaṇ
