mūlikkal
karuṅkal, ceṅkal eṉa vaṇṇam koṇṭum.
vaṭṭakkal ,caturakkal eṉa vaṭivu koṇṭum,
mākkal, parukkikkal eṉat taṉmi koṇṭu varuvaṉa 	pala.
kaṟkālam, kaṟpōtu, kaṟṟāḻi eṉa lakara oṟṟu, ṟakara oṟṟākavum,
kaṉṉāṭu, kaṉmali,kaṉmā eṉa lakara oṟṟu, ṉakara oṟṟākavum varuvaṉavum pala uḷa.
kaltaccaṉ:
kallāl tirukkōyil kaṭṭiya ciṟpi kaltaccaṉ eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ talivaṉ peruntaccaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉar.
taccu vēli, kalli iṇittuk kaṭṭum kaṭṭakkali. ka.ka.co.a
kalnāṭu:
kal + nāṭu = kalnāṭu. naṭu > nāṭu.
pōriṭṭu iṟantōrkku uriya naṭukal naṭṭu vippataṟ keṉṟu ūriṉ oru pakkattil otukkappaṭṭatum pārkkat takkatākavum aminta iṭam kalnāṭu eṉappaṭum.
takaṭūril paṭṭār nācanti ciṟukuṭṭiyār kalnāṭu ka.ka.co.a.
kalpuri:
kalpuri = kalmāṭam.
kōyil karuvaṟiyiṉ uṭpakkam taḷiki vippataṟku uriyatāka amikkap peṟum karuṅkaṟ palaki, kalpuri eṉappaṭum.
kalpuṟi(ri)kkup puṭavi iṇi 	-te. ka. to. 14: 16
puri uyarvākum	-tol. 785
kalmaḻi:
maḻinīr miku kuḷircciyāl kallākip poḻivati, ālaṅ kaṭṭi maḻi eṉpar. ālam eṉpatu nīr. paṉikkaṭṭi eṉpatu pōla vaḻaṅkuvatu ālaṅkaṭṭi ataṉik kalmaḻi eṉpatu maturi vaḻakkākum.
kalmuki:
muki, malar niliyuḷ oṉṟu. atu tiraḷiṉ mokkuḷ eṉappaṭum. 
mukimokkuḷ eṉpatu vaḷḷuvam (1274). mokku malarākum pōtu veṭippuṟum. avvāṟu kal ākiya maliyum veṭippuṟal uṇṭu ataṉi muntiyar kalmuki eṉṟaṉar.
kalmuki vēṅki malarum	-aiṅ. 276
kallakam:
kal + akam = kallakam.
kalparappuṭiya nilappakutiyum, appakutiyil uḷḷa ūrum kallakam ākum. kal āvatu tiṇṇiyatu valiyatu! keṭṭiyām taṉmiyatu kallakam . maṇpaṭalam arukiyatāy kaṟparappu mikkatāyk kuṟuṅkālap puṉceyp payirukku uriyatāka irukkum.
tiṉi, kēḻvaraku, kutirivāli, varaku, cāmi, koḷ, eḷ, kaṭali mutaliyavi viḷiyum nilam, kallakam. ippeyariya ūr tirucci- maturi cāliyil tuvaraṅ kuṟiccip pakutiyil uṇṭu.
kallakkāram:
akkāram = iṉippu. akkāra aṭicil eṉpatu kaṟkaṇṭuc cōṟu kallakkāram eṉap paṉaṅ kaṟkaṇṭi vaḻaṅkutal yāḻppāṇa vaḻakkākum.
kallaṭār:
kal + aṭār = kallaṭār. aṭu + ār = aṭār.
kal = mali, mali muḻi(kuki); aṭār = aṭuttup paṟṟum poṟiyām. mūṭu kalpalaki. oru vilaṅkip paṟṟip piṭikka malivāṇar kaṇṭa poṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu kallaṭār. kuki veḷiyi aḷaviṭṭu ataṉi mūṭum vakiyāl kalpalaki amittu, ataṉic caṟṟē veḷippuṟam cāya niṟutti uḷḷāka vilaṅku virumpiyuṇṇum poruḷit tūṇṭil puḻuppōl toṅkaviṭṭu vippatu aṭārām. mūṭupalakiyiṉ uḷḷē pukuntu tīṉiyip paṟṟi iḻukkum pōtu aṭippup palaki uḷcāyntu mūṭik koḷḷum. uḷḷē pukunta vilaṅku veḷippaṭāmal māṭṭik koḷḷum. piṉ ataṉip paṟṟik koḷvar. elippoṟi, pulikkūṇṭu āyavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉōṭi kallaṭārām.
tiṉiyik kavara varum paṉṟiyip paṟṟuvataṟkākap puṉavarāl vikkappaṭṭatu. piṉṉē vēṭṭiyarāl puliyip paṟṟa appoṟi ākkappaṭṭatu.
tiṉiyuṇ kēḻal iriyap puṉavaṉ
ciṟupoṟi māṭṭiya peruṅkal aṭāar	-naṟ. 119
irumpuli vēṭṭuvaṉ poṟiyaṟintu māṭṭi
peruṅkal aṭāar	-puṟam. 19
puḻitoṟu māṭṭiya iruṅkal arumpoṟi
eṉṉum malipaṭukaṭāttāl(194) cuṭṭappaṭuvatu aṭāar poṟiyēyām. aṭuttu varac ceytu paṟṟutaṟkaminta poṟi. aṭu + ār= aṭār. ārttal = kaṭṭutal, paṟṟutal.
kallampalam:
kal + ampalam = kallampalam.
kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatum ūravi kūṭuvataṟkāka amintatum kallampalam ākum. ivvūr kallampalattu cantirātittaval āṟṟut taṇṇīr aṭṭuvatāka kuṭutta ṉilam te.ka.to. 8:607
ūravi kūṭutaṟkuriya potiyil eṉpatu ūrppotu viṭattaminta maram; piṉṉark kallampalam ēṟpaṭṭamic cāṉṟu itu.
kallaḷi:
kal + aḷi = kallaḷi.
kallāvatu mali; atiluḷḷa iyaṟki muḻi(kuki) kallaḷi eṉappaṭṭatu.
aḷḷal eṉpatu cēṟu. atil cēṟṟu naṇṭu cēṟṟik kiḷḷi ataṟkuriya aḷiyi(vaḷiyi) amikkum. avvaḷip peyar, kallil aminta muḻikkum ākiyatē kallaḷi eṉpatām.
pulavu nāṟu kallaḷi	-kuṟun. 253
pulicērntu pōkiya kallaḷi	-puṟam. 86
kallaṟi:
kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa aṟi kallaṟi ām. āṉāl kallaṟi eṉpatu iṟantāri aṭakkam ceyvataṟkuk kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa aṟiyē kallaṟi eṉak kiṟittava camaya vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. kallaṟit tōṭṭam eṉpatu accamayap potu iṭukāṭu ām. peṭṭiyil vittu aṭakkam ceytu ataṉik kallaṟiyuḷ vittu ataṉi mūṭikkaṭṭiya kallaṟi mēl peyar mutalām maṟṟavi poṟittuc camayak kuṟiyoṭu tōṉṟac ceyyum vaḻakkiṉatu. piramiṭu eṉṉum perum pārikik kallaṟi paḻaṅkāla māmaṉṉar niṉivuc ciṉṉa aṭakka niliyaṅkaḷām.
kallāmēṭu;
kal + ām + mēṭu = kallāmmēṭu > kallāmēṭu.
kaṟpāṟiyum ataṉ mēl maṇ mēṭumāy aminta nilamum ūrum kallāmēṭu ākum. kallakam eṉpataṉiṉum mēṭāṉatu. taṇṇīrt taṭṭuppāṭu uṭiyatu. maḻi peytālum uḷvāṅkutalō niṟṟalō illāmal uṭaṉē vaḻintu ōṭuvatu. uriya vakiyil kariyiṭṭut taṭuttāl taṇṇīr kiṇaṟu vaḻiyākavē nīrpeṟa muṭiyum. kallutal = tuḷittal tuḷittaṟkum ariyakal mēṭu eṉṉum poruḷum tarum allavā!
kallāṟu:
kalli uruṭṭik koṇṭu varum āṟu kallāṟu. kuṟumaṇal atil kāṇal aritu. piṟavaki āṟukaḷ varumāṟu:
	aiyāṟu		=	aintāṟukaḷ kūṭi varum āṟām 						kāviriyēaiyāṟu.
	karumpāṟu		=	iṉcuvi nīr uṭiya āṟu.
	kāriyāṟu		= 	karumaṇ vaḻivanta āṟu.
 	kāviriyāṟu 	=	kākkaḷ ūṭu ōṭum peṟṟiyāl kāviri; 		icippāṭalil kāvēri.
	kuṭakaṉāṟu	=	kuṭakil iruntu varum āṟu.
	kumariyāṟu	= 	kumari nilattuk kumari maliyil iruntu vantatu			 kumariyāṟu
	kūṭṭāṟu		=	īrāṟu kūṭuvatu kūṭutuṟiyām kūṭṭāṟu.
	kūvakavāṟu	=	kiṇaṟaṉṉa cuṉi toṭaṅki vanta āṟu.
	teṟkāṟu		=	ticiyāl peṟṟa peyarāl itu. vaṭakkāṟu				 eṉpatiyum eṇṇuka. tamiḻaka nila amippāl			 mēṇi kīḻaṇi uṇṭēyaṉṟi mēlāṟu, kīḻāṟu			 illi.
	paḵṟuḷiyāṟu	=	kalavittup palavāṟukaḷiṉ kūṭṭāṟu.
	pāmpāṟu		= 	neḷintu vaḷintu cellum āṟu.
	pūvāṉiyāṟu	= 	pūppōlum tivali teṟikka ōṭum āṟu
	(pavāṉi)		
	periyāṟu				=	periya āṟu; pērāṟu. (vaṇṭip periyāṟu).
	poruni āṟu	 = 	ali koḻikkap porupaṭi pōl					celvatum kaṭaloṭu kalappa tumām āṟu.					 poruntam eṉpatum, āṉ poruntam					 eṉpaṉavum atu. poruni yiṉ tāmparac				 ciṟappu tāmpiravaraṇi āyatu.
	poṟiyāṟu 	= 	amintu cellum camanilattāṟu.
	poṉṉi 				= 	poṉpōlum nelvaḷam perukkum āṟu.
	mulliyāṟu	= 	mullik kāṭṭiṉ ūṭaṟuttuvarum āṟu.
	mūṇāṟu 				= 	mūṉṟāṟu kūṭiya āṟu.
	viyi āṟu 	= 	viyam (ōṭam) vittuk kaṭakkum āṟu.
kuṟippu:
vaciṭṭā nati mutalākap peyarum, nati oṭṭum vantāl appeyar poymip puṉiveṉṟu muṭivu ceyka.
* āṟukaḷ kāṇka.
kalli vayakkiṉa nilam:
karaṭu muraṭākavum kaḷḷi muḷḷiyākavum irunta nilattit tōṇṭiyum camappaṭuttiyum viḷinilamākac ceytatu kalli vayakkiṉa nilam vayal. nilamākkul, vayakkutal eṉappaṭṭatām.
nāṭṭāṇmi maṅkalattut tiṭal kalli vayakkiṉa nilam iraṇṭumā (te.ka.to. 8. 556)
kallumuṟi:
kaṭṭuppaṭuvataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka ūrkkūṭṭattār muṉvikkappaṭum kal, kallumuṟi eṉappaṭum. itu tirupparaṅ kuṉṟa vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. muṟi eṉpatu eḻuttu ōli. kallē eḻuttu muṟiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭa kalveṭṭi nōkkalām. muṟiyil eḻutappaṭṭatē kallilum cempilum veṭṭappaṭṭa paḻa varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟum ākalām.

kallum karaṭum:
	kal 	=	vaḷamaṟṟa pāṟi allatu kuṉṟu.
	karaṭu 	= 	kallum maṇṇum kalanta tiraṭu.
vaḷamāṉatu mali; vaḷamaṟṟatu karaṭu eṉka. karaṭu muraṭu eṉpatil karaṭu paṟṟiya kuṟippik kāṇka.
kal eṉpatu karaṭu eṉpataṉ iṇiccollāka vantamiyāl taṉik kallik kuṟikkāmal nīrppaciyaṟṟu vaṟaṇṭa kallāñ caraḷi eṉappaṭum nilappakutiyik kuṟikkum eṉka. kallum karaṭumāya iṭam pāli eṉappaṭum. mulliyum kuṟiñciyum muṟimiyil tirintu nalliyal paḻintu naṭuṅku tuyar uṟuttup pāli eṉpatōr paṭivam koḷḷum eṉpatu cilampu (11: 64-66)
kallum karaṭumāya vaḻi kallatar attam (cilap. 16:57) eṉa āḷappaṭum.
kallum karampiyum:
	kal 	= 	ciṉṉañ ciṟiya kaltukaḷ allatu maṇal
	karampi 	= 	ciṉṉañ ciṟiya karumaṇ kaṭṭi.
ariciyilō, paruppilō kallum karampiyum kiṭakkiṟatu. poṟukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu vaḻakku.
karical maṇṇum, ataṉ ciṟiya kaṭṭiyum karampi eṉppaṭum. karuniṟa maṇṇātalāl karampi eṉappaṭṭatu. karaṭu, karaṭi eṉṉum peyarkaḷi niṉika.
nel, payaṟu aṭikkum kaḷaṅkaḷil kiṭanta kallum karampiyum avaṟṟuṭaṉ cērntu vantu viṭuvatu uṇṭu. āṉāl, ippoḻutu kalantu viṟpatē kaliyāki viṭṭatu.
kallum karital:
kallum karital = irakkamillāṉum iraṅkal.
karippār karittāl kallum kariyum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. kalliyum kariya vikka muṭiyum eṉpati atu kāṭṭum . āṉāl, ikkal karital, kalpōṉṟa uḷḷam karintu iraṅki utavutalām. cilar kiyi aṟuttuk koṇṭālum toṭṭut taṭavac cuṇṇāmpum tarār eṉap pēr peṟṟiruppar. attakiyarum, cila vēḷikaḷil ētō utavak kaṇṭāl, antak kallumā karikiṟatu antak kallukkuḷḷumā īram irukkiṟatu eṉpar iraṅkātavar iraṅkutal eṉpatu poruḷām.
kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭal:
aracaṉāl aṟattiṟkeṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa nilattiṉ elliyākak kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭappaṭṭami kuṟikkum toṭar itu.
nāṭṭōmukkut tirumukam vara etireḻuntu ceṉṟu toḻutu vāṅkit talimēl vittup piṭicūḻntu piṭāki naṭantu elli terittu kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭi aṟavōli ceyta nilam. periya āṉimaṅkalam ceppēṭu (ka.ka.co,a)
kallūri:
kallūri malkiya kālam itu. iccolli vaḻaṅkiya perumiyar tiruttakka tēvar (cintā 995) kalvi nāṉku cuvarkaḷukkuḷ aṭaṅkiviṭutal ākātu. āṅkiruntu ūrntu ūrukkum nāṭṭukkum ulakukkum āka vēṇṭum. kaṟṟavar aṉṟik kallārum kēṭṭaṟiyum celvarāyt tikaḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatāl kallūri eṉappaṭṭatām. kal eṉṉum ēvalē kalvi cuṭṭiya arumiyatu. kallār neñcil nillāṉ īcaṉ eṉṉum toṭaril varum etirmaṟi mutaṉili kalvi cuṭṭal aṟika.
kalleṭuttal:
iṟantārkkuc cuṭukāṭṭil ceyyum mūṉṟām nāḷ caṭaṅku itu.  erintupaṭṭu eñciya elumpi eṭuttu aṭukki nīrāṭṭip putittu ataṉmēl niṉivuk kuṟiyākak kalnāṭṭuvatu kalleṭuppākum.  naṭukal mūlam ituvām.
kavacam:
kavacam = meymaṟi, taliccirāy, kittōl.
kavavu > kavacu + am =kavacam.
cuṟṟi vaḷittup paṟṟip piṭittal kavacamām.
kavavu akattiṭumē	-tol. 840
kavavukki nekiḻāmal	-cilap. 1: 61
o.nō.: 
viravu > viracu.
paravu > paracu.
kavaṭu:
kava > kavaṭu. kava > kavi = iraṭṭi.
kavaṭu = iṭuppil iruntu iraṇṭākap piriyum toṭikaḷ. kavaṭṭukkuḷ pāytal eṉpatu iru kālkaḷukkum iṭiyē virintu cellutal. atupōl iruvar ceyalkaḷukku iṭiyē oruvar pukuntu avar viruppam pōl ceytal kavaṭu pāytalām. orē orumiyām poḻutil, vañcamām iraṭṭi niliyil oruvar ceyalpaṭutal kavaṭu eṉappaṭum. avvāṟu ceyalpaṭuvār kavaṭar āvar. iraṭṭi yākap piriyum kavaṇ. kavaṭṭi eṉṉum kalleṟi kippoṟi uṇṭu. matilmēl ōr uṟuppu, tolivil varuvārik kuṟivittu aṭippatu kallumiḻ kavaṇum eṉpatu cilampu (15:208).
kavaṭṭi:
māṭṭut tarakar. kuḻumoḻiyākak kavaṭṭi eṉpar. itu iraṇṭu urupā eṉpatik kuṟikkum. kavittali = iraṭṭittali. kavimakaṉ eṉpār oru caṅkap pulavar. kavimakaṉ eṉṟu avartam pāṭalil kūṟiyatāl peṟṟa peyar atu (kuṟun. 324).
kavaṇi:
tōl tuṇṭik kavaṇi eṉpatu nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. kavaṇukkup payaṉpaṭum tuṇṭuttōl, piṉṉar maṟṟit tōl tuṇṭiyum kuṟippatākalām.
kavarimā:
erutu pōṉṟatoru vilaṅku. aṭar mayirkkaṟṟi yuṭiyatu. imaya maliyām paṉimalip pakutiyil vāḻvatu. imayattu vāḻum ataṉip puṟamum (132), patiṟṟuppattum pukalkiṉṟaṉa. (11, 21, 23).
kavari > cavari = caṭi.
mayirnīppiṉ vāḻāk kavarimā aṉṉār eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (969). nīttal eṉpatu muṟṟilum nīṅkal. Ú¤jh® bgUikí« (âU¡.), nīttal viṇṇappamum (tiruvā.) kāṇka.
kavar:
oru vāykkālil iruntu pirintu cellum kiḷi vāykkālik kavar eṉpatu uḻavar vaḻakkam. oru paṉi, mēlē iraṇṭākap pirintu cellutalāl kavarpaṉi eṉṉum pērum ūrum perampalūr vaṭṭārattil uṇṭu. kavarttal, pirital.
kavali:
kavali:1
orē vēḷiyil uṇṭākum iruvakic ceyaltavippu. itic ceyvatā atic ceyvatā eṉṉum iṭarnili (ma.va.)
kavali:2
palavākap piriyum vaḻi.
kavali maṟuku	- mali. 482
kavali:3
palavākap piriyum kiḻaṅku. e-ṭu: kavalik kiḻaṅku.
kavāṭam:
kavavu + vāṭam = kavavāṭam > kavāṭam (kapāṭam (vaṭa))
kavavutal = poruntutal. kavāṭam = niliyip porunti yamintatum iraṇṭākip porunti yamintatum ākiya katavu.
kavāṭam tiṟamiṉō	- kaliṅ. kaṭitiṟappu
o.nō.:
taḷam + vāṭam = taḷavāṭam.



kavi:
kavi eṉpatu kuṉi, kaviḻ eṉṉum poruḷ tarum ēvaṟcol. atu tali kaviḻntu allatu kavintu irukkum kuraṅkukkup peyarc collāyum  amiyum.
kaḷavu ceytu piṭipaṭṭavaṉ ūrmaṉṟil niṟuttappaṭṭa pōtil talikaviḻntu niṉṟu kālāl nilaṅkiḷittalip paṇṭē yaṉṟi iṉṟum kāṇalām.
kaḷavuk kātalaṉ taliviyi nōkkiṉāṉ; avaḷ nilam nōkkiṉāḷ. avaḷ kaṇṇukku oppākēṉ eṉṟu kuvaḷi nāṇik kaviḻntatu.
kāṇiṟ kuvaḷi kaviḻntu nilaṉōkkum
māṇiḻi kaṇṇovvēm eṉṟu	
eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ (1114).
kalaṅkaviḻtalum kalayaṅ kaviḻtalum nām aṟintavi. aracukaḷ kaviḻtali itaḻkaḷ vaḻiyē evarum aṟivōm.
pūṉikkāli eṉṉum ceṭikkuk kavi eṉpatoru peyar. ataṉ pū kaviḻntiruppatāl peṟṟa peyar. kaviḻ tumpi eṉṉum ceṭip peyarum kaviyum pūvāl peṟṟatē.
kaṇṇēril etir veḷiccam tākkukiṟatu. ataṉik kaṇṭu otuṅka vēṇṭum. iyalpāka eṉṉa ceykiṟōm! neṟṟip puruvattiṉ mēl kiyik kaviḻttuk kaṇṇi iṭukkip pārkkiṟōm. ippārviyic caṅkappāṭal kavikaṇ nōkku eṉkiṟatu (puṟam.3).
mukil ceṟintu maḻi poḻiyum niliyil tāḻvati vāṉam kaviḻntatākak kūṟutal vaḻakku mēkaṅ kavikiṉṟatu maḻi uṭaṉē varum eṉpatik karutuka.
kavital eṉpatu kavikam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. kutiriyiṉ vāyic cuṟṟik kavintuḷḷa kaṭivāḷattiṟkuk kavikam eṉpatu peyar.
kuṭivaminta poruḷ kuṭi eṉappaṭum. kuṭital toḻil paḻamiyāṉatu. kuṭivari kōyilkaḷ ataṟkuc cāṉṟu. innāḷil toṭari (Trin) celvataṟkut takkavāṟu kuṭivukaḷ malikaḷil uṇṭākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kōlār taṅka vayal vaḷam kuṭivu vaḷamē. cuṟṟic cuṟṟi nīril nīntiyāṭutal kuṭital eṉappaṭum. kuṭivu eṉpatum kavivu eṉpatum oru poruḷatātal aṟika.
kuṭital vaḷivu vaṭṭam ākiya poruḷkaḷit tarutalāl, naṭuvuyarntu cūḻavum tāḻntu vaḷintuḷḷa kuṭikkuk kaviki eṉpatu peyarāyiṟṟu.
cevikippac coṟpoṟukkum paṇpuṭi vēntaṉ
kavikikkīḻ taṅkum ulaku
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (389). ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉ araṇmaṉiyil kuṭipiṭikkum ēvalarum pāvalarākat tikaḻntaṉarām. ataṉāl, kaviki ēntiyavarum kaviki yēntiṉar eṉṟoru vaḻakku moḻi eḻuntatu.
kavittal eṉpatu vaṭṭa vaṭivap poruḷiṉ vāyppuṟam talikīḻāka vittalākum. muṭi cūṭṭutalum kavittalēyām. atu muṭikavittal eṉappaṭum. iruki viralkaḷiyum mūṭi vittali irukiyum kavintamākki eṉṉum kantapurāṇam (kāviri.40) kavittam eṉpatu kūttiṉ ki vakiyuḷ oṉṟumām.
kavippu eṉpatum taliyil kavikkum muṭiyik kuṟikkum. kuṭiyik kuṟittalum uṇṭu. vaṇikar peṟṟa ciṟappup paṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ kavippar eṉpatu oṉṟu. aracarāl oruvaki muṭi kavikkappaṭṭa ciṟappāl peṟṟa peyar atuvākum.
koṭukkum ki kavital kaṇkūṭu. atu vāṉam kavintu poḻivatu pōlvatu. ātalāl koṭik kiyi vāṉam vaḻaṅkutalōṭu oppiṭṭuk kāriṉi veṉṟa kavikiyāṉ eṉkiṟatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māli (9:29)
kavicci allatu kaviḻcci eṉpatu cuṟṟic cuṟṟi allatu cuḻaṉṟu cuḻaṉṟu ōriṭattu allatu oru poruḷil aṭikkum nāṟṟattik kuṟikkum. itaṉik kavicciyaṭittal eṉap pala vaṭivukaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
ivaṟṟi nōkkak kavi eṉṉum colliṉ vaṭivum poruḷum teḷivām. itu vaḷarntu pala collāy virinta niliyum viḷaṅkum. ākavē, kuraṅkik kuṟikkum kavi eṉṉum col tamiḻccollē eṉpatu veḷippaṭiyām.
iṉik kaviti eṉpatu emmoḻic col eṉiṉ kavi eṉpataṉ vaḻiyē kiḷarnta coṟkaḷ kālantōṟum vaḷarntu perukiya vaḷarcciyil piṟkālattōrāl amittuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kavi, kaviti, kaviñar eṉpavi eṉka. kavi, kaviti oppu nōkkuka. paḻu, paḻuti.
ceyyuḷ, pāṭṭu, pā, yāppu, tūkku, toṭarpu, paṉuval iṉṉavi paḻiya āṭciyuṭiyavi. attaku paḻamiyāṭci yiṉṟi ēṟattāḻa āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiya tamiḻvaḻip puttākkac col kaviti mutaliyaṉavām. kaṟpār uḷḷattik koḷḷi koṇṭu kaviyac ceyyum collum poruḷum vāyntatu kaviti.
teṉṉuṇ tēṉiṉ ceñcoṟ kaviyiṉpam eṉṟār kampar. kavippā amutam eṉpār pāvēntar. ivi kavittu - kavarntu - iṉpañ ceytalik kuṟippavi.
kaviṉ:
kavvip piṭikkat takka aḻakuṭiyatu kavi, kaviṉ. uḷḷattik kavittu īrttu niṟutta vallatākaliṉ kaviṉ eṉap peṟṟatām. ki puṉintiyaṟṟāk kaviṉ peṟu vaṉappu eṉpatu nakkīrar vākku (tirumuru. 17)
kavir:
aṭimaram oṉṟu; atu iraṇṭākap pirikiṟatu, ataṉik kavi eṉpar. atil pirivatu kompu eṉappaṭum. koppu eṉpatum atu.
kaviyākik kompākik kāṭṭakattē niṟkum
aviyalla nalla maraṅkaḷ
eṉpatu auviyār pāṭal.
kavireṉpatumiraṇṭākap pirivatēyām. muḷ muruṅkip pū. iraṇṭākap pirintu vaḷintu oṉṟākat talikkūṭum. paṟṟuk kuṟaṭu pōl atu tōṉṟum. aḻakiya civappu niṟamākap pū irukkum.
kavir itaḻ aṉṉa kāṇpiṉ cevvāy
eṉṉum akappāṭal (4) makaḷir civanta vāy kavir itaḻpōla iruppatik kūṟukiṟatu.

kāviyaṅ kaṇṇum mūṭā
kaviritaḻ tāmum kōṭā
eṉpatu ariccantira purāṇam.
kavaṇ, kavaṭṭi, kavaṭu, kavir, kavi, kavittali, kavali mutaliya ellām kavar eṉṉum aṭiccol vaḻippaṭṭavi yām.
kavul:
iccol vañcam eṉṉum poruḷil teṉkāci vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. yāṉi taṉakkut tīmi ceyta varip paḻivāṅkuvataṟkuk kaṉṉattuḷ kalli vittuk koṇṭirukkum eṉṉum nampikki vaḻi ipporuḷ uṇṭākiyirukkalām. kavuḷ = kaṉṉam.
kavuḷi:
kippiṭi aḷavām veṟṟiliyik kavuḷi eṉpatu veṟṟilik koṭikkālkārar vaḻakkam. kiyāl paṟṟip piṭikkum aḷavu kavuḷiyām. kavavukki eṉpatu cilappatikāram (1:61).
kavittali:
irutali kavittali eṉavum peṟum. tali iraṇṭum uṭal oṉṟum aminta makavu kavi makavu eṉappeṟum. kavi makaviṉi uvamippaṭuttiya pulavar perumakaṉār kavimakaṉār eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟār (kuṟun. 324).
kavimaka nañcuṇ ṭāaṅ
kañcuval perumeṉ neñcat tāṉē
eṉpatu avar vākku.
iḷañciṟār kal ēvi viḷiyāṭutaṟkup payaṉpaṭuttuṅ kavaṇ, kavaṇi, kavaṭṭi eṉṉum peyaruṭiya karuviyi aṟika! oru marakkompu iraṇṭākap pirivatik kavaṭṭi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatiyum, iru toṭip poruttuvāyik kavaṭu eṉpatiyum karutuka. uḷḷoṉṟu vittup puṟamoṉṟu colli iruneṟip paṭac cellum taṉmi, kavaṭu eṉṟu vaḻakkil uṇmiyik kaṇṭu teḷika. pāmpiṉ piḷavupaṭṭa nā, kavinā eṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkil uṇmiyum koḷka!

kavvāṅkal:
kavaṭṭi eṉṉum kavaṇiyil vittu aṭikkum kallik kavvāṅkal eṉpatu nelli vaḻakkākum. kavvak kalantu eṉpār vaḷḷalār (kaṭitam). iraṇṭaṟak kalattal atu. kal kavaṭṭiyil iṟukkamāka  iruppatu kuṟikkum itu villi iṟukkip piṭikkum ki (villaka viral) eṉṉum caṅkat toṭari niṉivūṭṭum. pulavar villaka viraliṉār (kuṟun. 370). kavaṭṭi eṉpatu irutalik kaṭṭi . kavi = iraṇṭu. kavaṭṭiyik kavaṇṭi eṉpatu pērāvūraṇi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. kavuṭṭi eṉpatu kōvi vaḻakku.
kavvi:
kavvi:1
kavvu + ai + kavvi, kavval = kaṭittal, paṟṟutal.
aravō mutaliyō kavvik koṇṭāṟ pōṉṟa kaṭuntuyaram.
vēntar vēntaṉ kavviyoḻin tuyarntaṉaṉ	- kam.pāla. 245
keḷavi > kavvi.
kāyeri yuṟṟaṉar aṉiya keḷaviyar-	kam. ayō. 478.
kavvi:2
keḷavi > kavvi = alar.
kavviyāl kavvitu kāmam atuviṉṟēl
tavveṉṉum taṉmi iḻantu	- tiruk. 1144
* keḷavi kāṇka.
kaḻakam:
kaḻaṅku eṉpatōr āṭaṟpeyar. kaḻaṅkāṭal eṉpatu atu. ataṟkup payaṉpaṭum kāy kaḻaṟcikkāy ākum. kikkaṇ eṭuttu mēlē ēṟaviṭṭuk kīḻē varuṅkāl piṭittu mīṇṭummēlēṟa viṭutal kaḻaṅku viḷiyāṭutal ākum. ataṉāl akkaḻaṅkāṭiya iṭam kaḻakam eṉappaṭṭatu. kaḻala viṭappaṭṭa paralkaḷiyuṭiya kālaṇi kaḻal eṉappaṭṭami aṟika.
kaḻaṅkāṭṭam pōṭṭiyāṭṭamāy amintatu. ataṉ vaḷarcciyāy amintatu cūtāṭṭam. ātalāl accūtar kūṭiya iṭamum avarkaḷ kūṭṭamum cūtar kaḻakam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
paḻakiya celvamum paṇpum keṭukkum
kaḻakattuk kāli pukiṉ
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (937).
piṉṉē kampar kālattil iḷiyar payilum kaḷarip payiṟcik kaḻakamākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avar, kantaṉi aṉiyavar kaliteri kaḻakam eṉṟār (pāla. nāṭṭup. 48). cūtar kaḻakam kaḷarik kaḻakamāy vaḷarntu mēlē aṟiñar āyvuk kaḻakamāy viḷaṅkiyatāl,
kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅkaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇuṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ
eṉṟār parañcōtiyār (tiruviḷi. pāyi.)
colliṉ poruḷ nili kālantōṟum māṟutal aṭitalum ammāṟutal perumip poruḷum ciṟumip poruḷum tarutalum vaḻakkamām. innāḷil kaḻakam patippakamāy, kaṭciyāy, araciyalāy virintami aṟivaṉavē.
kaḻal:
kaḻal = āṭavar kālaṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. paralkaḷ kalakala eṉa olikkak kālil kaḻalaviṭappaṭṭa aṇi ākaliṉ kaḻal eṉappaṭṭatām. vīrakkaḻal eṉpatum atu.
tāḷ, kaḷaṅkoḷak kaḻal paṟintaṉa	- puṟam. 4
villōṉ kālaṉa kaḻalē	- kuṟun. 7
kālatu puṉikaḻal eṉpatu vīrattiṟkum veṉṟikkum kaṭṭiṉa; pōrtoṟum veṉṟu kaṭṭiṉa eṉṟumām (puṟam. 100, pa.u.).
kaḻaṟṟi viṭutal:
kaḻaṟṟi viṭutal = pirittal.
oru kaṭṭil iruntō, piṇippil iruntō pirittal kaḻaṟṟal eṉappaṭum. aṇikalaṉkaḷit tirukuvāy, pūṭṭuvāy ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu pirittalum kaḻaṟṟutalē. ittakiyaṉa parupporuḷām kaḻaṟṟutal.
oru nikaḻcciyil iruntō, kūṭṭattil iruntō, cikkalil iruntō uṟavu, naṭpu ākiyavaṟṟil iruntō tammip pirittuk koḷḷutalum kaḻaṟṟutalāka vaḻakkil ūṉṟiyatu. avaṉ muḻutākat taṉṉi nammiṭamiruntu kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭu viṭṭāṉ. eṉpatu peruvaḻakku.
kaḻaṉi:
kaḻal > kaḻaṉ > kaḻaṉi = maṇṇiṉ iyaṟki, ceṟivu.
maṇtiṇinta nilaṉ eṉappaṭum (puṟam. 2). āṉāl kaḻaṉi, nīrālum paṇpaṭuttalālum ilitaḻiyālum ceṟivu kuṟintu kālvittāl cēṟum cetumpum ākkappaṭutalāl kaḻaṉi eṉappaṭṭatu. kaḻaṉi nelnaṭavukku uriya nilamākum. marutam meṉṉilam eṉappaṭum. ataṉiṉum meṉṉilam kaḻaṉiyām. āṉāl kaḷar, aḷaṟu, uḷimaṇ, uvarmaṇ allāta maṇ kaḻaṉi ākum.
kāl vittāl patiyat takkatāy maṇ vilaki akalvatāy uḷḷatē kaḻaṉi eṉka. kaḻaṉi āyiram viḷiyuṭṭāka, kāvum kaḻaṉiyum eṉpavi pulamiyar vaḻakkākum.
kaḻaṉṟatu:
kaḻaṉṟatu = payaṉaṟṟatu, toṭarpaṟṟatu.
poruttuvāy kaḻaṉṟuviṭṭāl akkaruvi payaṉpaṭutal illi. kaḻaṉṟa akappi eṉac cilaric colvatuṇṭu. tēṅkāy ōṭum, kipiṭik kāmpum uṭiyatu mara akappi. ataṉ kāmpu kaḻaṉṟuviṭṭāl tēṅkāy ōṭṭi vittup payaṉkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. kāmpi vittum payaṉkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. muṉṉatu oḻukippōm; piṉṉatu aḷḷa vārātu. itaṉik karuttil koṇṭu kaḻaṉṟa akappi eṉṟāl payaṉiṉmip poruḷ vaḻakkil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. uṉakku mari kaḻaṉṟu viṭṭatā eṉṟāl ‘mūḷik kōḷāṟā? eṉpatu poruḷām. iṅkuk kaḻaṟṟutal eṉpatu toṭarpiṉmip poruḷatu.

kaḻi:
kaḻi eṉpatu mikutip poruḷ tarum. ātalāl,
cāla uṟutava naṉikūr kaḻimikal
eṉṟār naṉṉūlār (431).
kaṟpak kaḻimaṭam aḵkum	- nāṉmaṇi. 27
kallātāṉ oṭpam kaḻiyanaṉ ṟāyiṉum
koḷḷār aṟivuṭi yār
eṉṟatu kuṟaḷ (404).
iṅkuk kaḻiya eṉpatu mikutip poruḷē tantatu.
kaḻipē ririyāṉ eṉṟu kuṟaḷē (946) colvatāl kaḻi, kaḻiya, kaḻivu eṉpaṉa oppaṉa eṉavām.
kaḻimutukkuṟimi eṉkiṟatu kuṟuntoki (217), mutukku uṟital eṉpatē pēraṟivām. ataṉiṉum pēraṟivik kaḻimutukkuṟimi eṉkiṟatu. kaḻineṭilaṭi eṉpatum aṉṉatē.
uḷḷatu eṉṟatu muṉ ciṟavātuḷḷatu eṉṟavāṟu eṉṉum cēṉāvariyar uriyi mīḷa nōkka vikkiṟatu kaḻimutukkuṟivu. ēṉeṉil, muṉṉarē mutukkuṟivuṭiyatu aḵtātaliṉ. ātalāl iyalpāka uḷḷatu mēlum ciṟakkum eṉak koḷḷal takavām.
kaḻiperuñ ciṟappu	- tol. 1013
kaḻipaṭar uṟīi	- tol. 1029
iṉik kaḻivu eṉṉum col iṟanta kālam (1103), muṭinta ceyal (737) eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ātalāl, kaḻi eṉpatē uriccol vaṭivu eṉakkoḷḷa ēvukiṉṟatu. kaḻivu eṉṉum āṭciyil mikutip poruḷ varutal aṟintāl aṉṟi appāṭam koḷḷal āyat takkatām. 
kūrppum kaḻiyum uḷḷatu ciṟakkum eṉap pāṭam iruntu paṭi eṭuttōr kaḻiyum eṉpatik kaḻivum eṉa eḻutiṉarō eṉa eṇṇa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. āṉāl, tolkāppiya mūlam pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷ āḻ nōkkāyvup patippil pāṭa vēṟupāṭu uḷḷatākak kāṭṭap paṭavilli eṉpatum aṟiyat takkatām.
kaḻicaṭi:
kaḻicaṭi eṉpatu vacic collāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. avaṉ(ḷ) oru kaḻicaṭi, kaḻicaṭip payal, antak kaḻicaṭiyuṭaṉ ēṉ pēcukiṟāy? ippaṭik kēṭkum ceytikaḷ nāḷum uṇṭu.
kaḻital, akalutal poruḷatu; vaḻital, vaḻintōṭutal eṉpaṉavum avvaḻip poruḷavē. kaḻiccal nōy (pēti) eṉa oru nōyē uṇṭu. vayiṟṟup pōkku eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷik kāṭṭum. kakkal, kaḻiccal eṉpavi (vānti, pēti) cērntiruntāl eṉṉām?
kālkaḻi kaṭṭil eṉpatu iṟantōrik kiṭattum kālillāk kaṭṭilām (pāṭi). ivaṇ kaḻital iṉmiyik kuṟittatu. kaḻintatu poḻutu eṉpatil poḻutu muṭintu pōṉatic cuṭṭiṟṟu. poḻutik kaḻikkiṟāṉ eṉpatu vēliyillātu nāḷik kaḻittalik kuṟittatu.
kaḻivāy kaḻimukam uppaṅkaḻi eṉpavi kaṭal cārntavi. nilattiṭṭuk kaḻinta nīrppakuti kaḻivāy eṉappaṭṭatu. akkaḻiyil paṭaku vantu cellum tuṟi kaḻimukam, uppu eṭuppataṟkākap payaṉpaṭuttum nīrppakuti uppaṅkaḻi.
kaḻikalaṉ makaṭūu eṉpatu muntiyōr uri: kalaṉ kaḻi makaḷir eṉpatum apporuḷatē. kaṇavaṉi iḻanta kimmi makaḷir maṅkala aṇiyik kaḻaṟṟal vaḻiyāka vanta peyar itu. kaḻittuk kaṭṭutal eṉpatu tīrttuk kaṭṭutal.
karuccitivik kaḻippu eṉpatu ciṟṟūr vaḻakku. karukkalippu, karuccitippu, karuvaḻippu ivaṟṟilellām karu uṇṭu. kaḻippu eṉṟālē, karukkalippik kuṟikkum collāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṉic ceytalil tērntavaḷ kalippuk kāri eṉappaṭukiṟāḷ. akkaḻippik koṇṭu ceyviṉi mutalākac collappaṭum tīviṉi ceypavaṉ kaḻiviṉiyāḷaṉ eṉappaṭukiṟāṉ. kaṇṇēṟu kaḻittalum kaḻippē.  ataṟku veḷḷik kiḻamikaḷil vaṟṟal  uppup pōṭṭu eriyūṭṭuk kaḻippuc ceytal vaḻakku.
kaṇṭatik kaḻiyatit tiṉṉātē eṉṉum vaḻakki evar aṟiyār? kaṇṭatu, pārtta poruḷ; kaḻiyatu, uṭalukku ākātu, ovvātu eṉa vilakkappaṭṭa poruḷ. cilar vāyaṭakkam, maṉavaṭakkam koḷḷāmal tiṉṟu keṭuvatit taṭukkum kaṭṭaḷi itu.
kaḻivup pañcum kācātal teriyumē! kaḻivu vikkōl tāḷum, kūrit takaṭu āka aṟiviyal vaḷam utavukiṟatē! kaḻippaṟi nakarattil kāṇappaṭṭuc ciṟṟūrkku vantami cīrallavā! kaṭṭaṇak kaḻippaṟi koṭi kaṭṭip paṟakkum kāṭciyip paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷilum cāli vaḻikaḷilum evar aṟiyār? vēṇṭāp poruḷkaḷip pōṭṭu vikka vīṭukaḷil kaḻivaṟiyuṇṭu; celvarkaḷ vīṭṭuk kaḻivaṟikaḷil pala ciṟu kuṭumpaṅkaḷē vāḻalām.
nālvakik kaṇakkilē kaḻittal illiyā? kūṭṭik kaḻikkat teriyātavar pāṭu, kaṇakkil eṉṉa pāṭu? kūṭṭik kaḻikkat teriyāta oruvar, vīṭṭu vēliyāḷākak kūṭa, kālantaḷḷa muṭiyātē.
kaḻīi, kaḻuvi, kaḻūu eṉpaṉavellām akaṟṟutal, appāl paṭuttutal, pōkkutal eṉṉum poruḷavē. ettaṉi pērkaḷ eṉṉeṉṉa vakikkellām ki kaḻuviyatākac colkiṟārkaḷ! kaḻuvāy tēṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam kūṭap palarkku varuvatilliyē! mācu pōkki maṇiyākkutal maṇi kaḻuvutal eṉṟē vaḻaṅkum. kaḻūumaṇi eṉpatu ilakkaṇa ilakkiya āṭcikaḷ.
kaḻicaṭi eṉpatik kāṇpōm. oruvarik kaḻicaṭi eṉṟāl evvaḷavu ciṉam uṇṭākiṟatu? ciṉam maṭṭumā? eppaṭic cīṟukiṟār? cāykkaṭi eṉṟāl eppaṭi aruvaṟuppākak karutuvārō appaṭiyaṉṟō kaḻicaṭi eṉṟālum karutukiṟār.
kaḻicaṭi eṉpatu eṉṉa? tali cīvukiṟōm; cīvum pōtu mayir, cīppuṭaṉ varukiṟatu; utirkiṟatu; cilar cīvāta pōtum mayirtāṉē utirntu koṭṭutal aṟintatē; anta mayiri eṉṉa matippu matikkiṟōm; atu taliyil irunta pōtu evvaḷavu matippu ataṟku? ettaṉi ettaṉi eṇṇey - maṇam - cīvutal - aḻakuṟuttal! ettaṉi muṟi kaṇṇāṭiyil taṉṉit tāṉē pārttuk kaḷittal! ellām eṉṉa āyiṉa? utirnta mayir uṭalilō uṭiyilō oṭṭiyiruppiṉ aruvaṟuppāyt toṭāmal toṭṭeṭuttu ūtit taḷḷukiṟōm. illiyēl viralāl cuṇṭik kīḻē vīḻttukiṟōm. kiyiyum kaḻuvukiṟōm. inta māṟṟam ēṉ? ataṉ nilimāṟṟamē immatippu māṟṟattiṟku aṭippaṭi. itaṉit teḷivākat terinta tiruvaḷḷuvar kaṟpavar neñcil paṭumāṟu,
taliyiṉ iḻinta mayiraṉiyar māntar
 niliyiṉ iḻintak kaṭi
eṉṟār (964). avar itaṟku etiriṭiyiyum eṇṇiṉār. niliyil tiriyātavar avar. ataṉāl,
niliyil tiriyātu aṭaṅkiyāṉ tōṟṟam
 maliyiṉum māṇap peritu
eṉṟār (124).
tiruvaḷḷuvar ippaṭic colliyirukkiṟār eṉpatit terintavarō teriyātavarō oruvar kaḻicaṭi eṉṟār. caṭi eṉpatu talimuṭi; ataṉ kaṟṟi; ataṉ piṉṉal; atiliruntu kaḻinta mayirkku eṉṉa peyar? kaḻicaṭi. caṭiyiliruntu kaḻinta mayir kaḻicaṭi. ilinuṉi, ‘nuṉi ili’yāka villiyā?, ilvāy, vāyil ākavilliyā? caṭi kaḻi kaḻicaṭi āyiṟṟu. ilakkaṇam terintavar itaṉi ilakkaṇap pōli eṉpar. evarum eṉṉa ilakkaṇamum collik koḷḷuṅkaḷ; eṅkaḷukkuk kavali yilli eṉṟu potumakkaḷ coṟkaḷip paṭittuviṭṭu viṭukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ paṭitta collukku viḷakkam iyalpāy - iṉimiyāy - atē poḻutil arumiyāy irukkiṟatu! ēṉeṉil appaṭippāḷiyiṉ nōkkam paṭippāka iruntatē yaṉṟik kuṟukiya nōkkup pukavilli. viḷakkam iruntatē yaṉṟi viruppu veṟuppu aruntatu illi.
oru kuṟaḷiṉ poruḷi oru vaḻakkuccol kaḻicaṭic col tantuviṭukiṟatu eṉṟāl potumakkaḷ vaḻakku eppaṭi eppaṭiyellām pōṟṟik koḷḷat takkatu.
kaḻittal:
kaḻittal = karukkalippu.
kaḻittal kaṇakkil uṇṭu. kaḻittuk kaṭṭal, otukkiviṭal, tīrttuviṭal poruḷil uṇṭu. āṉāl ikkaḻittal avvakippaṭṭataṉṟu. kaḻippukkup paṇṭuvacciyar muṉpē iruntaṉar. appoḻutu kaḻippatu paḻippataṟku iṭamāṉa ceyalāka iruntatu. karuccitittal kaṭumpāvam eṉṉum karuttum iruntatu. itukāl malaccikkal nīkka maruttuvam pōl iyalpākiviṭṭatu.
ippoḻutē kaḻittuviṭṭāl eḷitu kaḻikka nāṭpaṭṭāl veḷippaṭṭu viṭum kaḻikka oṉṟum cuṇakkam vēṇṭā. evarukkum aiyam varātu. eṉpaṉa vellām eṅkum kēṭkum ceytikaḷ. evarum aṟiyār eṉa eṇṇi ellārum aṟiyac ceyyum ceyalkaḷ ivi.
kaḻippaṟi:
kaḻippu + aṟi = kaḻippaṟi.
kaḻippaṟi = iyaṟkiyāka uṭalil iruntu veḷiyēṟṟa mākum nīr, malam āyavaṟṟik kaḻikkum - pōkac ceyyum - aṟi kaḻippaṟi ākum.
kaḻital, kaḻiccal eṉpavi eṇṇāmalē veḷiyēṟum malaveḷippāṭām.
* kaḻivaṟi kāṇka.
kaḻimukam:
kaḻimukam; vaṭimukam eṉpatum itu. nīr niṟkāmalum tēṅkik kiṭavāmalum iyalpāka vaṭintōṭum kaṭaṟpakuti kaḻimukam ākum. kaḻital = vaḻital.
kaḻital aminta iṭam kaḻivāy eṉpatumām.
kiti vēlik kaḻivāy	- cilap. 7:43
kaḻiyōṭi:
kaṭalnīr nilattuḷ kālpōl uḷvāṅkic cellutal kaḻi eṉappaṭum. atu paṭakup pōkkuvarattukkup payaṉpaṭum. ataṉāl atu neṭiyatāka irukkum. kaḻi neṭumip poruḷatu. akkaḻiyil cellum paṭaku kaḻiyōṭiyākum. celuttuvārum appeyar peṟuvar. īḻattil kaṭaṟpaṭi ūṭaṟuttuc ceṉṟu uḷnāṭṭuḷ akkaḻiyip payaṉpaṭuttutalāl kaṭaṟpaṭik kaḻiyōṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
kaḻivaṟi:
kaḻivu + aṟi = kaḻivaṟi.
vēṇṭāp poruḷkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭutta utavāp poruḷ kaḷiyum pōṭṭu vikkum kaṭṭaṭattu orupakuti kaḻivaṟi yākum.
kaḻivaṟi vēṟu, kaḻippaṟi vēṟu.
* kaḻippaṟi kāṇka.
kaḻivu vaki:
	cakki 	= 	muntiri elāmicci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaḻivu 
	kotakku	= 	puḷiyiṉ kaḻivu.
	kūnti	= 	paṉaṅkāyiṉ kaḻivu.
	kōtu	= 	karumpiṉ kaḻivu. (col. ā.ka. 69)
kotakku kotti eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
kaḻu:
kaḻu:1
taṉmēl vittatu ūṭuruvik kīḻiṟaṅkumāṟu ceyyap paṭṭa vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa kolik karuvi. paṇṭi nāḷiṉ kolik karuviyākak kaḻu iruntatu. kaḻu vēṟṟutal kuṟṟat taṇṭavakiyuḷ oṉṟu. cilampil uriyāciriyar iraṇṭaṉ urupu virikka eṉṟatāl vanta puṉivu āyiram poṟkollarik kaḻu vēṟṟiyatu.
kaḻu:2
kaḻuvil māṭṭal eṉpatoru vaḻakku muṉpuṇṭu. tiruṭiyavaṉik kaṇṭu meyppitta piṉ tarum taṇṭaṉi vakiyuḷ oṉṟu kaḻuvil māṭṭal, kaḻuvil pōṭal eṉpavi.
iraṇṭu kālkaḷiyum iyaṅkā vakiyil iraṇṭu tuḷic caṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ iṭiyē vittu āṇi tirukkik kaṭṭiyi akalāmal ceyvatē atu.
kuṭṭiyil pōṭal, kuṭṭi māṭṭal eṉpatum atu. paḻanāḷil paḷḷikku vārāta māṇavarkaḷip paṟṟi vantu kaḻuvil māṭṭal, kōtaṇṭam pōṭal eṉṉum taṇṭaṉikaḷ tarappaṭṭatuṇṭu.
kaḻukkā maḻukkā:
	kaḻukku	= 	pūṇ tēyntu pōṉa ulakki.
	maḻukku	= 	kūr maḻuṅkip pōṉa arivāḷ mutaliya karuvikaḷ.
kaḻukkākavum maḻukkākavum iruppavi cevvi yaṟṟaṉavāyp payaṉpaṭuttutaṟku utavātaṉavāy amintavi. avaṟṟip pōl uḻippukku utavāmal iruppavaṉik kaḻukkā maḻukkāvāyt tirikiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku.
kaḻuntarāy uṉakaḻal paṇiyātavar eṉpatil kampar (pāyiram) kaḻuntu eṉpatip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟār. kaḻuntu pūṇpōṉa ulakkiyām. maḻuṅkutal moṭṭiyātal poruḷil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉik kaḻukkaṭṭi maḻukkaṭṭi eṉpatum vaḻakku.
kaḻutu:
kāṭu kāvaṟku amikkappaṭṭa kāl ūṉṟu mēṭi kaḻutu ākum. nāṉku allatu āṟu kālkaḷi ūṉṟi mēlē kampu ili taḻikaḷip parappiyatu kaḻutu. kāval kāppavaroṭu kāṭṭu viḷiporuḷ vippukkum payaṉpaṭum cumitāṅki atu.
uḷimāṉ tuppiṉ ōṅkutiṉip perumpuṉattuk
 kaḻutiṟ kāṉavaṉ piḻimakiḻntu vatinteṉa	- akam. 102
tiruccirāppaḷḷi - ceṉṉi neṭuñcāliyil toḻutūri yaṭuttuk kaḻutūr eṉṉum ūr uḷḷatu. kaḻutiṉip paraṇ, paraṇi eṉpatu ma.va.
kaḻuti:
kaḻu > kaḻutu > kaḻuti.
veḷuttal, calavi ceytal, tuvittal eṉa innāḷil vaḻaṅkum toḻil paḻanāḷil kaḻuvutal eṉappaṭṭatu. kāl kikaḷi nīrāl aḻukkup pōkkal kālki kaḻuval eṉṟum mukam kaṇ ākiyavaṟṟi nīrviṭṭut tuṭittal mukam kaḻuvutal kaṇ kaḻuvutal eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nīrāl aḻukkup pōkkalē paḻanāḷil kaḻuvutal eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam kaḻāatu uṭīi
eṉṉum kuṟuntokip pāṭṭil (167) tayirpicinta kiyik kaḻuvāmal uṭiyilē tuṭittuk koṇṭu tāḷitam taṉcuvi kuṉṟāmal ākkum īṭupāṭṭil camikkum maṉiyāṭṭiyic cuṭṭukiṟār kūṭalūr kiḻār.
kaliṅkamāvatu uṭi; atu kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam; ivaṟṟāl kaḻuvutal tūymippaṭuttutal eṉṉum poruḷil varutal pulappaṭum.
kaḷiṟu nīrttuṟiyil paṭiya iḷañciṟār ataṉ kompik kaḻuvutali,
ūrkkuṟu mākkaḷ veṇkōṭu kaḻāaliṉ
nīrttuṟi paṭiyum peruṅkaḷiṟu
eṉṟu auviyār pāṭukiṟār (puṟam. 94).
kaḻuvutalāl polivaminta muttu māliyi
kaḻūu viḷaṅku āram
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟu (19).
kaḻuvāy eṉpatu pōkkum vaḻi; nīrviṭṭuk kaḻuvutalāl ākiya oru caṭaṅku. vaḻuvāy maruṅkil kaḻuvāyum uḷa (puṟam. 34) eṉṟu ālattūr kiḻār pāṭukiṟār.
kaḻuvutaṟkuk koṇṭu cellum tuṇikaḷip potikaḷākkik koṇṭu cellutal paṇṭē vaḻaṅkiya vaḻakkām. kaḻuvutaṟkuk koṇṭu cellum tuṇippotiyic cumattalāl kaḻuti eṉap peyar koṇṭatu eṉpati aṟiyalām. piṉṉi uppu, nel mutaliyavi koṇṭu cellap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. itaṉi,
niripparap poṟiya narippuṟak kaḻuti
eṉa uppuccumi cumattalic cuṭṭiya kuṟippāl aṟiyalām (akam. 207).
kaḻuti pōṉṟatum uyaramāṉatumām oṉṟu kōvēṟu kaḻuti. makkaḷ ēṟic cellum ūrtiyāyiṟṟu. kallāñcaraḷi, karaṭu, mēṭu, malic carivukaḷil poruḷ ēṟṟumatikku vaṇṭip pōkkup pōl ākiyatu. ulakaḷāviya cumitāṅkiyām pēṟum peṟṟatu.
tōṟṟavar nāṭṭi iḻimippaṭutta veṉṟavar, kaḻuti ērpūṭṭi veḷvarakum veḷ eḷḷum vitittal paḻanāḷ vaḻakku.
taṉi oruvar kaḷavō allatu iḻiceyalō ceytāl, ūravar kaḻutimēl ēṟṟik karumpuḷḷi cempuḷḷi kutti ūrvalamākkic cāṇakam peytal ciṟṟūr vaḻakkāka aṇmi vari iruntatu.
iṉi, tuṇicumakkum kaḻuti eṅkum pōkāmal kākka irukālkaḷiyum taḷittal vaḻakkam. taḷittal kaḻu eṉappaṭum. kaḻup pōṭutalāl kaḻuti eṉappaṭṭatumām. ēṟpaṉa koḷka.
kaḻuti tēyntu kaṭṭeṟumpu āyatu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. cetta kaḻutimēl ettaṉi kaḻuti ēṟiṉāl eṉṉa? kuṭṭiyāy irukkiyil kaḻutikkum eṭṭuppaṅku aḻaku eṉpavi pōlap palappala paḻamoḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kaḻutikkāl kaṭṭil:
maṭakkuk kaṭṭiliṉ kāl vaḷintu, oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu piṉṉi iruppatāl oppu vaki kaṇṭu ataṉik kaḻutik kālkaṭṭil eṉpatu virutunakar vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. kūriya oppup pārviyum nakiccuviyuṇarvum kūṭiya collāṭci itu.
kaḻutip piṟavi:
kaḻutip piṟavi = cumi cumattal.
kaḻuti eṉṟāl poti cumakka veṉṟē aminta vilaṅkātal veḷippaṭi. mJ nghš áy®¡F¤ jh§f kh£lh¡ FL«g¢ Rik mikªJÉL« nghJ, ‘v‹ ãwÉ fGij¥ ãwÉah» É£lJ’ RkªJ jhnd Mf nt©L«; nt©lh vd¤ jŸËdhš e«ik É£L¥ nghFkh? eṉa nonturikkum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
cari, cumi tāṅka muṭiyāteṉa ōṭiviṭa vēṉum muṭiyumā? atutāṉ kālil taḷi pōṭappaṭṭuḷḷatē! ivaṉukkut taḷi maṉivi makkaḷāmē! taḷi pōṭal kālkaṭṭu ātalik kāṇka!
kaḻutippuli:
kaḻuti pōlum niṟamum pulipōlum variyum uṭimiyāl kaḻutippuli eṉa makkaḷ peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar.
kaḻutippuli iṟanta vilaṅkukaḷiyē uṇpatākavum, elumpik kaṭittuṇṇali yuṭiyatu eṉpatākavum kūṟuvar (tamiḻ ilakkiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷum vilaṅkiṉac coṟkaḷum pak: 47)
kaḻuttiru:
kaḻuttilē pōṭappaṭum tirumaṅkala nāṇik kaḻuttiru eṉpatu nāṭṭuk kōṭṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. kaḻuttil aṇiyum tiru eṉpatām. maṅkalam, maṅkalanāṇ, maṅkala aṇi eṉpavi maṅkala maṭanti eṉpataṉ aṭiyāḷam ātaliṉ avvāṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
kaḻuttu oṭital:
kaḻuttu oṭital = aḷavillāta poṟuppu.
tāṅka māṭṭāta cumiyit talimēl vittāl tali, tāṅkiya poruḷik kaḻuttut tāṅka māṭṭāmal vaḷiyum; kuḻiyum; cuḻukkum uṇṭām. talimēl uḷḷa poruḷi appaṭiyē taḷḷi viṭavum nērum. anniliyil at tāṅka māṭṭāc cumiyik kaḻutti oṭikkum cumi eṉpar. atu pōl oruvar kuṭumpam peritāki viṭṭālō, poṟuppu aḷavu kaṭantu mikuntu viṭṭālō kaḻuttu oṭikiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku. tāṅka muṭiyāta aḷavillāta poṟuppu eṉpatu poruḷām.
kaḻuttēr:
kaḻuttu + ēr = kaḻuttēr.
kaḻuttu ēr; muṉ ēri aṭuttup piṉṉē cellum ērik kaḻuttēr eṉpatu nellip pakuti vaḻakkākum. īṟṟaṭiyi aṭutta muṉ aṭiyi eruttaṭi eṉa yāppilakkaṇam kūṟuvati eṇṇalām. eruttu = kaḻuttu.
kaḻunīr:
kaḻunīr eṉpatu kuvaḷi. kuvaḷik kaṇṇaṉ, kuvaḷik kaṇṇi eṉpavi āṇ peṇ peyarkaḷ.
karuṅkuvaḷi, ceṅkuvaḷi eṉpaṉa karuṅkaṇ ceṅkaṇ ākiya iruvakik kaṇkaḷukkum uvami āvaṉa. nīril vāḻum koṭik kuvaḷi malarkaḷiṉ mēlpaṭṭa nīrttuḷi kaṇṇiṉ nīrttuḷiyeṉat tōṉṟiyamiyāl kaluḻnīr eṉappaṭṭu kaḻunīr ākiyirukkalām eṉpatu oṉṟu. iḵtu iyalpākak kaṇṭārkkup puṟattōṟṟattāl peyariṭa vāyppatu.
maṟṟoṉṟu ariya maruttuva āyviṉ aṭippaṭiyil amivatu. atu, uṭalil uṇṭākiya arattakkaṭṭik kaḻunīrccāṟu akaṟṟa vallatu eṉpatu. cennīr uṟivi akaṟṟum nalappāṭṭāl kaḻunīr eṉappaṭṭatākalām eṉpatu. muṉṉatu potumakkaḷ pāvalar paṭippākalām; piṉṉatu maruttuvar āyvu muṭipu ākalām.
kaḻuvāy:
kaḻuvutalāl ēṟpaṭuvatu kaḻuvāy. kuṟṟam ceytu akkuṟṟatti naṟceyalkaḷāl akaṟṟutal, kuṟṟam eṉa uṇarntu ataṉi mīḷac ceyyātu oḻittal eṉpavi tīrvu vaḻikaḷām.
eṟṟeṉ ṟiraṅkuva ceyyaṟka ceyvāṉēl
 maṟṟaṉṉa ceyyāmi naṉṟu
eṉpatu aṟaneṟi (tiruk. 655).
anneṟi viṭuttu, nīrāl aḻukkik kaḻuvuvatu pōl akattē koṇṭu ceyta pāvatti  nīrāl kaḻuviviṭa muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil,
puṟantūymi nīrāṉ amiyum akantūymi
 vāymiyāl kāṇap paṭum
eṉpatu aṟaneṟi (tiruk. 298). vaḻuvāykkuk kaḻuvāy uṇṭu; ataṉ vaḻiyākat tīrkkalām eṉpatu tamiḻneṟi aṉṟām; ayalneṟi aḵtām.
vaḻuvāy maruṅkil kaḻuvāyum uḷaveṉa
 nilampuṭi peyarva tāyiṉum oruvaṉ
ceyti koṉṟārk kuyti illeṉa
aṟampā ṭiṟṟē
eṉṉum puṟappāṭal (34),
ennaṉṟi koṉṟārkkum uyvuṇṭām uyvilli
ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟa makaṟku
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷ (110).
kaḻuvēṟṟal:
kaḻuvēṟṟal kuṟṟat taṇṭaṉiyākap paḻanāḷ vēntarāl tarappaṭṭatu.
koliyiṟ koṭiyāri vēntoṟuttal piṅkūḻ
kaḷikaṭ ṭataṉoṭu nēr	- tiruk. 550
eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaḷvaṉik kōṟal kaṭuṅkōl aṉṟu
veḷvēl koṟṟaṅkāṇ
eṉṟu āriyap paṭikaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ urittatākac cilampu (20:64-65) collum.
kaḻiyāvatu mūṅkil. nāḻi, marakkāl mutaliya mukattal aḷavik karuviyāka irunta mūṅkil, kūri vēytaṟkuriya variccu, kampu mutaliyavākavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. kaḻikkūttu, kaḻi tāval karuviyum āyatu. kaḷi kutti eṭukkavum mūṅkil piḷāccu kūrmiyākac cīvip payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
akkaḻiyil kalmūṅkil eṉa oṉṟu vayiram peṟṟa tiṇmiyiṉatu. ataṉik kūrmiyākac cetukki ataṉmēl kuṟṟavāḷiyiṉ irukki vāyi vittu, ēṟṟiviṭiṉ paṭip paṭiyē iṟaṅki uyir pōkip paṟavikku iriyāyp pōyoḻiyum avaṉ uṭal.
kaḻuvaṉ poṭṭal eṉpatu cāṉṟu. akkaḻuvēṟṟalic ceytavaṉ koṭiyaṉ allaṉ; koṭiyaṉit taṇṭikkak koṭikoṇṭa talivaṉām kō ēva, avaṉ āṇippaṭi kaṭaṉāṟṟalik kaṭamiyākak koṇṭavaṉ. avaṉmēl paḻiyilli yēṉum paḻippeyar peṟṟāṉ. kaḻuvaṉ eṉpatu avaṉ peyarāyiṟṟu. pāṭṭaṉ peyarip pēraṉ tāṅkum muṟiyāl innāḷ kūṭak kaḻuvaṉ pēriṉar uḷḷaṉar. paḻippeyar eṉa aṟiyār; avar pāṭṭaṉ pēreṉavē aṟivār.
kaḻuvēṟal kaḻuvēṟṟal vēṟupāṭu illiyā? kaḻuvēṟi yatu paḻiya tiruviḷiyāṭal; kaḻuvēṟṟiyatu putiya tiruviḷi yāṭal.
kaḷañciyam:
uḻavar kaḷattil iruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭa nel, pul, cōḷam, kampu, kēḻvaraku mutaliyavi keṭṭup pōkā vakiyil koṭṭi vikkappaṭṭa iṭam puri eṉappaṭum. puri nilamaṭṭattil iruntu uyarttik kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. puri uyarvākum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (785).
puri eṉappaṭuvataṟkuk kaḷañciyam eṉpatu maṟṟoru peyar. araṇmaṉi, kōyil ākiyavaṟṟil amikkappaṭṭa kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ mikap periyavi; uyaramāṉavi. avi aḵkacāli eṉappaṭṭaṉa. aḵkam - tavacam. aḵkam curukkēl eṉpatu ātti cūṭi.
kaḷamākkal:
kaḷamākkal = illāmi allatu veṟumiyākkal.
kaḷam, pōrkkaḷam, cūṭaṭikkum neṟkaḷam, vīrarkaḷ pōr purivatu ceṅkaḷam eṉavum paṭum. uḻavarkaḷ payir paccikaḷi akaṟṟi mēṭākkik keṭṭippaṭuttuvatu neṟkaḷamām. illāk kāl atil katiraṭippu, piṇiyaliṭal eṉpavaṟṟic ceyvataṟku vāykkātu. ērkkaḷamākkal itu. pōrkkaḷa mākkiṉāl eṉṉa ākum; ellāk koṭumikaḷukkum iṭamākum.
vāḻvār ellām vaṉcāvukku iriyāvar. ātalāl kaḷamākkal aḻippu vēliyākavē amintu viṭum. tam kuṭiyik keṭukkum makkaḷi, nī kaḷamākki viṭuvāy eṉap paḻippatu mutiyavarkaḷ vaḻakku.
kaḷam:
maṇalukku aṭiyil uḷḷa karumaṇṇik kaḷam eṉpatu nelli vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. kaḷam = karumi. kaḷar maṇ eṉpatu karuniṟac cēṟṟumaṇ; aḷaṟu eṉpatum atu. kālāḻ kaḷar eṉpatu vaḷḷuvam (500).
kaḷari:
kaḷappōrkkuriya karuvikaḷik koṇṭu payiṟci peṟum iṭam kaḷari eṉappaṭṭatu. cilampam, vāḷ mutaliya karuvip payiṟciyum mal mutalām payiṟciyum peṟṟa iṭam kaḷari eṉap paṭṭatu.
kaḷam eṉpatu pōrkkaḷam ākum.
kaḷarppaṭu:
kaḷar + paṭu = kaḷarppaṭu = kaḷarnilam allatu uvarnilam.
kaḷar = uvar; paṭu = paṭintu kiṭakkum nilam.
kaḷarppaṭu kūval tōṇṭi	- puṟam. 311
kaḷari eṉpatum itu.
kaḷari parantu kaḷḷi pōki	- puṟam. 356
						(ci.ta.co.ā. 42)
kaḷarppāḻ:
kaḷ + ar = kaḷar. kaḷar + pāḻ = kaḷarppāḻ.
kariya niṟamum uḷvāṅku cēṟum uvarum amintu viḷivukkup payaṉpaṭā nilam (pāḻ nilam), kaḷarppāḻ nilamākum. (ka.ka.co.a.)
uppeṭukkum kaḷarnilam uvarnilamākum.
kaḷarnilattup piṟanta uppu	- nālaṭi. 133
kaḷavaḻi:
pōrkkaḷac ceytiyik kūṟum nūl kaḷavaḻiyām. kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu eṉṟoru nūl kīḻkkaṇakkil uḷḷatu. pōrkkaḷa nikaḻcciyiyē puṉinturikkum annūl poykiyārāl pāṭappaṭṭatām. atu veṇpā yāppiṉatu.
pōrkkaḷam cerukkaḷam, maṟakkaḷam, amarkkaḷam, ceṅkaḷam eṉavum peyar peṟum. maṟakkaḷa vañci, cerukkaḷa vañci kāṇka.
ōo uvamaṉ uṟaḻviṉṟi ottatē
kāviri nāṭaṉ kaḻumalaṅ koṇṭanāḷ
māvutippa māṟṟār kuṭiyelāṅ kīḻmēlā
āvuti kāḷāmpi pōṉṟa puṉaṉāṭaṉ
mēvāri yaṭṭa kaḷattu
eṉpatoru kaḷavaḻippāṭṭu (36).
kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu eṉpatu peyarāyiṉum atil nāṟpattoru pāṭalkaḷ uḷa. puṟattiraṭṭil varum 1427 ām pāṭal kaḷavaḻi eṉṟiruppiṉum nūlil iṭampeṟa villi. 41 pāṭalkaḷumē kaḷattu eṉṟē muṭinili yaṭikiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 36 pāṭalkaḷ aṭṭa kaḷattu eṉṟum, 3 pāṭalkaḷ poruta kaḷattu eṉṟum, ovvoru pāṭal vīḻnta kaḷattu peyta kaḷattu eṉṟum muṭikiṉṟaṉa. kaḷattu eṉṉum muṟṟāṭciyiyē puṟattiraṭṭuk kūṟum pāṭṭum koṇṭuḷḷatu. civanta kaḷattu eṉpatu ataṉ muṭiniliyākum.
kaḷavu:
kaḷavu = uḷḷattik kavartal.
kaḷavu aimperuṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka eṇṇappaṭṭatu. piṟarkku urimippaṭṭa oṉṟi avaraṟiyāmal vañcittuk kavarntu koḷvatē kaḷavām. ippollāk kaḷaviṉit tavirttu ulakil peruka vaḻaṅkum kaḷavum uṇṭu. atu nalla kaḷaveṉa nāṭu koḷvatu. ataṉiyē tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliyaṉa kaḷaviyal eṉak kūṟum. akkaḷavu, oruvar uḷḷatti oruvar kavartalām. pāl orumippāṭṭāl naṭpākavum, pāl vēṟupāṭṭāl kātalākavum koḷḷappaṭum. kaṟpukku muṟpaṭṭatu kaḷavu eṉpatu tamiḻneṟi. iṟivaṉi aṭiyārkaḷ uḷḷaṅ kavar kaḷvaṉ eṉpatum kaḷavē. ikkaḷavē iṟiyaṉpām.
kaḷavu kaṇṇi:
kātal vayappaṭṭa talivaṉ oruvaṉ pērāciyāl naḷḷiruḷ pōtil taliviyik kaḷaviṟ koḷḷumāṟu cellutalik kūṟutal kaḷavukaṇṇi eṉpatām. itaṉi,
māṉāci
yāliṟivaṉ kaṅkulilē yārkaḷaviṟ cellutalē
cāluṅ kaḷavukaṇṇi tāṉ
eṉkiṟatu pirapantat tiraṭṭu (69).
kaḷaviṟ celluṅkāl ēṟpaṭum iṭiyūṟukaḷi viritturittu attaṭikaḷi ellām veṟṟiyaṭinta varalāṟṟi viḷakkuvatāka ippaṉuval amitalām.
kaṇṇāṟ ciṟikoṇṭu akappaṭuttutalik kaṇṇi eṉṟār. iṉṟum kātalik kaṇṇi pōṭutal, tūṇṭil pōṭutal, vali vīcutal eṉa vaḻakkāṟukaḷ uṇmi oppiṭak kūṭiyatām.
kaḷaviṟ puṇarccit taṭi, varuntoḻiṟku arumi eṉṟum, iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
iravaraṉ māliya ṉēvaru tōṟum
kāvalar kaṭukiṉum katanāy kurippiṉum
nīṭutuyil eḻiṉum nilavuveḷip paṭiṉum
vēypuri meṉṟōḷ iṉṟuyil eṉṟum
peṟāaṉ peyariṉum
eṉṟu varum kuṟiñcippāṭṭum (239-243),
tāyum nāyum ūruntuñ cāmi
 kāvalar kaṭukutal nilavu veḷippaṭutal
kūki kuḻaṟutal kōḻikuraṟ kāṭṭutal
ākiya ēḻum allukkuṟit talivaṉ
varuntoḻiṟ karumi poruntuta luriya
eṉṟu varum akapporuḷ viḷakkamum (161) karutat takkaṉa.
kaḷā:
kaḷ > kaḷā = kariyatu; karuniṟattatu ākiyatoru paḻam.
kaḷā, ciṟuciṟu muṭkaḷi yuṭiyatu. kottuk kottāyk kāyppatu. kākkiyil kariyatu kaḷāmpaḻam eṉṉat tikaḻvatu. iṉippum puḷippum kalanta cuviyatu. ūṟukāyākavum payaṉpaṭuvatu.
kaḷā vakikaḷ:
ciṟukaḷā, peruṅkaḷā, malikkaḷā, muḷḷukkaḷā, cottik kaḷā, koṭṭikkaḷā, cīmikkoṭṭikkaḷā, kottukkaḷā, ūcikkaḷā. (cāmpa. maru. aka.)
kaḷikaḷ:
kaḷi + kaḷ = kaḷikaḷ = kaḷippūṭṭum kaḷ; kaḷkuṭittuk kaḷippavarkaḷ.
ivi maṭṭumā poruḷkaḷ. kaḷitta kaḷikkiṉṟa kaḷikkum kaḷ eṉa viṉittokiyum ākumallavā! kaḷi eṉṉum kaṭkuṭippeyar, makiḻvup poruḷ taruvatāl kaḷippu eṉap potumiyuṟṟatu.
ucci mukarntuviṭṭāl kaḷveṟi koḷḷuvatāyp pāṭiṉārē pāratiyār.
uṇṭāṭṭup paṭalam eṉap pāvikaṅkaḷ, toṉmaṅkaḷ āyavaṟṟil maṭṭumā? kaḷḷē uliyāka ēṟṟutalic cuṭṭukiṟatē puṟam (113).
maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum miviṭi ūḻppavum
kaḷḷē kuṭikeṭukkum eṉṟāl, nāṭunāṭāka ākkum matuvaki vīṭuvīṭākap pukuntu viḷiyāṭiṉāltāṉ kuṭiyaracu pōlum! kuṭikeṭukkum ciṉṉatiriyum, matuvakiyum kuṭikēṭu eṉa oḻikkum aracē makkaḷ nala aracu ākum.
kaḷittal:
kaḷḷarunti makiḻtal eṉṉum poruḷuṭiya iccol, makiḻtal eṉṉum potup poruḷilum varum. kaḷittal eṉpatu viḷiyāṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil muṉciṟi (muñciṟi) vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. ciṟi = kāṭu, aṇi.
kaḷimpup pāl:
kaṭṭi paṭṭuḷḷa cīmpāl oṭṭaṉcattira vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil kaḷimpup pāl eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. taṭavu kaḷimpukaḷ kaṭṭiyāka vum nīrākavum illāmal iṭi nikartta niliyil iruppati aṟiyalām.
kaḷiveruṭṭu:
mañcuveruṭṭu, callikaṭṭu eṉpavi tarumapuri vaṭṭārattil kaḷiveruṭṭu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. makiḻvāka veruṭṭip piṭippatāl kaḷiveruṭṭu āyatu. ēṟu taḻuvutal eṉpatu paḻavaḻakku. māṭupiṭi eṉpatu veṭcippōr. atu pōrt toṭakkam. itu vīraviḷiyāṭṭu eṉpatu ataṉ vēṟupāṭu.
kaḷi:
eṅkēyō oruvarip pārkkiṟōm. avari muṉpiṉ teriyātu. namakku evvakit toṭarpuṭiyārum allar. pālvēṟupāṭu uṭiyārum allar. āyiṉum, nam uḷḷattik koḷḷi koṇṭuviṭukiṟār. paṉmuṟi avar mukattit tirumpip pārttu makiḻattūṇṭi viṭukiṟār.
kaḷḷam kavaṭu illāta mukam; kuḻanti mukam; pāl vaḻiyum mukam; pārttāl paci tīrum mukam eṉṟellām nām niṉittu uḷḷuḷ makiḻac ceykiṟār. attaku mukattik kaḷiyāṉa mukam eṉṟum, mukakkaḷi eṉṟum ataṉi uṭiyavarikkaḷi yāṉavar kaḷiyāṉa mukattar eṉṟum yārum tūṇṭāmalē namakkuḷ uṇarntu pūrikkiṟōm.
polivukkum allatu aḻakukkum kaḷikkum toṭarpu uṇṭā? kaḷi eṉpatu aḻakākumā? akaramutaliyil kaḷi eṉpataṟku aḻaku eṉap poruḷ vaḻaṅkiṉālum ēṟkat takkatu tāṉā? ēṟpatu eppaṭi?
kaḷi eṉpatu payirukku iṭiyē muḷittup payirukkuriya nīriyum ūṭṭattiyum koṇṭu vaḷarvatuṭaṉ payiri nalittum viḷivik keṭuttum tīmi ceyvataṉṟō? ēriṉum (uḻaviṉum) nallatu eruviṭutal; eruviṭutaliṉum nallatu kaḷi veṭṭal eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar ōtukiṟārē? (1038) muḷikkum pōtē muḷmaram pōṉṟa kaḷikaḷik kaḷintu viṭa vēṇṭum! illāviṭiṉ kaḷipavaṉ kiyiyē aḻittuviṭum kaḷi eṉkiṟārē (879)! ikkaḷi aḻakākumā? kavarcci yuṭiyatākumā?
ōṭukiṟōm; kaṭiṉamāka iṭiyīṭu iṉṟi uḻikkiṟōm. kaḷippu uṇṭākiṟatu. kaḷippāṟa vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam uṇṭākiṉṟatu. kaḷip pāṟutal, kaḷiyāṟutal, iḷippāṟutal ōyveṭuttal eṉṟellām amintu kaḷippu nīṅkip piṉṉar ōyvālum uṟakkattālum uṇavālum ūkkam peṟṟu mīṇṭum uḻippil iṟaṅkukiṉṟōm. kaḷippu kaḷi (aḻaku) ākumā?
kaḷi kaḷital eṉpatoru toḻil. kaḷital eṉṟālē kaḷi kaḷitaltāṉ. eṉiṉum teḷivu karutik kaḷi kaḷital eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉi eṇṇip pārttāl kaḷiyiṉ aḻakup poruḷ teḷivuṟa viḷaṅkum.
kaḷi eṉpataṟkuk kaḻaṟṟu, piṭuṅku, pōkku, aviḻ, akaṟṟu, nīkku, vilakku eṉa ēvaṟ poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. kaḷital eṉiṉ kaḻaṟṟutal, piṭuṅkutal mutalāka mēṟkūṟiyavi amiyum.
kaḷi maṇṭik kiṭakkum iṭattiyum kaḷi kaḷinta iṭattiyum oppiṭṭup pārttāl kaḷi naṉku veḷippaṭa viḷaṅkum. ataṟku, aḻaku polivu, kavarcci mutalāka aminta poruḷ nayam teḷivākap pulaṉākum.
kaḷitalukku uriyavi eviyō avi kaḷi eṉappaṭutal kaṇkūṭu. pul, kīri, ceṭi, koṭi, maram, kuppi, caṇṭu, cāvi, ili, taḻi evi eṉiṉum kaḷitaṟku uriyavi ellām kaḷikaḷē! kaḷikaḷik kaḷinta piṉ ēṟpaṭuvatām polivum nalamum kaḷiyē!
kaḷiyeṭuttu nīr pāyccip pacumik kōlaṅ koṇṭu niṟkum payir nilattip pārkkap pārkka uḷḷam koḷḷi koṇṭa uḻavaṉ atil kaḷiyik kaṇṭāṉ! pāṭupaṭṭuk kaḷinta kaḷippi ellām kaḷiyat takka kaḷiyik kaṇṭu kaḷittāṉ! kaḷikku aḻakup poruḷi aruḷiṉāṉ!
aḻakukkeṉavē vaḷarkkappaṭum, pōṟṟik kākkappaṭum pūṅkāvil kaḷiyeṉak kaḷivaṉavaṟṟiyē pūṅkā taṉ payireṉak koṇṭiruntālum, ataṉi oḻuṅkuṟutti veṭṭuva veṭṭik koyva koytu, kuppi kūḷam akaṟṟic ceppamuṟuttākkāl pūṅkā eṉappaṭumā? kaviṉpeṟu vaṉappu eṉak karutappaṭumō? kāṭu eṉappaṭum. kā eṉappaṭātu. kaḷitalāl ēṟpaṭuvatu kaḷi eṉpatip pūṅkā taṉ polivāl teḷivittali evarē aṟiyār.
mukam vaḻikkāmal viṭṭuviṭṭāl eṉṉa? kaḷi maṇṭik kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpatum, muṭiveṭṭi mukam vaḻittalik kaḷiveṭṭutal eṉpatum putuvatāka vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkukaḷ. mukam, vaḻikkāta mukattiṟkum vaḻitta mukattiṟkum muṉṉik kaḷiyum piṉṉik kaḷiyum, kaḷikku aminta iruvakip poruḷkaḷiyum naṉku viḷakkum.
kaḷital eṉpatu kaḷi kaḷitali maṭṭumō kuṟittu niṉṟatu. muṭi kaḷital, uṭi kaḷital, aṇi kaḷital, aḻukkuk kaḷital, tuyar kaḷital ippaṭi ettaṉi ettaṉi kaḷitalkaḷ vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa. aḻukkilā mukam, avalamilā mukam, vañcam cūtillā mukam amiya vēṇṭiya amippellām oruṅkaminta mukam, puṉmuṟuval pūttuk kuluṅkum mukam, malarum kaṇṇum, makiḻum collum aminta aḻaku mukam kaḷiyāṉa mukam eṉpatil eṉṉa aiyam! mukakkaḷi eṉpatiltāṉ eṉṉa aiyam.
kaḷiyiṉ poruḷ etiriṭippaṭṭa teṉiṉum, ettaku nayamuṭiyatāka vāḻvoṭum iyintatāka amintuḷḷatu eṉpati eṇṇa eṇṇak kaḷippu uṇṭākavilli. kaḷiyē uṇṭākum; kaḷippē uṇṭākum.
kaḷikaṇ:
viḷipayirkku iṭiyūṟāy vaḷarum payir, kaḷi eṉpatākum. kaḷi eṉpatu kaḷital eṉṉum toḻil vaḻiyāka vanta peyarām. akaṟṟutal, pōkkutal, nīkkutal, kaḻaṟṟutal, otukkutal mutaliyavi kaḷital poruḷil varuvaṉavē eṉpati aṟintōm.
kaḷiyik kaḷitalukku vanta kaḷital peyar, āṭi kaḷital, aḻukkuk kaḷital, umikaḷital eṉap parupporuḷ kaḷukku varuvatuṭaṉ, tuyar kaḷital eṉak karuttup poruḷ kaḷukkum varum eṉa aṟintōm.
piṟarāl kaḷitaṟku uriya tuyarum uṇṭu; kaḷitaṟku ariya tuyarum uṇṭu; kaḷitaṟku muṭiyāta tuyarum uṇṭu. kaḷivariya tuṉpattik kāṭṭuvār kampar (uyut. 2536). kaḷiyāta tuṉpattik kāṭṭuvār iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷār (cilap. 19:17).
kaḷikaṇ eṉpatu irucoṟkaḷē eṉiṉum oru coṟṟaṉmip paṭṭu niṉṟatu. kaṇ eṉpatu ōr uṟuppup peyar eṉiṉum avvuṟuppiṉ payaṉām aruḷ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭu niṉṟatu. kaḷikaṇ eṉpatu tuyar kaḷiyum aruḷ uḷḷam eṉṉum poruḷ tarum taṉic collākap paḻami toṭṭum perukkamākavum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
veruvanta ceyyāmi (añcat takka ceyalic ceyyāmi) eṉpati aṭuttuk kaṇṇōṭṭam eṉṟōr atikāram vaḷḷuvar vakuttatum atil, kaṇṇiṟ kaṇikalam kaṇṇōṭṭam eṉṟum (575), kaṇṇōṭṭam illavar kaṇṇilar eṉṟum (577), kaṇṇeṉṉām kaṇṇōṭṭa millāta kaṇ eṉṟum (573), kaṇṇōṭṭattuḷḷatu ulakiyal eṉṟum (572), aruḷiṉ pālaṉavām. kaṇṇōṭṭa māvatu kaṇṇāl kāṇappaṭṭāri aruḷic ceytal eṉa maṇakkuṭavarum paripperumāḷum urittaṉar.
taṉṉōṭu payiṉṟārik kaṇṭāl avar kūṟiyaṉa maṟukka māṭṭāmi eṉṟār parimēlaḻakar. avar kūṟiyaṉa eṉpatu avartam allal, vaṟumi eṉpaṉa urittu avaṟṟit tīrttaruḷa vēṇṭukiyām.

kaḷital:
vitittu vaḷarvatu payir; vitiyāmal tōṉṟi vaḷarvatu kaḷi; eppayir āṉālum payirāvatum kaḷiyāvatum vitippatu vitiyātatip poṟuttaṉavē. nel, payirtāṉ; pūṅkāvil muḷittāl kaḷi! pul, kaḷitāṉ! pūṅkāvil vittup payiriṭṭāl payir!

kaḷi veṭṭutal eṉpatu vēḷāṇmit toḻil kaḷuḷ oṉṟu. veṭṭutaṟkup payaṉpaṭum karuvi maṇveṭṭi; karuvip peyarilē uḷḷa veṭṭi, kaḷiveṭṭut toḻiliyum ceyyum eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāyiṟṟē.
kallilē veṭṭum eḻuttu kalveṭṭu! maṇṇi veṭṭum karuvi maṇveṭṭi. kaḷi veṭṭutal poruḷ tarum col kaṭṭal kaṭṭutal eṉpaṉa. kaḷital - kaḷtal - kaṭṭal eṉṟu vantatu.

iṭukkaṇ kaḷivatām naṭpu
eṉpatu tuyar kaḷitalik kūṟum kuṟaḷ (788). veṭṭal karuvi viṉi; kaṭṭal karuvi viṉiyum kiviṉiyum ām.
vaḻippaṟi eṉpatil paṟittal toḻilum, nāṟṟup piṭuṅkutal eṉpatil piṭuṅkutal toḻilum, pūkkoytal eṉpatil eṭuttal toḻilum uḷḷami viḷaṅkum. ivi karuvi viṉikaḷ alla, kiviṉikaḷē ām.
kaḷiyic curaṇṭutaṟ keṉṟē aminta karuvi, kaḷicuraṇṭi. kaḷi curaṇṭiyiṉ iliyaḷavu (takaṭṭaḷavu) ciṟitu. ataṉiṉum akalamāka amintatu kaḷiparaṇṭi. atu paṭar koṭikaḷip paraṇṭum aḷavu takaṭu virivuṭiyatu. curaṇṭutal, paraṇṭutal ākiya toḻilkaḷukkuriya karuvi curaṇṭiyum paraṇṭiyum! curaṇṭi illāmalē mutukic curaṇṭalum, poruḷic curaṇṭalum kaṇkūṭu; cila uyirkaḷ kālāl paraṇṭal iyaṟki.
kaḷi kuttal oṉṟu; kaḷi kottal maṟṟoṉṟu. muṉṉataṟkuriya karuvi kaḷikutti; piṉṉataṟkuriyatu, kaḷikotti. kaḷiyiṉ vērip pārttu nērākak kutti eṭukka utavuvatu kaḷikutti; irumpālum kaḷikutti uṇṭu; marattālum kaḷikutti uṇṭu. kaḷiyip paṭar pakutiyuṭaṉ kotti eṭukka utavuvatu kaḷi kotti.  kuttikkum kottikkum nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. piṉṉē vēṟupāṭu maṟintatum uṇṭu. mīṉkuttiyi mīṉkottiyeṉa vaḻaṅkutalum uṇṭē!

kokkokka kūmpum paruvattu maṟṟataṉ
kuttokka cīrtta iṭattu
eṉṟār vaḷḷuvar (490).
maṇveṭṭiyuḷ ciṟiyatu koṭṭu maṇveṭṭi eṉpatu. ataṉik kaḷi veṭṭutaṟkup payaṉpaṭuttuvatāl kaḷikoṭṭu eṉpar. koṭṭutalum kaḷiveṭṭutal poruḷil varuvatik kāṭṭuvatu itu.
iṉi, akaḻtal tōṇṭal kallal kiḷḷal eṉpavi paṟittal poruḷil varum coṟkaḷām. ivi nāṉkum nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭuṭiyavi. kiḷḷalil kallalum, kallalil tōṇṭalum, tōṇṭalil akaḻtalum oṉṟil oṉṟu nilattil āḻamākac celpaviyām. nakattil kiḷḷi eṭuttal eṉṉum vaḻakkāl viralaḷavu āḻam kiḷḷalām; kiḻaṅku kalli eṭuttal eṉpatāl ataṉiṉ āḻam kallal; tōṇṭutal maṇveṭṭi patiyum aḷavu kaṭantu celvatāl ataṉiṉ āḻamāṉatu. kampi pōṭṭuk kutti eṭukkum aḷavuṭiyatu akaḻtalātalāl ataṉiṉum āḻamāṉatu. atu aṟuku kiḷḷal aṉṟu; kallal aṉṟu; tōṇṭal aṉṟu; akaḻtalām. aṟuku pōl vērūṉṟi eṉṉum vāḻttē vērāḻam kāṭṭum. ēḻaṭi eṭṭaṭikaḷukkuk kīḻum karical nilattil aṟukiṉ vērum kiḻaṅkum paṭintiruttal kaṇkūṭu. kiṇaṟu akaḻvatu pōl akaḻnteṭuppatum kāṇalām.
tōṇṭaliṉ āḻatti viḷakkuvatoru col tōṇi. toḷḷam, tōḷam eṉpaṉavum tōṇiyiṉ peyarkaḷē. pariya marattik kuṭintu mitavi yākkutalāl tōṇiyāyiṟṟu. tōṇṭi amikkappaṭṭatu tōṇṭi, tōṇi āyiṟṟu.
kaḷiveṭṭi muṭittu akkaḷiyip paṟitta iṭattilēyē pōṭṭuviṭṭāl mīṇṭum muḷippatum uṇṭu. ātalāl, ataṉi vārip pōṭutaṟku oru karuviyuṇṭu ataṟkuk kaḷivāri eṉpatu peyar. kaḷi kaḷital viṉikkut tamiḻil ettaṉi ettaṉi coṟkaḷ?
kaḷiveṭṭal, kaḷikaṭṭal, kaḷikoṭṭal, kaḷi kottal, kaḷikoytal, kaḷipaṟittal, kaḷiyeṭuttal, kaḷicuraṇṭal, kaḷiparaṇṭal, kaḷikiḷḷal, kaḷikallal, kaḷitōṇṭal, kaḷiakaḻtal, kaḷipiṭuṅkal, kaḷivāral mutaliyaṉa. ittakiya tamiḻccolvaḷam, tamiḻiṉ taṉi vaḷam allavō!
kaḷiyeṭuttal:
	kaḷiyeṭuttal 	= 	tīyāri allatu vēṇṭāri vilakkal.
uḻavut toḻiliṉ oru pakuti kaḷiyeṭuttalākum. kaḷikaṭṭal, kaḷipaṟittal eṉpaṉavum atuvē.
kaḷiyeṭuttal payir nalattukkum payirk kāppukkum ceyyum ceyalām. atupōl tīyavarkaḷiyum kēṭarkaḷiyum curaṇṭupavarkaḷiyum ēmāṟṟuk kārarkaḷiyum tērntaṟintu tam kūṭṭiliruntu vilakkiviṭum tērcci cilarkku uṇṭu. avar ceyal kaḷiyeṭuttal eṉappaṭum. kaḷiyeṭukkā viṭṭāl cīrākātu eṉpatu vaḻakkam. aṭarntu nīṇṭupōṉa muṭiveṭṭu talik kaḷiveṭṭutal eṉpatu innāḷ nakarppuṟa vaḻakkil uṇṭu.
kaḷḷam kavaṭu:
	kaḷḷam	= 	kaḷavu.
	kavaṭu	= 	vañcam.
kaḷḷam kavaṭu illātavar eṉṉum iṇimoḻi. kaḷavum, kaḷavukku mūlamām vañcamum illātavar eṉpati viḷakkum. kaḷḷutal kaḷavāṭal, kavaṭu iraṭṭiyākap pirital. ōreṇṇatti uḷvittu ataṟku etiriṭiyāṉati veḷikkāṭṭic ceytal kavaṭu allatu vañcamām. uḷḷoṉṟu vittup puṟamoṉṟu pēcuvār uṟavu kalavāmi vēṇṭum eṉpatu tiruvaruṭpā (8).

	kaḷḷāḷ	= 	tiruṭaṉ allatu kaḷavāḷaṉ.
	uḷḷāḷ	= 	kaḷavāḷaṉukku uḷavāḷaṉāka irukkum kiyāḷ.

kaḷḷi:1
muḷḷuṭiyatum pālvaḻivatum maḻiyilāp pālinilak kuṟuntūṟumāvatum kaḷḷi. tōṟṟattāl pacumi; pālāl veṇmi; peyarāl karumi (kaḷḷi) ēṉ? pāl ularntāl kariya niṟamāka iruttalik kaṇṭu iṭṭa poruḷ poti peyar atu.

eṉpatu āṉma nēyap paḻamoḻi.

kaḷḷi vakikaḷ palavuṇṭu. caturakkaḷḷi, vaṭṭakkaḷḷi, ceṭikkaḷḷi, koṭikkaḷḷi eṉpaviyām.
